Given any time the site shows, in either language, then it is on a 24-hour clock; and given an event with a map link, wherever its facts may carry a link (the page, the hero, a series card), then the meeting point's name is that link, while inside a card that is itself one link it stays words (2026-09-19, `DECISIONS.md` §120).
13. Given a series on view — the listing's card, the calendar, the backoffice's folded dates — when one of its dates is cancelled, at another place than most of them, or at another hour, then that date is struck through (cancelled) and carries a mark whose tooltip and accessible name say which ("Anulată", "Nu în locul obișnuit: …", "Nu la ora obișnuită: …"); a date like the others carries none (2026-09-19, `DECISIONS.md` §122). A date marked as a special edition carries the sparkle and "Ediție specială"; one date wears one mark, ranked cancelled, special, elsewhere, at another hour (2026-09-20, `DECISIONS.md` §169).
14. Given a calendar file or feed produced on QA, when it is opened or subscribed to, then its name and every entry's title carry a `[QA] ` mark, so a copy sitting beside the production one in the same calendar app says which it is; production is never marked (2026-09-20, `DECISIONS.md` §175).
15. Given an event with `event_status = CANCELLED`, when a place frees, an address is confirmed, the desk confirms, promotes or checks somebody in, staff resend a link, a number is typed by hand, the thank-you is sent, or the maintenance job runs, then nothing is offered or allocated, no hold is expired, no number is settled, and no message is queued. The exceptions are the participant's own withdrawal notice and the state notice staff may resend; a hand-typed number is written but not emailed. The registrations keep the status they had. "Înscrierile mele" and the manage page show that status with an "Eveniment anulat" chip and no desk code, QR or self check-in. The confirmation page says the event was cancelled or is over (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §331, amending §160). Verification: integration `registrations/cancelled-event.test.ts`, `registrations/maintenance.test.ts`.
16. Given the listing, an event page, the calendar and the feeds were served from the public cache, when an organizer saves the event as Cancelled, unpublishes, archives or deletes it, then the next request shows it cancelled or answers 404: the save expires every cached event read before its request ends, and a refused save expires nothing (§333). Verification: integration `public-cache/revalidation.test.ts`; e2e `cms-publish.spec.ts` (unpublished → 404).
17. (Amended.) The event page's route pills give the distance and the climb glyphs of their own, a ruler and a rising line, beside the difficulty's and the surface's, and the cost's pill carries the cost's glyph, so every pill in the facts has one (2026-09-24, `DECISIONS.md` §356).
18. Given an event held with at least one partner (`events.co_hosts`, read through `readCoHosts`), when its listing card, series card (the next date's partners) or the listing's featured hero renders, then the chips row carries one small outlined chip with the handshake glyph and "În parteneriat cu X" / "With X". Two partners read "… cu X și Y" / "… with X and Y". From three on, the first is named and the rest counted: "… cu X și încă N parteneri" / "… and N more partners", Romanian taking "de" from twenty. The chip wraps inside the card at 320 px and never widens the page. When the calendar renders, grid or agenda, then the entry carries the handshake at its end and the same words in its tooltip and accessible name. When the event page renders, then its overline carries "·", the handshake and the same words after the type and the surface, wrapping on a phone. Each surface uses the reader's language and never the other's. An event with no partners, or whose partners were all removed, carries none of these (2026-09-24, `DECISIONS.md` §367). Verification: unit `events/partner-marker.test.ts`; e2e `partner-marker.spec.ts`.
19. Given a month-grid calendar entry, when a pointer rests on it or on any mark inside it, or the keyboard focuses it, then exactly one tooltip opens. It shows the time and the whole title, then the date's note when it has one, then the partner line when it has one, each on its own line. No element of any calendar entry, grid or agenda, carries a `title` attribute. The link's accessible name carries every line and stays its name while the tooltip is open. In the agenda, which has no tooltip of its own (criterion 5, `DECISIONS.md` §261), each mark keeps its own tooltip and is a named image a screen reader reads (2026-09-24, `DECISIONS.md` §367). Verification: unit `events/partner-marker.test.ts`, `events/calendar.test.ts`; e2e `partner-marker.spec.ts`, `event-pages.spec.ts`.
20. (Amended.) "At another place than most of them" compares the place the reader's page names, in the reader's language, without the street address. Two dates are at the same place when their map links are the same link (scheme, "www.", host case, a trailing slash, the fragment and `g_st`/`utm_*` ignored), or when their names are equal once folded: diacritics, case, and every run of punctuation or spacing dropped, then trailing comma-separated address parts removed from the end, never the first part. Those parts are exactly: the club's city; its county; the country; a six-digit postcode; a Romanian street by its first word (strada, str, bulevardul, bd, b-dul, calea, aleea, șoseaua, șos, splaiul); an English street by its last word (street, st, road, rd, avenue, ave, boulevard, blvd); a house number. So "Parcul Sportiv Tractorul – intrarea dinspre Patinoarul Olimpic, Brasov" and the same name without ", Brasov" carry no mark. A different entrance, a square, another park, another city or another kilometre still does (2026-09-24, `DECISIONS.md` §367). Verification: unit `events/same-place.test.ts`, `events/series.test.ts`, `events/partner-marker.test.ts`.
21. Given an event held with one or more partners, when its listing card, series card, featured hero, calendar entry (grid, agenda, tooltip) or event page overline renders, then it carries one handshake glyph and the generic label "Eveniment în parteneriat" / "Partnered event" in the reader's language — never a partner's name and never a count — and the partners themselves, each named with its links, appear only in the event page's partner cards (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §375).
22. The partner marker's emoji glyph is aria-hidden by default wherever no caller-supplied aria-label or role names it, matching every other glyph on the site, while a caller that does name it (PartnerMark) is still read aloud. (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §379).
23. A marked event shows a headlamp pill (glyph and word, in the reader's language) after the route's numbers and before the cost on the listing card, the event page and the featured hero, and a line in the calendar entry and the .ics description; an unmarked event shows none of it.
24. An event held with a partner (`events.coHosts`) carries a generic marker — one gray handshake glyph and the words "Colaborare" / "Partnership", never a partner's name or count — on the listing card's own chip, the event page's overline, and the calendar's grid and agenda entries; the full partner list stays the event page's own partner cards. The handshake's ink is a grayscale filter matched to `text.secondary` in each colour scheme, except on a **filled** (race) calendar entry, where it instead matches `primary.contrastText` — the near-white or near-black tone that entry's own fill uses in that scheme — so the marker reads there too rather than nearly vanishing against `primary.main` (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §379).
25. A calendar entry opens exactly one tooltip at a time: in the month grid the entry's own tooltip carries every line (time, title, the date's note, the partner marker) and its marks are drawn bare with no tooltip or accessible name of their own; the agenda row carries no tooltip of its own, so its handshake keeps the only one, naming itself with the generic marker's words (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §379).
26. (Amends criterion 21, §375.) Given an event held with one or more partners, when its listing card, series card, featured hero, calendar entry (grid, agenda, tooltip) or event page overline renders, then it carries one `Handshake` glyph (Material's, drawn with no colour of its own so each surface's own ink shows through `currentColor`) and the generic label "Colaborare" / "Partnership" in the reader's language — never a partner's name and never a count — and the partners themselves, each named with its links, appear only in the event page's partner cards (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §391, reverting §379/§386's grayscale-filtered emoji).
27. A night event carries a headlamp pill on the listing card, the event page and the hero, «Alergare de noapte» / «Night run» on a group run and «Eveniment de noapte» / «Night event» on any other type. Its tooltip gives the day's sunset, and, when the start alone was not dark, the end's time and whether it is the event's end or the programme's last row, in both languages (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §394).
28. The calendar's month view and the `.ics` / Google Calendar description carry the night line with the sunset, worded as a run on a group run, computed from the same start, end and programme as the pill (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §394).
29. Every sentence about a night event names the occurrence's own start before that day's sunset: the pill's tooltip, the calendar entry, the `.ics` description, the reminder and the editor's automatic line. «Începe la 19:00, apusul la 19:00» / "Starts at 19:00, sunset at 19:00". A sunset time is never the only time shown (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §404).
30. When the automatic answer is night only because the span runs past dusk, the sentence also names that end, in the words of where it came from: «se termină la 20:40» / "ends at 20:40" for «Durata», and «ultimul punct din program la 20:15» / "the programme's last row at 20:15" for the day's last programme row (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §404).
31. When the sun alone made the call, no end is named and the start is after that day's sunset, the sentence says the start came after the sunset: «Începe la 20:00, după apusul de la 19:00» / "Starts at 20:00, after the 19:00 sunset" (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §404).
32. When the automatic answer is night and the start is before that day's sunrise, every sentence names the sunrise and never the evening's sunset. This covers the pill's tooltip, the calendar entry, the `.ics`, the reminder and the editor's automatic line. A 05:30 group run on 13 January 2027 at the club's place reads «Începe la 05:30, înainte de răsăritul de la 07:55» / "Starts at 05:30, before sunrise at 07:55" (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §404).
33. The shape of the sentence (plain, end, programme end, after sunset, before sunrise) is decided by one function, `nightShape` in `domain/night.ts`, which the tooltip, the calendar entry, the `.ics` and the reminder all use (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §404).
34. Given a night date, when the reader hovers or focuses the night pill on the listing card, the event page's route row or the hero, then its tooltip says only when the sun sets — «Soarele apune la {sunset}» / «The sun sets at {sunset}» — while the month view's line, the .ics description, the reminder and the editor's automatic line keep §404's full sentence (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §415).
35. Every sunrise, sunset, civil dawn and civil dusk the platform names is its computed instant rounded to the nearest minute. Each computed instant is within five seconds of the US Naval Observatory's published time for the same place and date, and the printed minute equals the Observatory's except where the instant lies within five seconds of a half minute (tests/unit/events/sun-reference.test.ts, §428).
36. Whether a date is a night event, and the sunset its pill names, are computed at the event's own place: the map link's pin, else the typed «Coordonate», else CLUB_COORDINATES. The club's place is used while the place is to be announced (§428, the rule of §416).
37. The night pill reads «Noapte» / «Night» on every event type and wears a crescent moon (Material's ModeNight), never the flashlight. The calendar entry, the .ics line and the reminder keep their full labels by type (§428).
38. A single-date listing card for one date of a repeated event (§113), left alone by the filters or shown as the lead event, wears the series card's repeat chip with the series' rhythm: weekly, fortnightly, or the number of dates. A one-off event wears no repeat chip.
39. The manage page of a registration for a cancelled event says first, in its own line, that the event was cancelled — «Evenimentul a fost anulat; nu trebuie să faci nimic.» — and never borrows «Înscrierile mele»'s chip-less line (`tests/unit/registrations/manage-cancelled-line.test.ts`) (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §546).
40. Criterion 15 above describes the provisional numbers of §214/§220/§444 and is superseded by this decision: a number exists only once the registration is confirmed (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
41. Amends criterion 7 (§129): the club feed is kept by the CDN until a save that changes an event expires it through its public-cache tags, or until its clock does (the next event's instant, at most a day). It is no longer read afresh on every request (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §549, amending §129).
42. Given a public read or a static page whose render started before a write and is stored after the write expired its tags, then the write's own request expires the same tags once more, three seconds after its response (Next's `after()`), under a profile Next does not drop as already done, so the next visitor after that sees the write. No request, query or render is added, and a call Next refused, or one made outside a request, schedules nothing (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §583).

**Verification:** integration `events/publication.test.ts`; e2e `event-cancelled.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-030-01 — Exactly one registration mode

- **Source:** BR-BUS-030
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.1
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given an event with mode `NONE`, when its page renders, then no registration action is offered.
2. Given an event with mode `EXTERNAL`, when its page renders, then it links to the external provider URL over HTTPS, and no local registration is created by following it.
3. Given an event with mode `EXTERNAL`, when a local registration is attempted through the API, then it is rejected.
4. Given an attempt to set capacity or a declaration on a non-internal event, when it is saved, then it is rejected.

**Verification:** integration `events/registration-mode.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-012-01 — A race groups child distance events

- **Source:** BR-BUS-012, BR-BUS-032
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.1, §12.3, §12.6
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a race with three child events, when the race page is requested in a published locale, then it renders the shared name, date, and place once, and lists each distance with its own free-place count.
2. Given a participant with an active registration on one child event, when they submit for another child event of the same race, then it is treated as a duplicate and no second registration is created.
3. Given the database, when a second active registration for the same participant and race is inserted directly, then the partial unique index rejects it.
4. Given an event with no `race_id`, when it renders, then nothing about races appears.
5. Given a race, when its structured data renders, then it is a `SportsEvent` whose `subEvent` entries are the child events.
6. Given M1, when the registration form for a child event renders, then it is the ordinary single-event form; distance selection within one page is M2.

**Verification:** integration `events/race-grouping.test.ts`; e2e `race-page.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-034-01 — Public free-place count is exact

- **Source:** BR-BUS-034
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.6
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a capped internal event with capacity 20, 15 confirmed registrations, 1 unexpired declaration hold, and 0 waiting-list entries, when a visitor opens the event page, then 4 available places are displayed.
2. Given the same event with 3 eligible waiting-list entries, when a visitor opens the event page, then 1 available place is displayed.
3. Given a hold whose deadline has passed but which the maintenance job has not yet processed, when the page is read, then that hold is not counted as occupied.
4. Given an event without capacity, when the page renders, then no numeric count is displayed and registration is shown as open.
5. Given a pending email confirmation, when the count is computed, then it does not reduce the count.
6. Given the free-place count was shown on the event page and on the listing's featured event, when a registration then takes or gives back a place — a hold, a confirmation, a cancellation, an erasure, an offer lapsing through the maintenance job — then the next visitor to either page sees the allocator's new count: the public cache the count is read from is expired by the transition itself (`registrations/repository.ts#transitionRegistration`, §333). Verification: e2e `registration-entry.spec.ts`; integration `public-cache/revalidation.test.ts`.
7. Given a waiting-list offer whose deadline passes with nothing written, when the page is read after the deadline, then the count is not answered from before it: the cached count is keyed by the next offer expiry (`public-cache/clock.ts`), so criterion 3 holds with the cache in front of the count (§333). Verification: unit `public-cache/clock.test.ts`.
8. Given a capped internal event, when a visitor opens the event page with the register button showing N places left, then a second line reads "(capacity − N) înscriși din capacity locuri" — the same two numbers already on the page, arithmetic only, never a second query; the line is absent for an uncapped event.
9. Given an event where a TEST registration holds a place alongside REAL ones (only possible outside production), when the fill line and the free-place count are read, then both derive from the same `readPublicAvailability` figure and cannot disagree; `kind` is not a condition anywhere in the computation (AGENTS.md §12.6).
10. Beside the register button of an open, capped event, a line states the taken places out of the capacity ("12 înscriși din 50 de locuri" / "12 of 50 places taken"). It comes from the same cached read as the free places, so taken plus free equals the capacity. An uncapped event shows neither number (`tests/integration/registrations/registration-fill-render.test.ts`, `tests/e2e/registration-entry.spec.ts`).
11. The listing card and the event page read one availability through one function (`readRegistrationDoor`), so they never disagree about a race's free places. When that read fails (§281), the card shows the window with no number and no button (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §409).
12. A declaration hold whose first email is still PENDING or PROCESSING in the outbox is not lapsed: the sweep releases nothing and the lapsed count stays 0 — a 30-minute hold with its email pending at +45 minutes is kept, sent at +60 it runs to +90, and it is released to the person waiting at +91; a started event still closes every hold (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §513).
13. The registration queue panel lists a waiting-list offer past its stored deadline while its first email is still queued, by the same guard the capacity count uses (`offerAwaitingItsFirstEmail`), so the panel's lines and its «Rezervate» figure never disagree; an offer past its deadline whose email has left is not listed.
14. Every form a family sitting sends after «Da, încă o persoană» writes that person's registration at once, `PENDING_EMAIL_CONFIRMATION` with a place reserved through the allocator under the event's lock, so the public count drops by one per form: three people leave 47 of 50 places free before any address is confirmed, and the confirmation gives each their own place without counting it twice; a single registration still holds no place before its address is confirmed (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §543).
15. While anybody waits (a `WAITLISTED` registration), an open event's card and page do not count places free: they say «Locurile se dau din lista de așteptare» / "Places are given from the waiting list", then the offered places and the people waiting, and offer the waiting list's door («Intră pe lista de așteptare»); with the line at its limit the door is the refusal of §348; with nobody waiting — an open offer alone included — the free places, the offer named after them, and the register button as before — in both languages, whatever the automatic-offer setting (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §615).
16. When more places are occupied than confirmed, the page's places line, in every state that shows it (open, full, waiting list full, no waiting list, and while anybody waits beside free places), says how many are confirmed and how many are in progress (a pending declaration, an open offer, a family's hold) — «105 înscriși din 150 de locuri — 86 de confirmați, 19 în curs de confirmare» — in both languages, in three parts that exist only when they are true: the first number is the occupied count in every state (the registrations holding places, confirmed and in progress, at most the capacity — the count the free places are built on, a lapsed declaration hold freed when the line has no room not counted, so places plus free equal the capacity), «N confirmați» and «M în curs de confirmare» when anything is in progress, and, when the waiting list has a claim on free places, «K locuri păstrate pentru lista de așteptare» / "K places kept for the waiting list" (capacity less the free places less the occupied); 4 confirmed, 2 pending, 4 waiting, capacity 10, automatic offers off reads «6 înscriși din 10 locuri — 4 confirmați, 2 în curs de confirmare, 4 locuri păstrate pentru lista de așteptare»; the line is the plain one when nothing is in progress and nothing kept, or when the cached entry predates the count. `readPublicPlaces` counts only CONFIRMED rows as confirmed and `kind` appears in no condition, so on QA «N de confirmați» includes TEST rows that the start list's «Cine vine (N)» (REAL rows only) leaves out (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §615).
17. on a full race the place «Dă-i un loc acum» gives is one supplementary place its press adds to the capacity, audited, never a place held within its deadline, offered or reserved for somebody else; the public count reads the raised capacity with that place taken (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §642).
18. A live invitation by email that was not sent «În afara locurilor» counts as one occupied place from its send until its deadline, its acceptance or its withdrawal — compared with its deadline on every read — so the public free places and the places line drop by one per invitation, and «Cine vine» does not change (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §647).
19. While an event's «Folosește lista de invitați speciali» is on, or while any real registration on the hidden list holds a place (confirmed or a hold) with the switch off, its places line («N înscriși din M locuri») is followed by «Organizatorii, voluntarii și sportivii invitați pot fi la start în afara locurilor anunțate; ei nu ocupă din aceste locuri.» (English likewise) in every state that draws the line; with the switch off and nobody on the list holding a place, or on an uncapped event, nothing; no string of a public catalogue namespace (anything but `Admin` and `Feedback`) says «lista ascunsă», «invitați speciali», «invitat special», "hidden list" or "special guest" (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
20. Given an open invitation whose deadline was after an unreachable window's step `since`, when the step moves, then the invitation's deadline moves later by the step's amount, capped by the start alone — of a closed window, not at all when it was sent or re-sent after its end (`last_sent_at`), by what was left of it when inside — with its `ACCEPT_INVITATION` token kept the same grace past it, audited `event.invitation_deadline_moved_for_outage`; an invitation that lapsed while the platform was unreachable and whose counted place was given meanwhile is not revived, lapses, is audited `event.invitation_not_revived_for_outage` and never marked outside the places, so the public free-place count never counts a place twice (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §657).
21. Given a capped event open for registration whose «Arată public numărătoarea» is off, then the places line («N înscriși din M locuri — …») is not drawn in any state, the card and the page say no «N locuri libere», and a full event's lead reads «Mulțumim! Toate locurile s-au ocupat. Intră pe lista de așteptare.» / "Thank you! All places are taken. Join the waiting list." with no capacity — the event reads as an uncapped one, the door still chosen from the real counts; the switch is read off the cached availability entry (key part `"numbers-switch"`) and off the event row, and off in either withholds the numbers (2026-10-06, `DECISIONS.md` §668).

**Verification:** integration `capacity/public-availability.test.ts`; e2e `event-page.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-034-02 — Capacity can never be exceeded

- **Source:** BR-BUS-034
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.6
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a capped event with 1 free place, when 20 confirmations — or 20 restarts of cancelled registrations by verified participants (2026-09-19, `DECISIONS.md` §151) — are attempted concurrently, then exactly 1 succeeds and the rest are waitlisted or rejected, and the final occupied count equals capacity.
2. Given any capacity-changing transaction, when it runs, then it locks the event row or an equivalent serialization point.
3. Given an attempt to lower capacity below the current occupied count, when it is submitted, then it is rejected with a clear message.
4. Given capacity is increased, when the transaction commits, then existing waiting-list entries are allocated before any later direct registration.
5. Given an internal event with people on its waiting list, when the editor saves a higher number of places — or no number — then, in the save's own transaction and after the event row is locked, the new places are offered through the one allocator to the first in line, in order, each a 24-hour offer with its `WAITLIST_SPOT_OFFER` queued; a lifted cap offers to everyone waiting; the same number again offers nothing; a series save "for all dates" or "this and following" does the same on each date it reaches, against that date's own line and places; the save answers how many were offered and the editor's banner says so ("Salvat — locuri oferite listei de așteptare: 12."); a save that is not a raise never calls the allocator, so a lapsed offer stays for the job that notifies it; an event cancelled — in the same save or before — offers nothing, whatever its number; one date of a series too full refuses the whole save and undoes the offers already made; lowering below the places taken, an offer among them, is still refused by criterion 3, counted behind the event row's lock; and every allocation that locks the row reads the places from the locked row, never from the page that rendered before the raise (2026-09-19, `DECISIONS.md` §147).
6. The club's deadlines are one setting ("Termene") holding the email-link hours, the declaration-hold minutes, the waiting-list offer hours, the reminder hours (0 = none), the self-check-in hours, the race-week days and the series horizon days. Each has a default equal to the former constant and fixed bounds. A stored value outside its bounds, or unreadable, is read as its default (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §377).
7. A declaration hold, a waiting-list offer or an email-confirmation link created after a change takes the new length. One created before keeps the deadline it was given, however the setting moves afterwards. The email link's lapse is written on the registration, and a restart gets a lapse of its own (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §377).
8. The allocator's offer step (`fillAvailableSpots`) takes the club's deadlines as a required argument and reads none itself. Every caller reads them before its transaction and passes them in: the registration paths, the editor's capacity raise for one date or for every date of a series, and the maintenance job once per run. No read of `platform_settings` happens inside the transaction that holds the event row lock, even when the instance's memo has expired (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §377).
9. The backoffice queue panel's help sentences name the club's hold and offer as currently set, read once per request from the database, and its times stay in the event's own zone (§369). A public page reads the setting from the data cache and never wakes the database (§333) (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §377).
10. The public form sent again with a registered address and a different name creates no registration and answers with the same screen as any other submission. The address receives one `REGISTER_ANOTHER_PERSON` message instead: a single-use link, hashed at rest, valid for the club's email-link window, to the event's form with the address fixed. When the address is at the club's limit, the message has no link (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §389).
11. The club's limit on registrations per address per event (default 4, range 1-10) is read and enforced under the event's lock, which `submitRegistration` takes before it reads which runners the address holds. Five emailed links pressed at once with one slot left produce exactly one registration and four `addressAtCap` refusals, proven against a real PostgreSQL server by `tests/concurrency/family.test.ts` on every run (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §389).
12. Two members of one family pressing the public form at once produce one registration and no error, on today's schema and after the contract release (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §389).
13. The family flow's gate reads the catalogue: with `registrations_event_participant_unique` absent the flow is on, with it present the flow is dormant — proven both ways by `tests/integration/registrations/family-gate.test.ts` (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §390).
14. A family sitting's places are reserved until one deadline, the first form's instant plus the club's window and declaration hold, capped by the close and the start, written once and moved by no form, press, send or late outbox job; one form and a «Da» every five minutes for two hours leave the event back at its capacity from minute 40 on; a form after the deadline opens a new sitting with its own; past it the maintenance job clears the reservation when somebody waits and the waiting list is offered the place; with no place free the form reserves nothing and its person joins the waiting list at the confirmation, and with the event full and its line closed the form is refused as a single one is (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §543).
15. A sitting's form that writes no registration (a kept form, a person the address already holds, an address at its limit) and the opening «Da» of a sitting whose first form wrote none each take one counted place in `family_place_holds`, through the allocator under the event's lock, until the sitting's deadline or the family's confirmation, which releases the holds before it allocates anybody; a held place is one per person the address sends at the event, its slot a keyed digest of the event, the participant's id and the name key that names nobody, and the server decides from every registration and held place of the address at the event, whatever sitting took it, whether a form adds a place, so a person holds at most one live family place and a replayed sealed half adds nothing; with no place free the form writes the person's row with `holds_place` false, which holds no place and counts in no capacity, so the club's limit counts the people a sitting sent the same for every address; an allocated registration's own held place is released before the count, and only on an event with a row of `family_place_holds`, so a single registration's allocation never keys the slot secret (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §543).
16. Driven through the real public actions and their sealed cookie, a sitting kept alive by «Da» past its deadline, a form after the deadline, and replays of the half from past the deadline and from before it give a fresh address, one that already holds the first person and one at its limit the same public count, redirect, half and rendered screen after every step, with places free and on a full event; a person whose place lapsed is held again once, nobody holds two live family places, and the club's limit per address is never said for a person the address already sent in a sitting, on a full event as with places free: a form for a person already sent, on a half that already counts the limit, is a re-send for every address (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §543).
17. The family place slot is keyed under `AUTH_SECRET`, or `JOB_SECRET`, or a fixed public development key only in local and test, so a server restarted mid-sitting keys the same slots; on qa and production a slot is never keyed without one of the two secrets, but refused (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §543).
18. Each internal event carries «Ofertele din lista de așteptare pleacă automat» (`events.waitlist_auto_offer`, default true; the editor's registration box, carried by a series' scoped save, every change audited with from and to). With it false, `fillAvailableSpots` offers nothing — read off the event's row under the caller's lock, so a cancellation, an erasure, an offer's expiry, a capacity raise and the maintenance job all free places and offer nobody — and the maintenance job's free-place sweep does not select the event; with it true everything is as before (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §615).
19. Given the event editor, when it draws the waiting-list setting, then its label is «Locurile din lista de așteptare se alocă automat» (en «Places from the waiting list are allocated automatically»), which supersedes the label quoted in criterion 18 («Ofertele din lista de așteptare pleacă automat»), and its help says that on «Da» a freed or added place is offered at once, by email and with a deadline, to the first on the list, and on «Nu» it stays free until the club gives it; the guide, `SETUP.md` and the tests quote the same words; the column, its values and the behaviour are unchanged (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §630).
20. The capacity changes only by the event editor's save or by an Administrator's press of «Trimite-i oferta» or «Dă-i un loc acum» for one chosen person on a full event that carries the capacity its dialog named (exactly the locked capacity + 1), which adds exactly one place to that one event row under the event lock, after the stale holds are expired and the places counted again, in the transaction whose offer or place then occupies it — never the allocator on its own, and never past the count: two Administrators pressing at once for two people on the last free place, both pages read while it was free, make one offer on the free place and one refusal (`SUPPLEMENTARY_PLACE_UNCONFIRMED`) with the capacity unchanged, and two pressing at once on a full event, both naming capacity + 1, make one raise and one refusal — never two offers on one place, never a raise the press did not confirm, never capacity + 2; `kind` appears in no condition (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §642).
21. A registration on «Lista de invitați speciali» (the special guests list, `registrations.outside_capacity`) is in no term of the capacity formula in any state — confirmed, a declaration hold, an open offer or a family's reservation; the allocator gives it a place directly whatever the counts and never waitlists or refuses it for want of one, `fillAvailableSpots` never offers it anything and no stale-hold sweep releases its hold for somebody waiting; every change of list serves the line first under the event lock (`fillAvailableSpots` after the stale holds expire, even when the press changes nothing), putting a row that holds a counted place on the list releases that place and the ordinary refill follows, putting a `WAITLISTED` row, or a `WAITLIST_OFFERED` one, on the list gives it a declaration hold at once with the ordinary deadline and one `COMPLETE_DECLARATION` queued (an offer's queued email never tried withdrawn), so past the offer's deadline `expireStaleHolds` leaves it `PENDING_DECLARATION` and `findEventsNeedingMaintenance` does not select its event for it — `WAITLISTED` and `WAITLIST_OFFERED -> PENDING_DECLARATION` only for a hidden-list row, and taking off the list a row that would hold a counted place is refused with `NoFreePlaceError` unless `occupied < capacity` once the line is served — so a hold released for a waiting runner is offered to them, never taken by the row taken off — so two connections racing for the last place while a row comes off the list never end above capacity; a cancelled or expired hidden-list row restarted through the form comes back counted, the mark cleared and journalled; only the column is a condition, `kind` none (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
22. Accepting an invitation seats the registration in the invitation's place under the event lock while the invitation still counts, then marks it accepted, so the occupied count never drops in between and nobody waiting is offered that place; on a full race a send adds one supplementary place per invitation that needs one only when the form named exactly the resulting capacity, and two Administrators inviting onto the last free place at once make one invitation and one refusal (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §647).
23. A registration of an invited address that gets its place by another route than the invitation's link — the confirmation of the public form, a restart, a staff entry, the desk, a late signature or «Dă-i un loc acum» — takes the address's open invitation over in the same locked transaction: the place is the registration's with no supplementary place and no instant where it is free, the invitation is marked accepted with that registration and audited with `adopted`, a place the registration did not need goes to the line, and nobody waits behind a place held in their own name (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §647).
24. A send of invitations never takes a counted free place while anyone eligible waits: under the event lock the places free for invitations are the capacity less the occupied places less the `WAITLISTED` rows with no offer, whether automatic offers are on or off and before or after the registration close, so each invitation beyond them needs one confirmed supplementary place or the whole send is refused with nothing written, the waiting row keeps its place, and the dialog's forecast subtracts the same waiting (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §647).
25. A registration on the hidden list (`registrations.outside_capacity`) stays out of every term of the capacity formula whatever the event's «Folosește lista de invitați speciali», «Numără și invitații speciali» and «Arată public numărătoarea» say: the free places, the places line and the waiting list's turn never count it (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
26. The club's «Termene» also hold `verificationRetryHours` (hours, 2 to 72, default 20) and `verificationRetries` (a count, 0 to 3, default 1, where 0 switches off re-sending the verification email). Each is bounded like the others, and a stored value outside its bounds, or missing, is read as its default; neither moves any deadline (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §653). Verification: unit `notifications/confirmation-retry.test.ts`, `deadlines/deadlines.test.ts`.
27. The club's «Termene» setting also holds `lastCallHours`, the hours before a participation window's deadline the last call to sign goes: 0 to 168, 48 by default, 0 = none before the deadline; the «Termene» page shows it with its help in both languages, and «Emailuri» states it in the description of the declaration email (2026-10-05, `DECISIONS.md` §665). Verification: unit `deadlines/deadlines.test.ts`.

**Verification:** integration `capacity/concurrency.test.ts` against real PostgreSQL; integration `cms/capacity-raise.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-034-03 — No queue leapfrogging

- **Source:** BR-BUS-034, BR-BUS-035
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.6, §10.7
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a full event with 3 eligible waiting-list entries, when a confirmed participant cancels, then the released place is offered to the first waiting entry and not to a new registrant.
2. Given the same moment, when a new visitor submits a registration, then that person joins the end of the waiting list.
3. Given a released place and a concurrent new registration, when both transactions run, then the waiting entry wins deterministically.
4. Given a CANCELLED event, when a place is freed, an address is confirmed, the registration closes or staff try to confirm on paper, give a place or check in, then no place is allocated, no number is settled and no message is queued. The desk verbs are refused, and resend sends only the state notice. Verification: integration registrations/cancelled-event.test.ts.
5. Criterion 4 above describes the provisional numbers of §214/§220/§444 and is superseded by this decision: a number exists only once the registration is confirmed (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
6. Given an event with at least one `WAITLISTED` registration, when a new registration is allocated — a confirmed address, a restart, a late signature, the desk, a family's form — then it joins the waiting list even while a place is free, under the event lock and whatever the event's automatic-offer setting; a family that arrives while anybody waits reserves nothing. An open offer is not somebody waiting: with nobody `WAITLISTED`, a newcomer takes a free place as before, a full line's lapsed hold included (§160). The form's own first check at submit refuses WAITLIST_FULL under the same rule — a full line with places free is refused before any email — and the registration form page shows the waiting-list notice or refusal by the card's rule (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §615).

**Verification:** integration `capacity/queue-priority.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-039-01 — The public participant list is opted into, off by default, and names only

- **Source:** BR-BUS-039, BR-BUS-070
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.10, §12.3, §12.6
- **Priority:** SHOULD
- **Release:** M1
- **Status:** built, and recorded in `DECISIONS.md` §32; switched off in every
  environment. The wording of the approved privacy notice that would allow the club to switch it
  on is still the club's to write, and until it exists the setting stays HIDDEN everywhere.

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a newly created or newly duplicated event, when its participant-list setting is read, then it is `HIDDEN`.
2. Given an event whose setting is `HIDDEN`, when its public page renders, then nothing about who is registered appears — no list, no heading and no count.
3. Given an event whose setting is `NAMES`, when its public page renders, then it lists the registered name of every `CONFIRMED`, `REAL` registration whose participant asked to be on it, ordered by confirmation time, and nothing else about any of them.
4. Given a registration that is not `CONFIRMED`, of kind `TEST`, or that did not ask to be listed, when the list renders, then that person does not appear and no count reveals them.
5. Given the registration form of an event whose list is switched on, when it renders, then it offers a plainly worded, unticked "I want to appear on the participant list" — a tick puts the name on, no tick keeps it off, and the row's `list_opt_out` is the tick's opposite; the staff entry form asks the same way (2026-09-19, `DECISIONS.md` §143; asked only where a list exists since §85).
6. Given an event whose registration mode is not `INTERNAL`, when `NAMES` is saved, then it is refused by the service and again by a database constraint.
7. Given the public queries, when they are read, then no participant email address can be returned by any of them.
8. Given a confirmed participant, when the confirmation email is sent, then it carries a second action link worded by their current choice — "Nu vreau să apar pe lista publică de participanți", or "Vreau să apar" for someone who did not tick the box — minted at send time with the `LIST_CONSENT` purpose, hashed at rest, single use; when the link is opened, then the page shows the current choice and one button, and the GET changes nothing; when the button is pressed, then `list_opt_out` flips, one audit row `registration.list_consent_changed` records from/to and never a name, the token is consumed, a fresh one is minted in the same transaction and the page re-renders under it; a used, expired or wrong-purpose token gets the generic refusal with the resend path; the same switch is offered on the participant's status page and on "Înscrierile mele"; the public list excludes a participant who opted out after confirmation and includes one who opted back in (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §302).
9. Given a registration row written without an answer to the list question, when it is stored, then `list_opt_out` defaults to true and the row is absent from the public participant list (migration 0060, §323).
10. Given an event whose participant list is NAMES, when the list renders, then a confirmed registrant who did not opt in is one row reading "Participant (nume ascuns)", with no name, club or position, grouped after every named row; the heading's total count includes it.
11. Given an event, when an Administrator sets its participant-list visibility to NAMES, then the save is refused unless an approved privacy notice is currently in force (closing the gap §32 left open until production approved one on 2026-09-22).
12. While the privacy notice in force names {{participantListStates}} in every language, the public participant list shows beside each name one of three words — Confirmat, Înscris, în așteptarea confirmării (PENDING_DECLARATION, WAITLIST_OFFERED), Pe lista de așteptare (WAITLISTED, queue order, no position) — and lists the ticked pending and waiting after the confirmed rows.
13. The public list never shows a registration whose address is unconfirmed, a cancelled, expired or erased registration, a person who did not tick «Vreau să apar», or a TEST registration; the query selects a name, a club and a group, never the raw state.
14. While the notice in force does not name the marker, the list is the confirmed names alone with no state words, and no pending or waiting row is read or counted.
15. A pending or waiting-list runner appears on the public list only if their tick was given under a privacy notice version at or above the first approved one that names `{{participantListStates}}` in every language (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §421).
16. The public list closes by itself the club's «Termene» `publicListDays` after the event's end, or its start where it has no end. The default is 30 days, the range 1 to 365, the setting is Administrator-only and audited, and the check is made at request time. The privacy notice's `{{publicListPeriod}}` prints the club's own setting, and the list is marked not to be snippeted (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §421).
17. A runner's Strava and Instagram appear beside their name on the public list only while the approved privacy notice in force describes them and only when that runner ticked «Arată și Strava și Instagram»; the query returns neither otherwise (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §500).
18. The public list prints a Strava link only for an https profile on strava.com or www.strava.com (athletes or pros), rewritten as https://www.strava.com/<athletes|pros>/<id>; a strava.app.link share link, an http address or any other host prints nothing, while the registration form still accepts the app.link shape (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §500).
19. The public list prints an Instagram link only for a username of the form's shape, as https://www.instagram.com/<handle>/ (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §500).
20. The retention sweep clears a minor's Strava, Instagram and public-list tick together, and expires the public "places" cache after the step commits when it cleared any row (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §500).
21. A refusal of the public-list switch in the event editor names it «Program, regulament și declarație › Lista publică a participanților › …»; the switch keeps its name `event.participantListVisibility` and its privacy-notice guard.
22. While the privacy notice in force names `{{participantListStates}}`, the public participant list ends with a legend, «Ce înseamnă stadiile de pe listă» / “What the states on the list mean”, with one sentence per state it shows and none for a state no row has, in the page's language, the legend's title after the help outline glyph, every sentence at most 200 characters: «Confirmat» — the declaration signed, online or on paper at the desk, and the place theirs; «Înscris, în așteptarea confirmării» — a place held, the declaration not signed, asked at registration and again «cu {N} înainte», owed «până cu {N} înainte de start» or «până la start», while the event's window has not opened, and inside the window or on an event without one the club's declaration hold, «păstrat {hold} de la înscriere, până semnează declarația», chosen at render time; «Pe lista de așteptare» — no place yet, the first gets an offer by email and the club's offer window to sign. Every number is read from the event and the club's «Termene», never written in the catalogue. Each state word on a row carries the same sentence behind a «?» that is also its accessible name. Without that notice, no legend and no «?» (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §556). Verification: unit `events/list-state-legend.test.ts`; e2e `public-list-states.spec.ts`.
23. Given an event that publishes its list, when the registration form, the manage page, «Înscrierile mele», the confirmation email or the staff form names the public-list tick, then it reads «Vreau să apar pe lista de participanți & rezultate» / “I want to appear on the participants & results list” (the form adds « — opțional» and leads with the trophy glyph), and the privacy notice's section 4 quotes the same words and names the list and the results as one disclosure behind one tick (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §570).
24. Given an event's public participant list, when the privacy notice in force, in every language, names `{{participantListNumbers}}` and at least one listed confirmed registration carries a race number (`bib_number`), then the table shows a «Nr.» column with that number beside each named confirmed runner, «—» for a confirmed runner without one and for the pending and waiting rows, nothing for an anonymous row; the provisional number is never selected or shown; without the notice's marker, or with no number on the page, the table is unchanged (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §613).
25. Criterion 24 amended: the race-number column's heading is «BIB» in both languages, its accessible full name stays «numărul de concurs» / «race number», and the desk's printable confirmed list heads its number column «BIB» too (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §625).
26. Given an event whose list is `NAMES`, while the privacy notice in force names `{{participantListStates}}` in every language, the ticked `WAITLISTED` registrations (under §421's notice rule) appear — with «Pe lista de așteptare», their count, their legend sentence and the caption's and the note's clause — only when the event's «Lista de așteptare e publică» (`events.waitlist_public`) is on; off, no waiting row is read or counted, the pending group is unchanged, and with the notice gate off nothing beyond the confirmed is shown whichever way the switch is (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §628).
27. `events.waitlist_public` is false by default and on every duplicate; the service stores true only beside `participant_list_visibility = NAMES` on an `INTERNAL` event and false otherwise, whatever was posted; beside a published list a caller that does not post it leaves it unchanged (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §628).
28. Given a capped event open for registration whose cached places read knows the confirmed count, when more places are taken than confirmed, then the list's title counts the confirmed plus those in progress («Cine vine (6)») and the line under it reads «6 înscriși — 4 confirmați (3 cu numele afișat), 2 în curs de confirmare» / «6 registered — 4 confirmed (3 with their name shown), 2 completing their registration», from the door's cached read with no query of its own; with nobody confirmed and two in progress, the title reads «Cine vine (2)» and «2 înscriși — 0 confirmați (0 cu numele afișat), 2 în curs de confirmare» stands above «Încă nu și-a confirmat nimeni participarea.»; the note under the table opens «Numărul din titlu îi cuprinde pe confirmați și, cât timp înscrierile sunt deschise la un eveniment cu număr de locuri, pe cei în curs de confirmare»; with nobody in progress, on an uncapped event, while registration is not open, or from a cache entry without the counts, the title and line are the confirmed alone as before; the rows and the §396 gate are unchanged (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §632).
29. `events.waitlist_count_public` is true by default and for every existing event; the editor shows «Arată public câți așteaptă» under «Lista de așteptare e publică» whatever that switch says, saves it by its marker (a form without the box leaves it unchanged), writes `event.waitlist_count_public_changed` with `from` and `to` on every date a save changed, and a series' scoped save, every date a series makes and a duplicate carry it; it hides a number and never publishes or hides a name (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §634).
30. A confirmed registration on «Lista de invitați speciali» whose person ticked the list appears in the table like any ticked confirmed runner, with no public marker; unless the event counts the hidden list («Numără și invitații speciali» with «Folosește lista de invitați speciali» on) it is left out of «Cine vine (N)», the «N confirmați — M cu numele afișat» line and the in-progress count, and it is always left out of the places line; an unticked one is not even a «Participant (nume ascuns)» row; while the numbers are said and such a row is in the table, the note under it adds «Cei la start în afara locurilor anunțate (organizatori, voluntari, sportivi invitați) apar în tabel fără să fie numărați: numerele de mai sus numără locurile.» when the hidden list is not counted, and «Numerele de mai sus îi numără și pe cei la start în afara locurilor anunțate (organizatori, voluntari, sportivi invitați); dintre ei, în tabel apar doar cei care au ales să apară.» when it is; the disclosure gates are unchanged (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
31. The «Lista de invitați speciali» group's three settings, under a heading with the incognito glyph and a switch whose «?» says who the list is for, the two settings under it and how it differs from an invitation by email, are saved by one marker and «Arată public numărătoarea» by its own (a form without a marker edits none of its fields), the group's audited as `event.hidden_list_changed` and the count's tick as `event.participant_count_public_changed`, each naming only what moved, carried by a scoped series save, «Repetă evenimentul» and a duplicate, and carried by the editor's preview; the two under the switch are shown only while it is ticked, and «Arată public numărătoarea» is shown on every event beside «Arată public câți așteaptă»; the editor's summaries call a public participant list that is off «listă nepublică» and «Nepublică», never «lista ascunsă» or «invitați speciali» (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
32. «Cine vine» shows the same rows whatever the ticks say; with «Numără și invitații speciali» on and the switch on, its title and «confirmați» count every real hidden-list registration with a place (holds among «în curs de confirmare» on a capped event only), the not-counted note is gone and the note says instead that the numbers count them too and that only those who chose to appear are in the table; with «Arată public numărătoarea» off — on any event, the hidden list on or off — it shows no number in the title, no counted line, no «în curs» words and no position column; the places line and the free places never count the hidden list; with the switch off the hidden list is never counted; no rendering of it says «lista ascunsă», «invitați speciali», «invitat special», "hidden list" or "special guest" (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
33. With «Numără și invitații speciali» on, the note that the numbers count people the table does not show appears only when the numbers above it include somebody on the hidden list: its confirmed, or its holds only where the headline adds them (a capped event's known counts); on an uncapped event a hidden-list hold alone brings no such note (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
34. Given an event whose «Arată public numărătoarea» (`events.participant_count_public`, true by default) is off, then «Cine vine» shows no number (criterion 32) and every other public surface of the event says no number derived from its capacity or its registrations — the event page's door, the listing card, the hero, the calendar's and the series card's registration line and the register page's full state carry no places line, no free places, no capacity, no room on the waiting list and no offered or waiting count, whatever «Arată public câți așteaptă» says — while the state words stay (the register button, «Locurile se dau din lista de așteptare», the full, waiting-list-full and no-waiting-list sentences, the waiting list's button); the admin side and the emails are unchanged; ticked, every page and card is byte for byte as before (2026-10-06, `DECISIONS.md` §668).
35. The editor draws «Arată public câți așteaptă» under «Arată public numărătoarea», indented, shown only while the parent is ticked and still posted (its value and marker) while hidden; criterion 29's placement under «Lista de așteptare e publică» and criterion 31's «beside» are amended (2026-10-06, `DECISIONS.md` §669).
36. Given an event whose «Arată public numărătoarea» is off and «Arată public câți așteaptă» on, then no public surface says how many wait, and the waiting person's own page, «Toate înscrierile mele», the `WAITLIST_JOINED` email and «Ce îi spui» say the private count's sentence — no length and no place (`waitlistCountShown`); this amends criterion 34's "the admin side and the emails are unchanged" for the waiting count, everything else in it standing (2026-10-06, `DECISIONS.md` §669).

**Verification:** integration `privacy/public-surface.test.ts`

### 4.3 Participant identity

#### BR-REQ-031-01 — Registration without an account

- **Source:** BR-BUS-031
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.3, §15.1
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given the registration form, when it renders, then it asks only for full name, email, and acknowledgment of the privacy notice, and offers no password field or login link.
2. Given a completed registration, when the participant record is inspected, then it holds no password, no provider token, and no staff role.
3. Given a submitted registration, when the response renders, then it states that an email has been sent, without revealing whether that address was already registered.
4. Given `TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` and `TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY` both set, when the public form renders, then it carries the Cloudflare Turnstile widget, and a submission whose token Cloudflare does not confirm — or that carries none, or when Cloudflare cannot be reached — is refused with a field error the person can act on; with either key unset nothing is shown or checked, and the honeypot and the timing check stand either way; the staff form never shows it (2026-09-18, `DECISIONS.md` §97).

5. Given the registration form, when it renders, then under its title it states the event's date and time in the event's zone and its meeting point, and offers as links, each a 44 px target: the event's page, its rules when the organizer wrote any, the terms and the privacy notice — so what is being signed up for and under which terms is on the form itself (`DECISIONS.md` §102).
6. Given a registration submitted again for the same event by the same address, when the re-sent message renders, then its opening sentence says the person is already registered, with that phrase underlined in the HTML part and plain in the text part, while the screen after the form stays the same for everybody (criterion 3) (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §309).
7. Given Turnstile switched on and a too-fast refusal, when the registration form's "Trimite din nou înscrierea" is pressed before Cloudflare's token exists, then the press is held with the same waiting sentence as the main send button and is sent once the token arrives, or once the eight-second release opens; it is never dropped (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §324).
8. Criterion 3 is extended. Given the same submission posted to the public form for an address holding no registration, one registration, or the club's limit of registrations at the event, when the response is compared, then the redirect and every cookie set are byte-for-byte identical. Only the outbox differs (§389).
9. Given Turnstile switched on with a wrong, missing or other widget's secret, or Cloudflare's internal error, when a person submits, then the check counts as not run and the person is not refused. A token Cloudflare rejects still refuses. `/api/health` degrades and `/admin/tasks` turns its row red only when the secret probe answers missing-input-secret or invalid-input-secret. A timeout, a 5xx or Cloudflare's internal-error reads as unreachable (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §420).
10. Criterion 3 is extended (§446). Given the same address posted to the public form for a different person (another name and another birth date than every registration on it), for a slip (the registered name with another birth date, or another name with a registered birth date), or for the same person again, when the responses are compared, then the redirect and every cookie set are byte-for-byte identical to a first registration's. Only the outbox differs.
11. The public registration form requires the city, asked right after the birth date. The staff entry keeps it optional.
12. Under the birth-date box, on the public form and at the desk, a line reads the typed date back in words, with its weekday, plus the age on the event's day. Nothing is shown before a full date is typed.
13. The end-to-end suite runs the registration form against a server with the anti-bot check on, using Cloudflare's published test keys (never the club's). It proves that a held press is sent by the check's answer rather than by the eight-second valve (§502).
14. Withdrawn — a duplicate of criterion 13, written twice by the `BR-V2.12` landing.
15. `/api/health` reports under `turnstile.lastDay` whether, in the last 24 hours, held presses were sent by the valve and widgets failed or never loaded — each as a level `none` / `some` (1–4) / `many` (5+), never a count, `null` when unread, with no effect on `status`; the signals travel with the registration form and are counted only after the registration went through, with no endpoint of their own (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §518).
16. Criterion 3 is extended (§519). Given the same public form sent for an address with no registration, with registrations, or at the club's limit, when the responses are compared, then the redirect and the sitting cookie's shape are identical. The screen after the form lists, prefills and warns only from this browser's own forms. Only the outbox differs.
17. Given a first form for a new address, an address already registered, or one at the club's limit, when the sealed family cookie is written, then it has the same length whatever the address holds, the stored leaving instant included (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §536).
18. The sitting's screen after each form says «Înscriere de familie: Ana, Mihai, Ioana — 3 locuri rezervate până la HH:MM.» with each name's place, from the browser's own half; driven through the real public actions and the sealed cookie they write, a fresh address, an address that already holds the person and an address at its limit show the same public count, redirect, half and rendered screen after every form and after replays of the browser's own earlier halves (before a form, before «Da»), with places free and on a full event («pe lista de așteptare»); a form whose sitting already fills the club's limit per address is refused at the form with its sentence and writes nothing (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §543).
19. Once the sitting's deadline is past, the sitting's screen names no place and says «Locurile nu mai sunt rezervate. Confirmă adresa din email; dacă mai e loc, îl primești atunci.» («The places are no longer reserved. Confirm the address from the email; if there is still room, you get it then.»); a form sent after it opens a new sitting whose screen names only its own people, reserved until its own deadline, never the lapsed ones (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §543).
20. The page the family's email opens says «Locurile sunt rezervate, dar nimeni nu e confirmat încă» while the sitting's deadline is ahead, and past it, while the link still lives, «Locurile nu mai sunt rezervate: confirmă oricum și fiecare primește un loc dacă mai e liber, altfel unul pe lista de așteptare.» («The places are no longer reserved: confirm anyway, and each person gets a place if one is still free, or else one on the waiting list.»), and its one press still allocates what is free (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §543).
21. After a first form, the screen's point is the email's line; «Înscriu încă o persoană cu această adresă» follows as one text-styled press with its glyph and one sentence under it, never a bold question and never a primary or full-width button, and the sentence says «Dacă înscrii încă o persoană…», never a «Da» the screen does not show (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §547).
22. A public registration never needs an account, with one exception: an event for the members alone. It takes a registration only from a members' session, with the account's own address shown and not asked, one person per account. A post without such a session answers as for an event that does not exist (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §552).
23. Given the registration form while the privacy notice in force names `{{promotionalMaterials}}` in every language, when it renders, then it shows under the public-list tick one optional, unticked, not required box labelled «Vreau să primesc oferte și beneficii de la <club> și partenerii săi.», the club's name filled from `CLUB_NAME`, with a glyph and the line «Opțional. Poți renunța oricând din pagina înscrierii tale.»; while the notice does not name it, no such box is shown (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §562).
24. Given a public submission with the box ticked, when the registration is inspected, then `promo_consent` is true with `promo_consent_at` the submission's moment only if the privacy notice the registration records names `{{promotionalMaterials}}`; a tick posted under a notice that does not name it, from a staff or desk entry, or for another adult on a family's address is not kept, a minor's is, and a test registration is kept exactly like a real one (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §562).
25. Given a public submission whose tick is kept, when the audit trail is read after a later withdrawal has rewritten `promo_consent_at`, then it still holds the form's row `registration.promo_consent_changed` (`to: true`, `via: FORM`) at the submission's moment; an unticked box on a row that never said yes writes no row (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §562).
26. Given a declaration signed with the offers-and-benefits box ticked, including a step of the family's wizard, when the notice in force describes it, then the registration says yes with the signing moment and one audit row `registration.promo_consent_changed` (`to: true`, `via: DECLARATION`); when it does not, the tick is ignored and the signature goes on (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §562).
27. Given the registration form, when its «Acorduri» block renders, then it is its own stack with no gap between rows, every box in it is MUI's small box at 44 by 44 pixels with its label in body2 (a one-line row 44 px, a row `max(44, 24 + 20 × lines)` px), each helper line is a caption under the label's words that describes the box, and the race's conditions are a regular-height button with the book glyph that still opens the text before its box can be ticked — with every word of the terms, fitness and privacy ticks unchanged, and the whole block measured under 580 px at 320 px and 280 px on a desktop (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §570).
28. Given the registration form's «Acorduri» block and the declaration page's boxes, when they render, then every box's label starts with its own `aria-hidden` glyph in a column of its own — the book for the race's conditions, a document for the terms, the medical case for the fitness declaration, the shield for the privacy notice, the trophy for the participants & results list, the share glyph for the socials, the megaphone for offers and benefits, the quill for the declaration — the optional boxes (the list, the socials, the offers) end « — opțional» / “ — optional” after their words, the offers sentence keeps its full stop and its helper no longer opens with «Opțional.», and a required box carries one required mark after its last word on a required input (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §570).
29. Given a page with a form protected by the anti-bot check, then Cloudflare's script is not requested and no widget is drawn until a person focuses, presses, types or presses a key inside that form, and no page without such a form (the listing, an event page's interest box before it is touched) requests it at all; the token is verified on the server only on submit (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §577, amending §97 and §185).
30. Until a person starts on a protected form, the anti-bot check draws nothing a person can see: no reserved height and no sentence naming Cloudflare; the sentence appears with the widget (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §593).
31. Given a submitted registration, when the screen after the form renders — and given the resend form or «Înscrierile mele»'s request form once sent, the event page's interest-form answer, the newsletter's two answers, the family confirmation page, the group-run declaration's done page, and the address-confirmed and race-declaration done pages — then the page shows a visible notice to look in Spam and Promotions and to move the club's emails to the inbox, the same words in Romanian and English on each (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §619).
32. Given the screen after the registration form (and every other page that waits for an email: the resend form and «Înscrierile mele» once sent, the newsletter's two answers, the family's confirmation, the group-run declaration's and the race declaration's done pages, the address-confirmed page; not the interest form's answer, which queues nothing) while a message a person waits for is late — Mailgun's road paused or its hour spent with one on it, one put off to the allowance's reset, or the oldest waiting longer than the promised wait plus ten minutes — when it renders, then a warning above its own wait sentence says how many messages wait, the oldest's wait, at most how long more when known (rounded up to five minutes; whole hours past ninety minutes), and — only where the page's email starts a deadline at its send — that the deadline runs from the moment it leaves and not to ask again in the page's own words — not to register again, on «Înscrierile mele» not to ask for the link again, on the newsletter's confirmation not to subscribe again (the resend form says instead that the first email's deadline runs from when that one left; pages whose email carries no deadline say neither), in both languages; the event page's registration box and the form's send button say it in one line; and while nothing is late each page's markup is exactly its markup without the notice (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §623).

**Verification:** e2e `registration-submit.spec.ts`, `registration-form.spec.ts` (5); integration `participants/identity.test.ts`; unit `registrations/turnstile.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-031-02 — Privacy-notice acknowledgment is recorded

- **Source:** BR-BUS-031, BR-BUS-053
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §15.1, §12.6
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a submission without the acknowledgment, when it is posted, then it is rejected and no registration row is created.
2. Given a submission carrying a superseded privacy-notice version, when it is posted, then it is rejected.
3. Given an accepted submission, when the registration row is inspected, then it stores the acknowledged version and a server timestamp.
4. Given a later privacy-notice version, when it is approved, then existing registrations keep the version they acknowledged.

**Verification:** integration `registrations/privacy-acknowledgment.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-072-01 — Public-results consent is captured at registration

- **Source:** BR-BUS-072, BR-BUS-070
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §12.6, §15.1
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given the registration form, when it renders, then it presents a clearly worded choice about appearing in public results by name, defaulting to the value decided in `BUSINESS.md` §9.
2. Given a submission missing the choice, when it is posted, then it is rejected.
3. Given an accepted submission, when the registration row is inspected, then it stores the choice and the wording version.
4. Given the registration management page before results are published for that event, when the participant changes the choice, then the new value is stored and audited.
5. Given M1, when any public page renders, then no results and no participant names appear; the consent exists only so M2 can publish lawfully.
6. Given M1, when the registration form renders, then it does not ask the public-results consent. A box posted anyway is ignored, and new rows store false. The column stays until M2 decides.

**Verification:** integration `registrations/results-consent.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-031-03 — Confirmation link lifetime

- **Source:** BR-BUS-031, BR-BUS-033
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §8, §16.2
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a confirmation link, when it is opened within 48 hours, then it presents an explicit confirmation action.
2. Given a confirmation link, when it is opened after 48 hours, then it explains that the link expired and offers to start again while registration is open.
3. Given an expired pending registration, when maintenance runs, then its status becomes `EXPIRED` with `expiry_reason = EMAIL_CONFIRMATION_LAPSED`.
4. Given a registration close time earlier than the 48-hour window, when the link is created, then its expiry is capped at registration close.
5. Given a registration still waiting for its email confirmation, when its link lapses, then the verification token lapses with it. A click after the lapse, before or after the sweep, lapses the row instead of allocating it, and the page says the link has lapsed, never "confirmed" (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §420).
6. Given a verification email held by a sitting, when it is rendered for sending, then its registration's email link and its token live the club's email-link window from that send, never shortened (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §519).
7. Given two registrations on one address at one event, both waiting for the address, when the link of either is pressed, then both leave `PENDING_EMAIL_CONFIRMATION` in the same transaction, the clicked one first, the other through the same allocator (never overbooked: with one place left the clicked person gets it and the other joins the waiting list), and a registration on that address submitted after the press still waits for its own link (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §588). Verification: integration `registrations/one-click-family.test.ts`.
8. Given a registration still waiting for its address whose verification email left, when the maintenance job runs the club's «Termene» `verificationRetryHours` (20 by default) after the last verification email for the address and the event left, and the link still has at least an hour, then the verification email is queued once more, with no deadline mark, keyed `registration:<id>:verify-retry:<n>`. Every verification email that left since the round's first counts toward 1 + `verificationRetries` (1 by default), the person's «Retrimite» and a staff resend included, and none is queued once the count is reached. A re-sent email that ended `FAILED` counts too, so the next run queues nothing and the job's plan does not wake for it. It is never queued for a confirmed registration, never twice by two runs at the same instant, never at 0, never while an email for them waits to leave, never to an address that bounced or complained on any message, and only one per address and event. A test registration gets it like a real one, at its own address (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §653). Verification: integration `notifications/confirmation-retry.test.ts`, unit `notifications/confirmation-retry.test.ts`.
9. Given re-sent verification emails due, when the maintenance job runs, then it queues at most 50, oldest due first, and the rest at a later run, and it queues none while the outbox is behind as the public pages judge it (`judgeEmailDelay` → late); the job's plan then wakes at the pinger's next call (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §653). Verification: integration `notifications/confirmation-retry.test.ts`, unit `notifications/confirmation-retry.test.ts`.
10. Given a re-sent verification email, when it is sent, then its inbox copy opens with «Nu am primit încă confirmarea adresei tale, așa că îți retrimitem linkul. Termenul curge de la primul email: {deadline}.» (en “We have not received your address confirmation yet, so we are sending you the link again. The deadline runs from the first email: {deadline}.”), the deadline being the registration's link lapse in the event's zone. Its «valabil» names the link's whole hours left, the deadline does not move, and the email is withdrawn rather than sent when the registration was confirmed or moved on, the event was cancelled or started, or the link has less than an hour left. «Setări» → «Emailuri» previews it beside the first verification email (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §653). Verification: integration `notifications/confirmation-retry.test.ts`, unit `notifications/confirmation-retry.test.ts`.
11. Given a confirmation refused because the places and the waiting list are full, or the event has no waiting list, when the page says how long the same link stays good, then it counts the email-link window from when the first email left, never from the form, in both languages and under 200 characters (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §654).
12.
