35. The platform's declaration template carries no waiver. Its liability paragraph is informed acceptance that says it is no waiver (Civil Code art. 1355(4)). The organiser disclaims only what is solely the runner's or a third party's doing, with 'în limitele permise de lege'. The bullets add: health 'to the best of my knowledge' with the cardiac risk; heat and cold; traffic duties; slow help; no alcohol or drugs; first aid and being stopped. There are 14 bullets. The paragraph on accompanying minors disclaims nothing (2026-09-25, DECISIONS.md §418). Verification: unit legal-documents/declaration-risks.test.ts.
36. The signing box accepts the declaration 'including, expressly, the limitation of the organiser's liability to the extent the law allows'. An adult's declaration page names the person who must sign personally. The signed PDF says the link went to the registration's email address. While any entry of the event's bundle carries an identity document, every page says to delete the file within seven days of the event (2026-09-25, DECISIONS.md §418). Verification: unit registrations/declaration-signing-words.test.ts.
37. The allowance forecast charges each hidden-copy address one message per copied participant message: four per completed registration, since the address-confirmation link is never copied. The panel's "of those, N are copies" line uses the same figure (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §419).
38. A signed declaration lands on its outcome page with a toast that matches it: confirmed ("Declarație semnată: înscrierea ta e confirmată.") or on the waiting list ("Declarație semnată: ești pe lista de așteptare."), in the reader's language, shown once. Without JavaScript the page's banner is the whole answer.
39. Given a group run that offers its surface's declaration and whose event has a minimum age above zero, when the signing page renders, then it asks for «Data nașterii» / "Date of birth", bounded by the latest date still that old on the run's day. When a signer's date is under the minimum on the run's day in the run's zone, the press is refused. The focusable summary names the birth date with «Vârsta minimă pentru această alergare este {age}, împliniți în ziua alergării.», and no row, message or throttle count is written. A missing or unreadable date is named with the other wrong boxes. A run with no minimum asks for no date. The date is never stored. The comparison is the registration's own (`isUnderMinimumAge`). (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §440, amending §393). Verification: integration `group-run-declarations/sign.test.ts`; unit `group-run-declarations/minimum-age.test.ts`.
40. Given the platform's two group-run templates, in both languages, then each carries, right after its opening, a sentence naming `{{minimumAge}}`. It is filled from the event with its unit («16 ani», «20 de ani», "16 years") on the signing page and in the signed PDF. On an event with minimum age 0 the whole sentence is left out, and with no value at all it shows the dotted blank (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §440). Verification: unit `group-run-declarations/minimum-age.test.ts`, `legal-documents/group-run-declaration.test.ts`; e2e `group-run-declaration.spec.ts`.
41. Given the club's copies on /admin/emails, when a save is refused, then the refusal summary links to each refused box by its label and marks it. Its sentence names the entries that are not addresses, or the ceiling of ten per box, or says that «Declarațiile semnate merg la» takes one address. Nothing is saved, and every box keeps what was typed. The service names each refusal by its list (`participants.bcc`), never by the setting's first word (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §457). Verification: unit `notifications/club-copies-visible.test.ts`, integration `notifications/club-notices.test.ts`.
42. Given the club's copies on /admin/emails, when the panel is read, then it names in force the declaration's archive address and its Cc and Bcc, the confirmation-notice list and the participants' hidden copy. When the declaration's Cc or Bcc is stored but no archive address resolves, a warning names them and says that none leaves until an archive address is set. The closed fold's summary names the mailboxes after their count (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §457). Verification: unit `notifications/club-copies-visible.test.ts`.
43. When one address holds two or more registrations at an event whose declarations can be signed, the opened declaration link shows a stepper: the opened person first, then the others in registration order. One person is signed per step, against their own registered name and identity document, and each signature records its own acceptance and sends its own confirmation with its own PDF (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §471).
44. A person on the address who signed before the wizard began (a declaration acceptance, and confirmed or on the waiting list) is a step shown as signed and counted in «Declarația N din M». A registration awaiting its address confirmation, queued without an offer and without a signature, or cancelled is not a step (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §471).
45. Each step's line gives its state in words in the reader's language: signed, on the waiting list, now, next, later, or no longer waiting for a signature. Only the current step carries «Declarația N din M», with M counting every person on the list (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §471).
46. «Semnez mai târziu» moves on without writing to any registration. The person put off stays on the list as later, and their own emailed link still signs them until the deadline (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §471).
47. The declaration request sent to an address with others still to sign names them: «Pe această adresă mai așteaptă semnătura declarațiile pentru: …», with the English equivalent, and says that the one link signs them all, one after the other (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §471).
48. Every page of a declaration PDF (signed, blank, the event's bundle, a group-run copy) names the version of the text on that page and the first sixteen characters of its hash, under the entry's title and in the page's footer. In a bundle, each page names its own entry's version (§499).
49. Given a race that registers on the site, when its declaration is shown, signed, confirmed on paper or printed blank, then the text is the version in force of the kind the organizer picked, or, with nothing picked, the course's kind (ASPHALT reads road, anything else trail). While no road text is approved, a road race gets the trail text; a trail race never gets the road text. A posted id or hash of the other kind's text is refused with CONFLICT and nothing is written (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §515). Verification: integration `registrations/race-declaration-kind.test.ts`; unit `legal-documents/race-declaration-keys.test.ts`.
50. Given the platform's two group-run templates, then neither states an age of its own. Each carries one sentence, «Declar că am cel puțin {{minimumAge}} împliniți la data alergării.», filled with the run's minimum, never under 18 (`groupRunMinimumAge`: a run saved with 14 reads 18). The consent box and the line above the form say that age, and a birth date is asked only above 18 (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §515, amending criteria 39 and 40). Verification: unit `group-run-declarations/minimum-age.test.ts`, `legal-documents/group-run-declaration.test.ts`; integration `group-run-declarations/sign.test.ts`; e2e `group-run-declaration.spec.ts`.
51. The declarations wizard's button says «Semnează și treci la următoarea persoană» only while another person's declaration follows in the sitting; on the last one, after a person put off for later, and on a single declaration it says «Semnează» (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §547).
52. The declarations wizard's pass reaches every press in both languages: it is written as one cookie per language, each on its own page's path, so the second, third and later persons' presses carry it and sign; a response that keeps one cookie per name no longer drops the Romanian page's pass (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §547).
53. Every new declaration acceptance — by the email link, on paper or in the family wizard — stores `text_hash`, the SHA-256 in lower-case hex of the declaration's title and every paragraph its signed PDF prints, the blanks filled as the signer's copy fills them, lines joined by a line feed, computed in the transaction that writes the row. The signed PDF, the club's archive copy and every page of a signed entry print «Versiunea N · Semnat la ZZ.LL.AAAA, hh:mm:ss (ora României) · Amprenta documentului (SHA-256): <hex>» from the row, without the hash part on a row from before it; the blank form prints none. The registration's backoffice page shows the hash's first 12 characters with the whole one in a tooltip (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §556). Verification: unit `legal-documents/signed-text.test.ts`; integration `registrations/declaration-proof.test.ts`, `registrations/signed-declaration.test.ts`.
54. An Administrator may mark a declaration acceptance «Păstrează: reclamație / litigiu în curs» with a reason, and clear it with a reason; each press asks first, toasts and writes an audit row naming the actor, the reason and the acceptance id, never the person. While it is set the retention sweep keeps the registration and its declaration past their period and every erase is refused; once cleared, the next sweep deletes them on schedule. Anyone below Administrator is refused on the server (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §556). Verification: integration `registrations/declaration-proof.test.ts`.
55. «Setări» → «Emailuri» → «Copiile clubului» holds three nested folds, closed by default, each with a glyph and a summary line — «Declarațiile semnate» (the archive address, Cc, Bcc and its warning), «Anunțul de confirmare», «Copia clubului la emailurile către participanți» (its warning and the Bcc box), the last two naming their saved addresses or «—» — under one form and one «Salvează»; every fold opens after the card's save, a refusal opens the fold around the box it names and leaves the others shut, and a reader who may not edit sees the three lists in force without boxes (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §559).
56. The event editor names the race declaration's two kinds «Eveniment montan» and «Eveniment pe șosea sau în parc» ("Mountain event", "Road or park event"), in the select, the newest-version line and the mismatch note, and a road race with no approved road text is said to sign «declarația pentru eveniment montan» (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §564).
57. Amends criterion 39. Given a group run whose minimum age is above eighteen, when its signing page renders, then the birth date is the registration form's typed box (BR-REQ-031-04 criteria 36–38): a text box, never the browser's date box, read and shown day first in both languages («11051990» and «11/05/1990» are 11 May, shown «11.05.1990»), with the placeholder «ZZ.LL.AAAA» / «DD.MM.YYYY», the date in words with the age on the run's day as its helper, and a whole date under the run's minimum refused live under the box with «Vârsta minimă pentru această alergare este {age}…». The press reads the typed text with the same `normalizeTypedDate`, so the service gets `YYYY-MM-DD` (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §573). Verification: unit `group-run-declarations/birth-date-day-first.test.ts`; e2e `group-run-declaration.spec.ts`.
58. Given the declaration page, when the sign button is pressed with the acceptance box unticked, then the page names that box in its error summary and under the box itself (`aria-invalid`, a helper text), with or without a script, and the server's refusal for an unticked box lands on `?invalid=accept` with the same two messages — never a silent press and never only the generic refusal sentence (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §616).
59. Given «Setări → Emailuri», then «Adresele clubului care resping emailuri» is drawn only for a role that reads the registrations, asserted on the server, and only when a club mailbox refused an archive copy, a confirmation notice or a copy in thirty days: per address — written only while it is still in «Copiile clubului», otherwise by its role's words alone — the role, the last refused message, its day and cause, the count and one thing to do, the panel open by itself and amber only while an address still in the settings asks for something; the club's account's refusals and any participant's, subscriber's, invitee's or colleague's address are never there (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §671).

**Verification:** integration `registrations/lifecycle.test.ts` (criterion 6), `registrations/signed-declaration.test.ts` (7–10), `registrations/declaration-archive.test.ts` (11), `notifications/club-notices.test.ts` (11–13), `jobs/retention.test.ts`; unit `legal-documents/merge-fields.test.ts`, `notifications/club-notices.test.ts`, `notifications/delivery.test.ts` (12); e2e `registration-form.spec.ts`; integration `notifications/club-copy.test.ts` (14, 16); unit `notifications/club-copy.test.ts` (14), `registrations/mask-id-document.test.ts` (16)

#### BR-REQ-033-03 — Staff cannot sign for a participant

- **Source:** BR-BUS-033, BR-BUS-037
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.8, §15.8
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given the backoffice, when a registration in `PENDING_DECLARATION` is opened, then no action marks the declaration as signed.
2. Given an administrator, when they attempt to move a registration directly to `CONFIRMED` through the API, then it is rejected.
3. Given a restart of a cancelled registration, when it completes, then the registration re-enters the flow before declaration acceptance.

**Verification:** integration `backoffice/declaration-guard.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-033-04 — Restart matches verification state

- **Source:** BR-BUS-033, BR-BUS-037
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.5, §15.1
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a cancelled registration whose participant is not verified, when it is restarted while registration is open, then the status becomes `PENDING_EMAIL_CONFIRMATION` and a verification email is queued.
2. Given an expired registration whose participant is verified, when it is restarted and a place is free, then the status becomes `PENDING_DECLARATION` without a new verification email.
3. Given the same case when the event is full, then the status becomes `WAITLISTED` at the end of the queue.
4. Given any restart, when it runs, then it passes through the same capacity transaction and cannot leapfrog an existing waiting-list entry.
5. Given any restart, when it completes, then the status is never `CONFIRMED`.
6. Given a cancelled or expired registration restarted through the public form, verified or not, when the restart completes, then `answers_written_at` is the restart's instant and `created_at` is unchanged; a first submission writes both on its own instant, the family sitting's correction of a form not yet confirmed (§543) moves it to the correction's instant, and every row written before the column carries its `created_at` (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §656).

**Verification:** integration `registrations/restart.test.ts`

### 4.5 Waiting list and promotion

#### BR-REQ-035-01 — Joining the waiting list

- **Source:** BR-BUS-035
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.7
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a full capped internal event with registration open, when a visitor opens it, then the waiting list is offered instead of direct registration.
2. Given a waiting-list submission, when the email is confirmed, then the status becomes `WAITLISTED` and no place is consumed.
3. Given an entry on the waiting list, when it is created, then no declaration is requested yet.
4. Given a closed or started event, when a waiting-list join is attempted, then it is rejected.
5. Given a capped event whose waiting list has a limit, and a line (WAITLISTED plus offers still open) already at it, when a registration would join the line, then it is refused at every door (the public form, the email confirmation, a restart, a staff entry, the desk, a batch of test rows) and the refusal writes nothing, the token spend included.
6. Given an event whose waiting-list limit is 0, when its places are gone, then registration closes as full and neither the event page nor the form mentions a waiting list.
7. Given a limit lowered below the current line, when it is saved, then nobody already waiting is removed, and the next registration is refused until the line is shorter than the limit.
8. Given a waiting list with no room and a declaration hold past its deadline, when a newcomer registers, then exactly one lapsed hold (the oldest deadline first) is released and the newcomer gets the place directly, and the event page and the form count that place as free before they register.
9. Given two registrations racing for the last slot in a limited line, when both are confirmed at once, then exactly one joins it and the other is refused.
10. While an event has no free place and its waiting list takes people, the listing card says in bold «Mulțumim! Toate cele {capacity} de locuri s-au ocupat — {n} așteaptă deja un loc.» (with nobody waiting, «… Fii primul pe lista de așteptare.»), then «Intră pe lista de așteptare — te anunțăm pe email când se eliberează un loc.», then the registration window in the quiet ink, above the waiting list's button; the event page says the same two sentences above the button and, under it, the room left in a capped list and «Când se eliberează un loc, primești un email și ai {hours} să confirmi — altfel locul trece mai departe.» with the club's own offer window; the join form says the thank-you lead, the join sentence and the offer sentence once, above the form; both languages (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §587).
11. While an event has free places and somebody is on its waiting list, the card and the event page say «{n} pe lista de așteptare» beside the free places, counted from the same cached read as the free places, with no further query (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §587).
12. A full waiting list reads «Locurile s-au ocupat și lista de așteptare e plină — ne pare rău.» and an event with no waiting list «Locurile s-au ocupat, iar acest eveniment nu are listă de așteptare.», with no button, on the card, the event page and the join form (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §587).
13. While an event has no free place and its waiting list has a limit and room, the listing card says the room left in the event page's words — «Mai sunt {n} locuri pe lista de așteptare» — between «Intră pe lista de așteptare …» and the quiet window, not bold; a waiting list with no limit adds no line (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §594).
14. While an event has free places and its waiting list has an open offer, the card and the event page say «{n} loc oferit din lista de așteptare» (Romanian's three forms through `countForm`; "{n} place(s) offered from the waiting list") after the free places, then «{n} pe lista de așteptare» only for the people with no offer yet — never the person offered counted as still waiting — both from the same cached read as the free places; the backoffice queue panel's «Oferite» is the same count (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §612).
15. Given an event whose list is published and whose «Lista de așteptare e publică» is off, when the registration form renders under a notice that describes the states, then the caption under «Vreau să apar» names only «Înscris, în așteptarea confirmării» and «Confirmat» and says that, while waiting for a place on the waiting list, the runner does not appear on the public list; with the switch on it names «Pe lista de așteptare» too (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §628).
16. Given a registration on the waiting list of an event whose «Ofertele din lista de așteptare pleacă automat» is on, when its own page (the manage link) or «Toate înscrierile mele» is opened, then under its state it says «Ești pe locul {n} din {m} persoane de pe lista de așteptare.» / "You are number {n} of {m} on the waiting list." — {n} is 1 plus the `WAITLISTED` registrations of the event ahead in the allocator's own order (`waitlisted_at`, then `id`) and {m} every `WAITLISTED` registration of the event, from one reader (`readWaitlistPosition`); Romanian's three forms through `countForm` on {m}; a one-person page shows the state's words as a heading above it; alone in the line it reads «Ești singura persoană pe lista de așteptare. Locurile eliberate se oferă în ordine.» rather than «locul 1 din 1 persoană»; a registration that is not waiting, or whose event is cancelled (no offer will go out, §331), shows no such sentence; an offered, cancelled or expired registration is not in the line, a `TEST` registration is, and the sentence names nobody else (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §629).
17. While the event's «Ofertele din lista de așteptare pleacă automat» is on, the sentence adds «Locurile eliberate se oferă în ordine.» / "Freed places are offered in order."; while it is off the page says no position and no length as a place: «Ești pe lista de așteptare, împreună cu alte {m−1} persoane. Clubul alege cui oferă un loc eliberat.» / "You are on the waiting list, with {m−1} others. The club chooses whom to offer a freed place." — Romanian's forms through `countForm` on {m−1} («cu o altă persoană», «cu alte 9 persoane», «cu alte 20 de persoane»), and «Ești singura persoană pe lista de așteptare.» followed by the same setting's sentence when {m} is 1, with offers in order too; the setting is read as it stands when the page is opened (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §629).
18. The places line of the event page — in every state that shows it — ends with «{n} pe lista de așteptare» / "{n} on the waiting list" whenever anybody is `WAITLISTED` (`readPublicPlaces`'s `waitlisted`, the cached count the door already reads, no further query, a test registration counted as in every public count), after the confirmed, in-progress and kept parts: «150 de înscriși din 150 de locuri — 133 de confirmați, 17 în curs de confirmare, 4 locuri păstrate pentru lista de așteptare, 10 pe lista de așteptare»; with nobody waiting the line is as it was; the number is said once in a card — in the full state the lead's «… — {n} așteaptă deja un loc.» carries it and the places line leaves it out, and the open state's separate «{n} pe lista de așteptare» line is the places line's part (amends criterion 11); it also amends BR-REQ-035-01 criterion 14 and BR-REQ-034-01 criteria 15 and 16: on the event page the places line, with the waiting count as its last part, comes first, and the open offer's «{n} loc oferit din lista de așteptare» line follows it, where the listing card (no places line) still says the offer, then the waiting; the places line may have a fourth part, the people waiting, after the three of criterion 16 (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §629).
19. Given the event editor or the create page, when the registration box shows «Pe site», then beside «Număr de locuri» a select «Lista de așteptare» / "Waiting list" offers, in order, «Nelimitată» (stores null), «Limitată la un număr de locuri» (reveals «Cel mult atâtea persoane pe listă», min 1, and stores that number) and «Fără listă de așteptare» (stores 0), opens on the stored value (null → «Nelimitată», 0 → «Fără listă», N → «Limitată»), says the three meanings in an «i» beside it in both languages, and switches «Limitată» to «Nelimitată», emptying the box, when its number is left empty or at 0; the server saves a posted «Limitată» with an empty or 0 number as null, ignores the number under the other two answers, refuses an answer that is none of the three naming the select, and stores 0 only for «Fără listă de așteptare», while a caller that sends only the number keeps §348's meaning: empty no limit, 0 no list, N a limit (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §633).
20. Given an event whose «Arată public câți așteaptă» (`events.waitlist_count_public`, true by default) is off, then no public surface says how many wait: the full state's lead on the card, the event page and the join form reads «Mulțumim! Toate cele {capacity} de locuri s-au ocupat. Intră pe lista de așteptare.» / "Thank you! All {capacity} places are taken. Join the waiting list." (never «— {n} așteaptă deja un loc» nor «Fii primul»); the places line ends without «{n} pe lista de așteptare», and while places are still free without «{n} locuri păstrate pentru lista de așteptare»; while places are given from the line the card and the page keep «Locurile se dau din lista de așteptare» and the offered places and drop the people waiting; the room line «Mai sunt {n} locuri pe lista de așteptare» stays; a waiting person's own page, «Toate înscrierile mele» and the `WAITLIST_JOINED` email say no position in either setting — «Ești pe lista de așteptare.» followed by «Locurile eliberate se oferă în ordine.» or «Clubul alege cui oferă un loc eliberat.», and «Când am trimis acest email, erai pe lista de așteptare; locurile eliberate se oferă în ordine.» / «…; clubul alege cui oferă un loc eliberat.» — never the line's length, a place in it (the newest's place would be the length) nor «singura persoană»; the door, the allocator and every count are unchanged (a `TEST` row counts as a real one), and with the switch on every sentence is as before (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §634).
21. Given an event whose «Arată public numărătoarea» is off, then the waiting person's own page, «Toate înscrierile mele» and the `WAITLIST_JOINED` email say criterion 20's private sentence whatever «Arată public câți așteaptă» says — no length of the line and no place in it; criterion 20's "with the switch on every sentence is as before" holds only while «Arată public numărătoarea» is ticked (2026-10-06, `DECISIONS.md` §669).

**Verification:** integration `waitlist/join.test.ts`; e2e `waitlist.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-035-02 — FIFO offers

- **Source:** BR-BUS-035
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.7
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given three waiting entries, when a place is released, then the entry with the earliest `waitlisted_at` is offered, ties broken by stable ID.
2. Given an offer, when it is created, then the status becomes `WAITLIST_OFFERED`, a hold with a 24-hour deadline is set, a scoped token is created, and an offer email is queued.
3. Given an offer, when the deadline would fall after registration close or event start, then it is capped at the earlier of the two.
4. Given concurrent promotion attempts, when they run, then exactly one offer is created for one place.
5. Given the club's offer window (unset = 24 hours), when a waiting-list place is offered, then the offer lasts the club's hours at that moment, capped by the close and the start; an offer already out keeps its deadline when the setting changes (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §377).
6. Given an event whose registration has closed or whose start has passed, when a place is freed, then nobody on the waiting list is emailed an offer, because an offer made then would expire the moment it is made. The waiting list stays WAITLISTED for the desk, an offer made before the close lapses at the close and is not handed on, and the `/admin/emails` forecast promises no such offer (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §420).
7. A waiting-list offer whose first `WAITLIST_SPOT_OFFER` is still queued is not lapsed before the close or the start. It occupies its place, the sweep and the maintenance job leave it, the forecast foresees no lapse for it, and its send re-bases the deadline from the send, with the SENT mark and the move committed together under the event's lock (tests/integration/notifications/deadline-rebase.test.ts, tests/integration/notifications/forecast.test.ts).
8. Given a full event with somebody waiting, when a place is released by a staff cancellation, a participant's cancellation, an erasure or a lapsed offer, then in the same transaction the head of the queue is `WAITLIST_OFFERED` and its offer email is queued marked to leave now, and it is sent after that request's response whatever the delivery timing says (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §596).
9. Given a scheduled capped event before its close with a WAITLISTED registration and fewer places occupied than its capacity — after a family reservation or place hold lapsed, or the event was cancelled and put back on — when the maintenance job next runs (and its plan wakes it at that lapse), then the free place is offered through the same locked allocation as a cancellation, never after the close, never on a cancelled event, and never to more people than there are places (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §612).
10. Given the queue panel of an event, when it lists the waiting, then each `WAITLISTED` row says when its current form was sent (`greatest(submitted_at, privacy_acknowledged_at)`, so a form sent again after a restart is dated by the second) beside when the person joined the line, in the event's zone; the rows are in that order, then id, when the event's `waitlist_auto_offer` is false and in `waitlisted_at` order, then id, the allocator's own, when it is true; the lead line says which; and the allocator, `lockOldestWaitlisted` and every automatic offer still use `waitlisted_at` alone (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §627).
11. The position a waiting person is told is the order the allocator offers places in. With `waitlist_auto_offer` on: given three waiting registrations, the one with the earliest `waitlisted_at` reads «locul 1», a tie on the instant is broken by stable id, and when the person ahead is offered a place the next one reads «locul 1» at the next opening of the page. With it off: each of the three reads how many others wait («cu alte 2 persoane») and that the club chooses, and none reads a position (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §629).

**Verification:** integration `waitlist/promotion.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-035-03 — Accepting, declining, and expiring an offer

- **Source:** BR-BUS-035
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.7, §15.7
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given an active offer, when the participant signs the declaration before the deadline, then the status becomes `CONFIRMED`.
2. Given an active offer, when the participant declines, then the place is released immediately and the next eligible entry is offered.
3. Given an active offer, when the deadline passes, then the status becomes `EXPIRED` with `expiry_reason = WAITLIST_OFFER_LAPSED`, an expiry email is queued, and the next entry is offered.
4. Given a held offer, when the public count is read, then the held place is not shown as available.
5. Given an expired offer, when registration is still open, then the participant may rejoin at the end of the queue.
6. Given a waiting-list offer whose deadline was after an unreachable window's start, when the window's deadlines move, then the offer's deadline and its live `WAITLIST_OFFER` token move together by the same amount, capped by the close and the start — an offer made after a closed window's end (`offer_created_at`) not at all, one made inside it by what was left of the window; and given an offer, a family's reservation or held place whose deadline passed while the platform was unreachable and whose counted place was given meanwhile (`computeOccupied` would exceed the capacity under the event lock), it is not revived: its deadline and `updated_at` are put back, the run's sweep lapses it as it would have, it is audited `registration.not_revived_for_outage` with no actor and kept by id in the window's `not_revived`; the job never marks a row outside the places and never raises a capacity (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §657).
7. Given an unreachable window and a claim the allocator or the sweep had already lapsed inside it before the run — an offer or a declaration hold `EXPIRED` (`WAITLIST_OFFER_LAPSED`, `DECLARATION_HOLD_LAPSED`) whose `hold_expires_at`, an address link `EXPIRED`/`EMAIL_CONFIRMATION_LAPSED` whose lapse, a family's reservation cleared to null by the allocator (`reservation_lapsed_at`, after the window began, and `reservation_lapsed_from`, the deadline that lapsed, both written in the same statement) whose `reservation_lapsed_from`, or an invitation stamped expired whose `expires_at` lies after the step's `since` and at or before the run — for a closed window too — lapsed after the window began — when the step moves that event under its lock, then a claim the step would have revived is left exactly as it is (never revived: status, deadline, stamp and token unchanged), audited once per window `registration.not_revived_for_outage` or `event.invitation_not_revived_for_outage` with no actor and `{ windowId, deadline, lapsedBy: "allocator" }`, kept by id in the window's `not_revived` (kinds `offer`, `declarationHold`, `emailLink`, `familyReservation`, `invitation`) and its count, named in the closed email and counted on «Sarcini» while it waits; a claim lapsed before the window began, one the move would not have revived (`movedDeadline` null: capped by the close or the start before the run, written after a closed window's end) and a family form sent while no place was free — never a reservation, its link moved by the run — are not recorded, and a second run records nothing twice (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §657).

**Verification:** integration `waitlist/offer.test.ts`; e2e `waitlist-claim.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-035-04 — Queue closes at event start

- **Source:** BR-BUS-035
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.5, §16.2
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given waiting-list entries on an event that has started, when maintenance runs, then each becomes `EXPIRED` with `expiry_reason = EVENT_STARTED`.
2. Given that transition, when it occurs, then no email is sent.
3. Given a started event, when the backoffice is opened, then no registration remains in `WAITLISTED`.

**Verification:** integration `waitlist/closure.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-035-05 — Exceptional promotion is audited

- **Source:** BR-BUS-035, BR-BUS-037
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.7, §15.8
- **Priority:** SHOULD
- **Release:** M2

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given the backoffice, when an administrator promotes the next entry, then no reason is required.
2. Given the backoffice, when an administrator promotes a specific entry out of order, then a reason is required and the action is refused without one.
3. Given an exceptional promotion, when it completes, then an audit row records the actor, the target, the reason, and the time.
4. Given any promotion, when it runs, then it cannot exceed capacity or bypass declaration acceptance.
5. Given a lapsed declaration hold after the registration close while somebody waits, when the maintenance job runs, then the hold is released to the queue as §160 says, its provisional number with it, and the desk can promote the waiting runner into the freed place instead of being told the event is full (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §420).
6. Criterion 5 above describes the provisional numbers of §214/§220/§444 and is superseded by this decision: a number exists only once the registration is confirmed (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).

**Verification:** integration `backoffice/promotion.test.ts`

### 4.6 Participant self-service

#### BR-REQ-036-01 — Unregistration requires an explicit action

- **Source:** BR-BUS-036
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §15.5
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a management link, when it is opened, then the registration is not changed and a confirmation page is presented.
2. Given the confirmation page, when the participant confirms, then the status becomes `CANCELLED`, the place is released, a cancellation email is queued, and waiting-list promotion is evaluated.
3. Given a link that has already been used to cancel, when it is opened again, then the current state is shown and no duplicate action occurs.
4. Given an event that has started, when unregistration is attempted, then it is refused with an explanation.
5. Given a pending declaration, a waiting-list entry, or an active offer, when the participant cancels, then the same explicit-confirmation behavior applies.
6. A registration cancelled from the participant's own manage link, or from "my registrations", lands on a page that also says so in a toast ("Gata: înscrierea ta e anulată." / "Done: your registration is cancelled."), shown once. A refused link or an event that has already started shows no toast.
7. Every unsent row in the /admin/emails queue says when it leaves: the estimated next round; late, past the health check's overdue threshold; held until a family sitting ends, read from the email's sittingHeld / familyHeld flag; being sent now; or never, once every attempt is spent (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §529).
8. «Trimite acum» asks first, names the emails due now, and counts those that stay held by reason (family, retry, the newsletter's reserve) as plain numbers. A late row tells only the Administrator to press it; other readers are told to tell the administrator (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §529).
9. Given the /admin/emails queue, when it counts the rows held for later, then a row flagged `sittingHeld` or `familyHeld` counts only as the family's hold and an unflagged row with a future turn counts only as a retry (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §536).
10. On a family's step of the declarations wizard, «Renunț la înscrierea pentru <nume>» asks first naming the person, then cancels only that step's registration through the allocator — the place released, an audit row `registration.cancelled_by_participant` with no staff actor and `{ from, via: FAMILY_WIZARD }`, the cancellation email queued — and the wizard shows the person as «înscriere anulată» and moves to the next person or to the end; a person who is not the current step, or a press without the pass or the person's own live link, is refused and nothing changes (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §547).
11. Every cancellation queues one `REGISTRATION_CANCELLED` per person, «Înscrierea pentru <nume> la <eveniment> a fost anulată», naming the event's date, «Locul a fost eliberat.» or that the person left the waiting list, and who the address still holds at the event with their states, from the address's own rows only (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §547).
12. Given «Nu mai pot ajunge» in an email about one registration, when its link is opened, then the registration's own manage page shows the person's cancel with «De ce anulezi?» and nothing changes; on a family's one confirmation or the family sitting's message it opens «Înscrierile mele», which lists each person's cancel. When the cancel is confirmed with a reason, then the registration is cancelled through the allocator and one REGISTRATION_CANCELLED email is queued (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §558).
13. Given a participant cancelling their own registration from the manage page, «Înscrierile mele» or the family wizard's «Renunț», when the press carries no answer to «De ce anulezi?», «Alt motiv» with no words, or more than 200 characters, then the same page names that box, the link is not spent and nobody is cancelled; when it carries «Accidentare sau boală», «Alt program» or «Alt motiv» with its words, then the registration is cancelled and keeps the answer and the words, and the audit row carries the answer alone, never the words (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §558).

**Verification:** integration `registrations/unregister.test.ts`; e2e `unregister.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-036-02 — Email action tokens are safe

- **Source:** BR-BUS-036, BR-BUS-070
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §12.8, §13.2
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a token, when it is stored, then only its hash is persisted.
2. Given a token, when it is used for a purpose other than the one it was issued for, then it is rejected.
3. Given a token, when it is past its expiry, used, or invalidated, then it is rejected.
4. Given any GET request carrying a token, when it is handled, then no state is mutated.
5. Given a new token for the same purpose and registration, when it is issued, then previous active tokens for that purpose are invalidated.
6. Given repeated validation attempts presenting the same token, when they exceed the limit for the window, then further attempts are refused with the same generic response an unknown token receives, and the limit is keyed on the token's hash rather than on the caller.
7. Given a consent withdrawal, when the manage token names no registration, or the form posts a registration id that is not a uuid, then it is refused as NOT_FOUND like any bad token, and never reaches the database as a cast error (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §324).
8. Each runner on an address confirms, signs their own declaration, gets their own race number and QR code, and is erased on their own. "Înscrierile mele" names each runner, and every email greets the runner of the registration it is about (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §389).
9. Signing binds the version read: a different version in force at the press is refused and nothing is written. The text is signed in the page's language, and the PDF and the email follow it (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §393).
10. Signing writes one `group_run_declarations` row and no participant, registration or place. It queues the signer's copy with the whole PDF and, when the club has a declarations mailbox, the archive copy with the identity document masked, in the same transaction. Neither message carries a token or an action link (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §393).
11. Every wrong box is named at once in a focusable summary, and nothing is recorded. The honeypot and the timing check answer as a signature and write nothing. A throttle keyed on the hashed canonical address answers "limited". A posted event or document id that is not a uuid gets the form's refusal before any query (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §393).
12. Given a family on one address (§389), when the form is sent once per child and each time emails a "register another person" link, then every one of those links stays usable for the club's email-link window, not only the newest. Each link registers at most one person and is single use. The address's limit is counted under the event's lock whenever a link is used, so several live links never add a registration the limit refuses (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §420).
13. Given a live declaration link on a registration that has since moved on (cancelled, expired or confirmed), when it is opened, then the page says where the registration stands instead of showing a form whose press fails. A signature that loses a race on a still-signable state says it could not be recorded (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §420).
14. The family link's email carries a second answer, «Nu înscriu această persoană», on the same single-use token; opening it (GET) changes nothing, and the press (POST) spends the token and deletes the kept form in one transaction.
15. The wizard is carried by a sealed, HTTP-only, short-lived pass. It is bound to the opened link (spent by its own signature, or live while its own person was put off) or to the live «Înscrierile mele» link, and to the people fixed when it was issued. It signs only the current step, never outlives the earliest hold still running, and signs nobody once done (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §471).
16. The fresh link wins: beside a live declaration link, a pass opened on another person is ignored, and a link's own signature with no wizard after it takes back any pass the device held (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §471).
17. «Semnează declarațiile» on «Înscrierile mele» exchanges the live link on the server for the wizard's pass, with no new token in any URL. When refused, a dead link keeps the page's notice; nobody left to sign, or one person alone, lands back on the list with an information toast naming the reason, in both languages (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §471).
18. While delivery is `scheduled`, the screen after the registration form, the resend page and the newsletter pop-up state the wait as the pinger's cadence at that hour (read from the public data cache), never «within a minute» (§513).
19. Given a family sitting of two people or more, when its one REGISTER_ANOTHER_PERSON message is sent, then it carries exactly one single-use token, hashed at rest and minted at send time, tied to the sitting. Opening its page writes nothing and spends nothing. Pressing it spends the token, confirms the address and gives each ticked person a place through the allocator, each in a savepoint of their own (§519).
20. Given a group run that is one of a series (§113: the same type and default-language title) and a text in force that names `{{series}}`, when the same person (the canonical address and the typed name read without case, accents or extra spaces) signs again on any date of the series under the same version, then no second row is written: the kept signature's copy is queued again, no second archive copy is queued, and the page answers exactly as it does to a first signature. A unique index on (version, series or date, signer) holds two presses at once to one row (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §523).
21. A signature covers the series only when the text version it signs names `{{series}}` in the signer's language. Under a text that names none, the row's `series_key` is null, the signature covers the date it was signed on only, the next date takes its own signature, and the signer's link opens that date (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §523).
22. A signature of a newer version is written beside the older one, which is kept as evidence and keeps its version relied upon. No public press deletes a signature. The Administrator's audited erase removes one, and a series signature erased no longer reads as signed on any date of the series, with one audit row (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §523).
23. The signing page, the signing press and the PDF fill a group-run text from one function (`groupRunMergeValues`). A series' text keeps the series sentence, with the series' name, rhythm and usual place, and drops the one-off sentence. A one-off run's text keeps the one-off sentence and drops the series sentence. When the series' place is not written in that language, the series sentence is kept without its place clause, and the series sentence and the one-off sentence are never both dropped (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §523).
24. A signature keeps the blanks as they were filled (`signed_facts`: the run's and the series' facts, nothing personal), and its PDF is drawn from them. Deleting a date moves a series' signatures to the next date, or to the latest before it when it was the last, and the PDF reads the same after the move. A one-off's signatures go with their date (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §523).
25. The signer's copy carries a link minted at send time, with only its SHA-256 on the row. Opened on a date the signature covers, the run's page says «Ai semnat deja declarația pentru aceste alergări (v. N, semnată …)» and shows no button while the signed version is in force. After a newer version, it says the older one was signed and offers the button again. Any other link reads nothing, and opening it changes nothing (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §523).
26. The run's offer line, the signing page's intro and its answer say that a returning runner signs once for the whole series only while the text in force names `{{series}}`. Otherwise they say this run, in both languages (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §523).
27. Both emails of a series signature name the series and its rhythm in the subject and body, in both language halves. A one-off's emails name the run and claim no series (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §523).
28. `/admin/tasks` shows «Declarațiile alergărilor de grup: o semnătură pe serie» as open while a group-run text in force does not name `{{series}}`, as done when every one does, and not at all while none is in force. Its words say that until the new versions are approved, a signature still covers one date (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §523).
29. The privacy-notice template says, in both languages, that a group-run declaration is signed once for a run that repeats. Signed again on the same text, no second signature is kept. Signed on a new version, the new one is in force and the older is kept as evidence of what was accepted then. Both are deleted only at the signer's request, and the archive copy is deleted three years from the signing (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §523).
30. The family's one email opens with «Înscriere de familie: Ana, Mihai și Ioana — 3 locuri rezervate până la HH:MM.», names every person of the sitting with their place («loc rezervat» or «pe lista de așteptare, după confirmare») until the sitting's fixed deadline and never past it, and who the address held before with their state, and its single-use link confirms all of them in one press and opens the declaration wizard for each (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §543).
31. «Gestionează înscrierea» lists the link's registration and every other active registration of the same address at the same event, each with their own QR, name, number, state, public-list choice and «Anulează înscrierea pentru <nume>», never another address's registration nor a link to the signed declaration's PDF; a press naming a registration of another address or event is refused with nothing spent, and a cancellation from the page spends the link whichever person it cancels; a press naming a sibling that is no longer active is refused the same way, and the «sent by email» sentence appears only for an online signature, a paper one saying «Declarația a fost semnată pe hârtie.» (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §547).
32. Every group-run self-declaration signed from now stores `text_hash`, the SHA-256 of the exact text its PDF prints in the signer's language, computed before the row is written in the same transaction; the signer's PDF and the archive copy print the proof line from the row, and the backoffice signatures list shows the hash's first 12 characters with the whole one in a tooltip. An Administrator may hold a signature for a complaint or a dispute: while held, no erase — single, batch, or the delete of the run's last date — takes it; once cleared, the erase goes through, with an audit row for each set and clear naming the event and the declaration id, never the signer (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §556). Verification: integration `group-run-declarations/proof-and-hold.test.ts`; e2e `group-run-declaration.spec.ts`.
33. Every group run's page shows «Reguli de siguranță» / “Safety rules” inside «Condiții de participare», above the optional declaration: seven short lines in both languages naming the club from `CLUB_NAME` — no individual supervision and no (mountain) guiding, the runner's own pace and way, shoes, clothing and a headlamp on a night run, own water and food, running only if their health allows it as far as they know, the organiser's general safety instructions for animals and weather, telling the organiser when leaving the group. No line names a number, «platforma» or «de obicei»; a race's page shows none of it (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §556). Verification: unit `group-run-declarations/safety-rules.test.ts`; e2e `event-pages.spec.ts`, `group-run-declaration.spec.ts`.
34. The link behind «Nu mai pot ajunge» is the message's own MANAGE_REGISTRATION token where the message already mints one; a family's MANAGE_PROFILE link on the family's one confirmation and the family sitting's message; and on every other message a MANAGE_REGISTRATION token minted at send time, hashed at rest, with the manage link's fourteen days, which supersedes the older one. Given the address to confirm and then the declaration request on the same registration, a send retried after a failure, or an ORGANIZER_MESSAGE after the confirmation, then each message's button is a live manage link at `#cancel` and the newest one works. A club copy mints none (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §558).
35. Given an action link invalidated because a newer link of the same purpose was issued, when it is opened, then the page says a newer email with a working link was sent (and when), tells the person to open the latest email and to check Spam and Promotions, and keeps the resend path; a link revoked for cause, an unknown token and a link opened at another endpoint still get the one generic response (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §619).
36. A declaration link minted for a registration that holds a declaration replaces that registration's live waiting-list offer link: the offer link is refused as `SUPERSEDED`, its `superseded_by_token_id` names the declaration link and its page says a newer email has the working link, so an open offer put on «Lista de invitați speciali» leaves one working link to sign with (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
37. An `ACCEPT_INVITATION` link is scoped to its invitation, hashed at rest and minted at the send; opening it reads in a read-only transaction and spends nothing; the form's press spends it once, a refused form takes the spend back, and a resend's newer link makes the older one read as replaced (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §647).
38. The invitation link's press asks, under the event lock and before it spends the token, whether the invitation is still open and before its deadline and the event still scheduled and not started; refused, it spends nothing, lands on the link's page with the refusal (withdrawn, expired, cancelled), and the page then reads the same — never «already used» for a person who has no registration (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §647).
39. An invitation's link to an event called off since the send (`CANCELLED`) — the GET and the press alike — says the event was cancelled («Evenimentul a fost anulat, așa că invitația nu mai e valabilă.»), never that the deadline passed; a race already started, or an event marked «Încheiat» (`COMPLETED`) even with its start ahead, says expired, never cancelled; the token stays unspent (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §647).

**Verification:** integration `tokens/action-tokens.test.ts`, `tokens/token-throttle.test.ts`

### 4.7 Backoffice

#### BR-REQ-037-01 — Registration detail shows the full picture

- **Source:** BR-BUS-037
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §14
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a registration, when an administrator opens it, then the view shows name, delivery email, canonical duplicate identity, status, email confirmation state, declaration version and acceptance time, waiting-list time or offer deadline, hold deadline, confirmation and cancellation times, email delivery history, and audit history.
2. Given an Author or Editor, when they attempt to open any registration view, then access is refused.
3. Given the registration's backoffice page, when it shows the timeline, then the first line says «Formular trimis» / «Form sent» with the current cycle's time, the same value the queue panel shows, in both languages, to the staff roles that read the registrations only; no public list and no participant page carries that time of anybody else's (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §627).
4. Given the registration's backoffice page, when the Administrator or the Organizer opens it, then under the state and above the verbs a block «Ce îi spui» / «What to tell them» says, in the registration's language (a caption names it when it differs from the reader's), the state once — as the participant's own page names it, a confirmed row in the confirmation's own sentence, a cancelled or expired row in its own sentence with no «Înscrierea ta: …» frame — and that page's own sentence for it: the declaration's deadline, a family's reservation, the waiting line under the event's settings, a check-in, a cancelled event alone; where an email or a public page already words the thing (the address to confirm, the waiting list, an open offer with its deadline written as the offer email writes it, the race number, a cancelled row, a lapsed declaration hold), those words, a unit test holding each pair equal in both languages; the expiry of the live link the state waits on (the address's, the declaration's or the offer's, never the «Nu mai pot veni» link) only where it adds an instant the block has not given — never on a held place, whose declaration link lives until the start, and never once the address link or the offer has lapsed — the spam hint while a link in an email is the next step and has left, and no address and no link; every line under 200 characters in both languages, all from the page's existing query (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §655).
5. Given a registration with an email that was bounced or marked as spam, when «Ce îi spui» renders, then it ends with one sentence in the registration's language saying an email the club sent to the person's address was rejected (or marked as spam) and the club's messages do not reach them, on any state, confirmed included, with no address; after a bounce it does not also tell the person to look in spam (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §663).
6. Amends criterion 5. «Ce îi spui» adds the rejected email's sentence while the address refuses the club's mail, and for an email of the registration's own that the club's account could not send, or one owed to an address that works again, says it has not reached them yet and will be sent again, naming what the press that clears it sends — the confirmation with its QR code for anything the confirmation carries, the race-day details for the reminder — never as the address's refusal and never for a family member's email; for «Detalii actualizate» of their own that did not reach them it says the event's details changed and are on the event's page, promising nothing; it drops the spam hint for every email of theirs refused and not sent again, and keeps it for a complaint, for an email sent again, for a family member's email owed and for «Detalii actualizate» owed (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §670).
7. Given a registration's page, then «Emailuri» — after the resend presses, at `#emailuri`, open by itself only while the email state asks somebody to act — tells the state's whole story (the email's long name, when, why by its cause, whether the address had been confirmed, the provider's code, once, and redacted words in small print), then lists every email newest first in two lines — its short name and whom it was for by role, and «În coadă», «Trimis», «Livrat», «Trimis prin Gmail · fără confirmare de livrare», «Respins — {cause}», «Marcat ca spam» or «Nu a plecat — {reason}» with its day, «Retrimis de {staff}» on a resend by hand — the participant's own five in view and the rest behind «Toate emailurile (n)», the club's archive copies, notices and copies behind «și cele către club (n)», never a club mailbox's address; under the address the list's line links to it with one to-do by reader (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §671).
8. Given «Ce îi spui» on a registration whose address refuses the club's mail, then it names the email in the participant's words and its date, the cause in plain words and what the person can do — make room, add the club to the contacts, say whether the address is right — and on a confirmed registration ends «Locul tău e confirmat; la masă te găsim după nume.», never suggesting a new registration; an email owed is said as owed, a family member's owed email and an email sent again say nothing (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §671).

**Verification:** integration `backoffice/registration-detail.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-037-02 — Resend is state-derived

- **Source:** BR-BUS-037, BR-BUS-080
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §15.8, §16.3
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M2

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given each registration status, when an administrator resends, then the message type matches the mapping in `SETUP.md` §21 and no other type can be selected; for a confirmed registration that is the confirmation itself (`REGISTRATION_CONFIRMED`, with the desk code, its QR and the manage link — "retrimite QR-ul" in the list), not a bare manage link (2026-09-18, `DECISIONS.md` §79); and, while the registration is confirmed and the event has not started, the reminder by name ("Trimite reminderul", `EVENT_REMINDER`) — refused otherwise (`DECISIONS.md` §81).
2. Given a resend, when it completes, then a new outbox row is created, marked as a manual resend with the acting administrator recorded.
3. Given a resend, when it completes, then the registration status is unchanged and no deadline is extended.
4. Given a cancelled or expired registration, when an administrator resends, then a `REGISTRATION_STATE_NOTICE` is queued that contains no confirmation link and no scoped token.
5. Given repeated resends, when they are attempted, then a rate limit applies and the refusal is recorded.
6. Given the scheduled delivery timing, when an Administrator resends a registration's message or an invitation with «Trimite acum, fără să aștepte trecerea programată», then only that row and its club copies are sent after the response, and an audit row names the staff member, the outbox row and the message type (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §540).
7. With «Pune la coadă pentru trecerea programată» the resent row stays waiting through the request and leaves at the scheduled pass (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §540).
8. A «now» the day's Mailgun allowance cannot hold, the club's copies counted, is refused with its own sentence, queues nothing and spends none of the hour's resends (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §540).
9. «Retrimite invitația» with «Trimite acum» whose Zitadel password link failed or is unconfigured shows no green toast (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §540).
10. Given a registration whose address holds others at the event, when an Administrator presses «Retrimite familiei», then one email is queued for the address at the family's earliest step (the earliest waiting person's verification link, else a declaration request, else one confirmation with every confirmed person's QR), the press is audited as `registration.family_resent`, any other role is refused on the server, and the list shows the family's rows next to each other where its first row falls (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §588). Verification: integration `registrations/one-click-family.test.ts`.
11. Given an event, when an Administrator presses «Retrimite declarația tuturor care nu au semnat», then every PENDING_DECLARATION registration whose declaration email is neither queued nor sent within the last hour and whose resend limit is not spent gets one COMPLETE_DECLARATION email with a fresh link (the earlier link invalidated), through the same path as the single resend; the press is audited with its counts, rate-limited to three an hour per event, Administrator-only, and changes no registration's state (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §606).
12. Given a registration on the waiting list, when an Administrator resends, then one `WAITLIST_JOINED` is queued in the registration's language, marked as a manual resend, rendered at send time with the standing of that moment (§629, §634), no state changes, and the «Nu mai pot ajunge» manage link in it is newly minted and supersedes the earlier email's (`BR-REQ-036-02` criterion 5); a cancelled event refuses it, and the confirmation dialog of «Retrimite emailul» — on the registration's page and in the list's row menu — names the email the press sends for each state, in both languages (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §641).
13. Given an Administrator's resend of `WAITLIST_JOINED`, when it is rendered, then it opens by saying it is sent again and where the person stands — «Îți retrimitem emailul: ești în continuare pe lista de așteptare la {event}.» / “We are sending this again: you are still on the waiting list for {event}.” — and never says the person was just added; the first send keeps «te-am adăugat pe lista de așteptare» (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §654).
14. Given a registration whose address refuses the club's mail, when an Administrator opens «Retrimite QR», «Retrimite familiei», «Retrimite emailul» or «Trimite reminderul», then the question's first sentence says what the last refusal means for the press — Mailgun will not send (suppressed, unsubscribed, a complaint's suppression), the address does not exist, the mailbox was full on that day, the receiving server refused it for its own reason, or it was marked as spam — and «Retrimite declarația tuturor care nu au semnat» opens with how many of those who wait have such an address; an email the club's account could not send or one owed to an address that works again adds nothing, and no warning blocks the press (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §671).

**Verification:** integration `backoffice/resend.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-037-03 — Administrative corrections are bounded

- **Source:** BR-BUS-037
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §14, §10.3, §15.11
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1
- **Status:** built, and recorded in `DECISIONS.md` §33. The administrative changes to a
  registration are entering one, correcting the answers the participant typed — the name among
  them, each field audited («Modifică datele», `DECISIONS.md` §645) — cancelling it, and — since
  `DECISIONS.md` §44 — erasing it (BR-REQ-037-06). There is deliberately no more: no verified-email
  edit, no consent granted by staff and no participant merge.

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a registration, when an administrator corrects the participant name, then the change is audited.
2. Given the backoffice, when it renders, then it offers no verified-email edit and no participant merge.
3. Given any administrative state change, when it completes, then an audit row records actor, action, entity, and time.
4. Given a cancellation by an administrator, when it commits, then the released place is offered to the front of the waiting list, exactly as a participant's own cancellation is.
5. Given a registration, when the backoffice shows it — on its own page and as the "Etapă" column of the list — then its journey is derived from the row as six ordered steps (submitted, email confirmed, place reserved, declaration signed, place confirmed, present), with the step the person is at marked current, a waiting-list entry waiting at the reservation step, a signed declaration recognised whether it was signed online or on paper, the race number on the confirmation and on the check-in, a cancelled or expired row keeping the steps it reached and naming how it ended, and a registration restarted on the same row showing only the steps of its current cycle; the list's cell names the last step done, never the one awaited, and reads the acceptance with one probe per row, never a query per row (added 2026-09-19, `DECISIONS.md` §145).
6. Given a public submission for an event by an address that already holds an active registration for it, when it is accepted, then exactly one `audit_logs` row `registration.resubmitted` is written in the same transaction as the re-send, on that registration, with the participant, no staff actor, and metadata holding only the state found and the message type re-sent (null when nothing was queued); the public answer is identical to a first submission's; the registration's timeline shows each such row as a dated line naming the state and the re-sent message; and the registrations list marks the row "Reînscriere ×N" with the last date, read in one grouped query for the page's rows and counted in no figure the club is given (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §312).
7. A staff name correction that would give a registration the same folded name as another registration on the same address and event is refused on the name field. The check and the update run under the event's lock, so a rename racing a family-link registration of the same name gets that refusal and never a database error (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §389).
8. Declining a family link writes an audit row (event.family_entry_declined) naming the event and the address holder acting by link, never the declined person's name or birth date.
9. Given an Administrator cancelling a registration from its page with «Clubul refuză înscrierea, potrivit termenilor» ticked, then the same cancellation runs — the place released to the front of the waiting list through the allocator, Administrator-only — and the person's cancellation email says, in both languages, that the club refused the registration, quotes the reason typed as the ground and links the terms; the audit row records the actor, the reason and `kind: REFUSED_BY_ORGANIZER`; a refusal with no ground is refused; unticked, the reason never reaches the person (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §618).
10. The dialogs that cancel a registration by staff, one or in bulk, say that the person is emailed at once, that the written reason stays in the journal and is sent only with the refusal under the terms, and that the freed place is offered to the first in line or stays free by the event's setting, in both languages (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §631).
11. Given the registrations list or a registration's page, when a registration is still under way, then its journey names, after its step, the deadline its state waits on — a held place «ține locul până …» or, past it, «termen depășit, locul se ține cât nu-l cere nimeni»; an open offer «loc oferit, până …»; a row waiting for its address «linkul expiră …» or «linkul a expirat», read from the row's own `email_link_expires_at` with no query per row; a family's reservation «loc rezervat până …» — the step on one line and the deadline free to wrap at 320 px, the hover title kept, an ended row naming none; and the registration's page says «Ține locul până» on a live hold and «Linkul din email expiră» on a row waiting for its address (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §635).
12. Given an Administrator on a registration's page, when they open «Datele înscrierii» (the read recorded as `registration.answers_viewed`) and save «Modifică datele», then only the answers that changed from the allowlist — the two names (the name of record following them, refused when another runner on the address carries it), the list name, birth date, sex, citizenship, country, city, phone, emergency contact, guardian, member tick and club, socials, T-shirt — are written, each through the form's own rule and cross-field rules, with one audit row `registration.answer_corrected` `{ field, from, to }` per column (and `registration.name_corrected` for the name of record), no state, place, number or email moved; the address, any consent, the declaration's statements and any non-answer key are refused by name, the guardian is refused once a declaration acceptance exists (the signed declaration still naming who signed) and a birth date that would then need one, or would make the signed minor's row an adult's, is refused on the birth date (`guardianSigned`, with `guardianAdult` for the latter), a guardian is owed and allowed on the one day the row was written — the day its answers were written, `answers_written_at` (a restart's instant, not the row's creation, so a row created while the person was a minor and restarted after the eighteenth birthday is an adult's row: a birth date corrected by a day owes no guardian and clears none of the socials) — on a row written while the person was a minor it cannot be cleared and is required when an adult's row is corrected to such a birth date, though the person is an adult today — a guardian on a row that was not a minor's when written is refused (`guardianAdult`) and a birth date corrected to an adult's before any declaration clears the guardian with its own audit row, the participant's own number as the emergency contact is refused with §231's `emergencySame` marker, a Strava link or username on a row written while the person was a minor is refused with `minorAtRegistration` (the minors' sweep's own test) — on the birth date when only the birth date moved — an empty or unchanged correction is refused, every other role is refused, the Organizer reads the answers with no form, the page's guardian box opens on that same day (`answers_written_at`, its `minorOn` too — closed on a minor's row restarted as an adult, open on one written again while still a minor), and the participant's own page shows the corrected values; the erase, the emergency contact's seven-day retention, and a withdrawal of the socials (by the person or by staff) or the minors' socials sweep remove the corrected values from the trail; verification: integration `registrations/answer-corrections.test.ts`, `registrations/answers-written-at.test.ts`, `registrations/member-tick.test.ts`, `registrations/member-tick-screens.test.ts` (the guardian box asks the day the answers were written, not the row's creation); unit `registrations/answers.test.ts` (`DECISIONS.md` §645; amended 2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §656).
13. Given the registrations list filtered to the members, when an Administrator opens «Bife de membru fără cont de membru», then it previews every ticked real registration in the list's scope (the event, or every event not started) whose canonical address matches no live `staff_users` account of any role, with name, event and masked address, each a ticked box to untick captioned «Bifat = se scoate bifa · debifat = Lasă bifa»; «Scoate bifa la cele {n}» clears, in one transaction, only the posted rows still candidates, each through the answers correction with its audit rows, plus one `registrations.member_ticks_cleared` `{ count, eventId }`; an empty preview says «Nicio bifă de scos»; every other role sees no button and is refused (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §645).
14. Given the registrations list, when its filter row renders in either language, then the members' filter and the bounced filter are each a tick with a glyph and a help line, like «Doar cu oferte și beneficii» — «Doar membrii <club> (declarați)» (`clubMember=1`, the club's name from `CLUB_NAME`) and «Doar cine nu a primit emailul» (`bounced=1`) — ticked exactly when their parameter is `1`, with no select and no empty option borrowed from the events select; the status select's empty option is «Toate stările», not «Toate evenimentele»; the filtering, the exports and the member sweep read the same parameters as before (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §650).
15. Given the registrations list, over one event or every event, when it renders, then a column «Până când» / “Until when”, right after «Unde a ajuns» and in the phone layout too, says on each row the instant it waits on, read from the row with no query per row: a held place's «Poate semna până {instant}» or, past it, «{instant} — termenul a trecut, locul e păstrat»; an open offer's «Poate accepta până {instant}» or «{instant} — termenul ofertei a trecut», an offer whose first email is still queued reading as open (§520); a family's reservation «Locul e rezervat până {instant}» while it holds; else the first email's link «Linkul e valabil până {instant}» or «{instant} — linkul a expirat»; «—» on every other row. `?sort=untilWhen` orders by the same instant, the soonest first and the rows with none last either way; the step's cell says the step alone, in place of criterion 11's deadline in the list's cell; the registration's timeline reads «Rezervarea expiră» on a lapsed offer (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §651).
16. Given the registrations list, over one event or every event, when it renders in either language, then two columns «Oraș» / “City” and «Vârstă» / “Age”, in the phone layout too, say on each row, read from the row and its event with no query per row: the city as typed followed by the country's ISO code in brackets only when the country is not `RO` («Bristol (GB)»), or «—» with no city (`cityLabel`); and the age on the event's day on the event's clock (`ageOnRaceDay`), or «—» with no birth date. A test registration shows both like a real one. `?sort=city` orders by the city, `?sort=age` by the birth date for the youngest first, the rows with none last either way (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §660).
17. Given the registrations list or a registration's page, when a row's participant's canonical address is the canonicalized address of a live account on «Echipa», then the row shows one «Membru (verificat)» chip whatever its member tick; a ticked row with no such account shows «Membru (declarat)»; neither shows no member chip. Each chip has a one-sentence tooltip saying what it means, in both languages. The match goes through `canonicalizeEmail`: a plus tag or `googlemail.com` still matches, and a Gmail dot does not (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §662, amending criterion 14: the members' tick now reads «Doar membrii <club>», without «(declarați)», and keeps the declared and the verified alike).
18. Given «Doar membrii {club}» (`clubMember=1`), when the list, its count, its summary strip or the export is filtered, then it keeps the declared and the verified rows alike, and with no member account it keeps the ticked rows alone. The export's «Club member» column, at the same CSV position, reads `verified`, `declared` or empty. A staff-entered row and a test row are verified by the same rule (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §662).
19. Replaces criterion 14's name of the tick. Given the registrations list, when its filter row renders, then the `bounced=1` tick reads «Doar cu un email respins» / “Only with a rejected email”, its help saying that it can happen after the confirmation and that the row's «Email respins» says which email, when and why; no screen offers staff a way to change the address, and the words say only the person can, by registering again (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §663).
20. «Vreau numărul de membru» is stored only while the event's bib design offers the members' bib and the member tick is on, at every door — the public form, a restart, a staff entry — and false whatever a stale form posts; «Modifică datele» corrects it as an answer, audited `{ field: "memberBibWanted", from, to }`, and clearing the member tick clears a standing wish in the same press with its own audit row (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §664).
21. «Vreau numărul de membru» starts ticked on the public form, on a family sitting's later forms and on an invited member's form, beside «Sunt membru» ticked; it starts unticked only when a refused press brings back the member tick without the wish (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §664).

**Verification:** integration `registrations/staff-crud.test.ts`; unit `registrations/journey.test.ts`; integration `registrations/admin-list.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-037-04 — The queue can be exercised without reaching anyone

- **Source:** BR-BUS-037, BR-BUS-060
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §12.6, §10.6, §15.10
- **Priority:** SHOULD
- **Release:** M1
- **Status:** built, and recorded in `DECISIONS.md` §30. No participant account type
  is added and the staff role enum stays at three: this is a property of the registration.

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a registration of kind `TEST`, when it moves through the lifecycle, then it occupies a place, expires on the same hold deadlines, and is promoted from the waiting list by the same allocator as a `REAL` one; running the same scenario as each kind produces identical transitions.
2. Given the capacity formula and the queue allocator, when they are read, then neither contains any condition on the kind.
3. Given the CSV export, when it is produced, then `TEST` rows are absent from it.
4. Given any screen that lists a registration, when a `TEST` row is shown, then it is labelled unmistakably.
5. Given an Administrator in an environment other than production, when they add N test registrations to an event, then N synthetic participants go through the ordinary submission and confirmation path, each on a distinct address in a reserved domain that can never receive mail.
