6. Given the same Administrator, when they remove the test registrations for that event, then those rows and the synthetic participants behind them are deleted and every real registration is left standing.
7. Given `APP_ENV=production`, when a test registration is attempted, then it is refused in two independent places.
8. Given an Administrator on an event with internal registration, when the event page renders, then it shows the queue as the allocator counts it — places, confirmed, held, free, waiting — and the waiting list numbered in the order it is served, with an offer's deadline where one is out (2026-09-18, `DECISIONS.md` §92).
9. Given `FEATURE_DISPLAY_NAME` unset or not `true`, when the public or the staff form renders, then no display-name field is offered and a posted value is ignored, so the list shows the registered name; the CSV export always carries the first name, the last name and the identity document beside the registered name (2026-09-18, `DECISIONS.md` §95).
10. Given an event held in a time zone other than the club's, when its queue panel renders, then an offer's deadline and the time a runner joined the waiting list are written in the event's own zone, as the runner's email writes them, in the reader's language with the weekday (2026-09-24, `DECISIONS.md` §369, §92, §349). Verification: integration `registrations/queue-panel-zone.test.ts`.
11. Given test registrations at an event, then the desk's counters and the events list's confirmed and checked-in counts leave them out. The desk list still shows them, labelled (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §420).
12. Given a waiting-list offer whose offer email is still queued (PENDING or PROCESSING, marked as starting its deadline) before the close or the start, when the event's queue panel renders, then the offer is listed in the line even past its stored deadline, as the place count holds it, with the words that its deadline runs from when the email leaves and no deadline time; once that email has been sent, an offer past its deadline is no longer listed (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §531, §520). Verification: integration `registrations/queue-panel-zone.test.ts`.

**Verification:** integration `registrations/test-kind.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-037-05 — An Administrator registers somebody who asked in person

- **Source:** BR-BUS-037, BR-BUS-060
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §15.11, §12.6, §12.12
- **Priority:** SHOULD
- **Release:** M1
- **Status:** built, and recorded in `DECISIONS.md` §33.

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given an Administrator and an event whose registration mode is `INTERNAL`, when they enter a name and an email, then a registration is created in `PENDING_EMAIL_CONFIRMATION`, of kind `REAL` and source `STAFF`, carrying the staff user who entered it.
2. Given that registration, when it is created, then the participant receives the ordinary verification email and nothing about it is confirmed by staff.
3. Given a full event with a waiting list, when an Administrator enters a registration and its address is confirmed, then it joins the back of that waiting list and no existing entry loses its position.
4. Given the form, when the relay confirmation is not ticked, then the registration is refused and nothing is written.
5. Given an address that already holds an active registration for that event, when an Administrator enters it, then they are told so plainly rather than receiving the public form's generic answer.
6. Given an Author or an Editor, when any of this is attempted, then it is refused.
7. Given any of these changes, when it completes, then an `audit_logs` row records the actor, the action, the entity and the time.
8. Given the registrations list, when a row renders, then it offers the verbs that apply to its status and the reader role — open, resend, confirm on paper, give a place, check in or undo, cancel — as a menu of the existing actions and never as a free status select, so no transition bypasses the allocator, the signed declaration or the audit row; and given no event in the query, then the list and its export are both filtered to the featured event, with every event one press away (2026-09-20, DECISIONS.md section 178).
9. Given the registrations list with a name search and no event chosen in the address — no `eventId`, an empty one, or an id no longer listed — when it renders, then it searches every event, says so in one line, and the event select shows its automatic option rather than submitting the featured event's id; given an explicit event id or `all`, then that is honoured as it is; and the export follows the same rule, `all` included, so the file is the set on screen (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §312).
10. Given a staff member entering a registration on /admin/registrations/new (the form the desk's walk-in opens), when the birth date typed says the person is under eighteen today, then a box for the parent's or legal guardian's full name appears, as on the public form, and a refusal that names it opens it whatever the date then says. Given a birth date, fourteen or over on the event's day and under eighteen, with the parent named, the registration is entered and keeps the parent's name. Without the name it is refused for guardianName alone, never for age. Given an adult with a name in that box, the row keeps no guardian (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §324).
11. Given the staff entry form, when an event is chosen, then the birth-date box's max is the latest birth date that is still fourteen on that event's calendar day in its own time zone, never later than today, and its min is 120 years ago. The limit follows the event select without a reload and matches the server's own age rule (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §324).
12. Only an Administrator can erase a group run's declaration, and only with a reason. The audit row is written first, in the same transaction, and names the actor, the reason and the event, never the signer. The row and its outbox messages go with it (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §393).
13. Given a staff entry that creates or restarts a registration, when the desk's fast track confirms it, then it confirms the row the service reports it wrote, found by id and never by re-reading the address. The public form's answer carries no registration id and stays the same for everybody (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §420).
14. Given an address with active registrations at an event, when staff enter a person whose name (by the runner's key) differs from every active one — twins with the same birth date included — then a registration of their own is created under the same participant, within the club's limit per address; the same runner again is refused with the address box and `STAFF_ALREADY_ON_ADDRESS`, an address at the limit with `STAFF_ADDRESS_AT_CAP`, and nothing is written; a desk fast track confirms only the row the entry wrote (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §493). Verification: integration `registrations/staff-crud.test.ts`, `registrations/lifecycle-audit.test.ts`; unit `registrations/family.test.ts`.
15. §425's export criterion, extended: the CSV and the xlsx end with «Declaration version» and «Declaration signed» — the registration's latest declaration acceptance, blank while none exists — after the terms columns, and the xlsx writes every timestamp on the club's clock.

**Verification:** integration `registrations/staff-crud.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-037-06 — An Administrator erases a registration

- **Source:** BR-BUS-037, BR-BUS-070
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §15.11, §12.12, §10.3
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1
- **Status:** built, and recorded in `DECISIONS.md` §44, which supersedes the "there is no
  delete" of §33. Cancelling keeps the row, which is right for a withdrawal and wrong for an
  erasure request; this is the second case.

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a registration that holds a place, when an Administrator erases it, then the place is released through the ordinary allocator and offered to the front of the waiting list before the row is removed.
2. Given a registration with a signed declaration, when it is erased, then the declaration acceptance is deleted with it, and so are its action tokens and queued messages.
3. Given any erasure, when it completes, then an `audit_logs` row records the actor, the reason, the status it was in and the time — and contains neither the participant's name nor their address.
4. Given that audit row, when the registration no longer exists, then the row is still readable: it carries no foreign key to the thing it describes.
5. Given an erasure, when it completes, then no message is sent to the participant.
6. Given any role below Administrator, when an erasure is attempted, then it is refused and nothing is removed.
7. Given an unknown registration, when an erasure is attempted, then it is refused rather than reported as a silent success.
8. Given an erasure from the registrations list, when the Administrator types the row's name to confirm it, then the typed name is compared with that registration's name using the same fold as the declaration's signature (`domain/name-fold.ts`). Forgiven: case, runs of whitespace (a non-breaking space included), diacritics (any combining mark, with a comma below or a cedilla), the typographic shape of an apostrophe or a hyphen, and invisible characters (zero-width spaces, soft hyphens). Nothing else is forgiven: every letter and digit, every hyphen and apostrophe, and every word, in order. A blank name never matches, and a name that does not match erases nothing (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §314).
9. Given an erasure, when it completes, then no earlier audit row of that registration has a participant_id, a name correction has no before or after, and no row but the deletion's keeps a typed reason. When it was the person's last registration, no row about the person (participant.data_exported) keeps the participant's id in participant_id or entity_id. All of this happens in the delete's transaction.
10. Given the erase panel (single or batch) and the banner after a single, batch or whole-event erase, when they render, then they list the copies the erase cannot reach: the archive mailbox and its Cc/Bcc copies, downloaded exports, the paper declaration, Mailgun suppressions, and the database backups' restore window.
11. Given an erasure of a registration that holds a place, when it completes, then no cancellation message is queued, not even transiently.
12. Given the event erase page for an event with real registrations, when it renders, then the list of what the platform cannot delete stands before the typed-title confirmation, as on the other three erase panels. The list names the archive mailbox and its copies, downloaded exports and downloaded declarations PDFs, any printed emergency sheet, the paper declaration, Mailgun suppressions, and backups kept at most 7 days (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §324).
13. A spare worn by an erased registration counts as taken: it is never printed blank again or suggested to anyone else (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §444).
14. An erase of a registration whose declaration is held for a complaint or a dispute is refused with its own code, `DECLARATION_HELD` — alone from its page or from the list, in the batch, or with its event (the event's erase and the event's delete) — and every form says the declaration is kept, never the generic CONFLICT sentence; nothing is deleted. The hold is asked again under a `FOR UPDATE` lock inside the erase's transaction (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §556). Verification: integration `registrations/declaration-proof.test.ts`, `group-run-declarations/proof-and-hold.test.ts`.

**Verification:** integration `registrations/staff-crud.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-037-07 — The desk confirms: address vouched for, declaration on paper

- **Source:** BR-BUS-037, BR-BUS-033
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §15.11, §10.8, §12.6
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1 — 2026-09-18, `DECISIONS.md` §67, which amends §33's "consent cannot be
  relayed": it still cannot, and this is not a relay. The participant signs a printed copy of the
  approved declaration; staff record that they did.
- **Status:** built.

A race morning has people at a table whose email never arrived, whose QR is on a phone that died,
or who never registered at all. Every one of them is standing in front of a member of staff — the
one situation in which "verify the address" is answered by looking up. The desk therefore has a
way through every step, and none of them is a way around the allocator.

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a registration in `PENDING_EMAIL_CONFIRMATION`, `PENDING_DECLARATION` or `WAITLIST_OFFERED`, when a member of staff confirms it at the desk, then it goes through the ordinary allocator under the event's capacity lock: to `CONFIRMED` when a place is free, to `WAITLISTED` when the event is full, and the outcome is stated as a sentence. Nothing at the desk can place anybody past capacity.
2. Given the address was never clicked, when the desk confirms, then `registrations.email_confirmed_at` is set and `email_confirmed_by_staff_user_id` names who vouched for it; the participant's own `email_verified_at` is deliberately left unset, because an attestation is a different fact from a delivered click.
3. Given the declaration, when the desk confirms, then a `declaration_acceptances` row is written with `method = PAPER`, `attested_by_staff_user_id` set, the registered name as the typed name, and the current approved version's id and hash — the same row shape the email path writes, so every reader downstream treats it identically; the database refuses a `PAPER` row with no attester and an `EMAIL_LINK` row with one. Without an approved declaration in the participant's locale the confirmation is refused, exactly as the email path is.
4. Given a registration entered by staff with the fast track ticked, when it is created, then no verification email is queued, the registration is confirmed as in criteria 1–3 in the same request, and the public registration window is not consulted — the desk decides — while a cancelled event and a non-local registration mode still refuse it.
5. Given a waiting-list registration, when the desk gives it a place, then it is refused with a sentence while `occupied >= capacity` under the lock, and otherwise confirmed on paper as in criterion 3; the audit row says the queue was jumped and by whom.
6. Given any of the above, when it completes, then an `audit_logs` row names the actor and the transition and never the participant; and given a confirmation, then the `REGISTRATION_CONFIRMED` email is queued as for any confirmation.
7. Given every staff role, including Contributor, when a desk verb (enter, confirm, give a place, set a number, check in) is attempted, then it is allowed; and given any role below Administrator, when cancel, erase, rename, resend or the printing mark is attempted, then it is refused. The list and the export are the Organizer's to read since `DECISIONS.md` §289 and nobody else's below Administrator (BR-REQ-060-01).
8. Given a `PENDING_DECLARATION` registration whose hold deadline has passed and which was kept because the queue did not want its place (BR-REQ-033-01 criterion 3, `DECISIONS.md` §160), when the desk confirms it on paper, then it is confirmed as in criterion 3 — the place was never released, so no capacity check is needed and none is made; and given the same registration after the event's own start released the hold, when the desk confirms it, then the allocator decides again under the lock and confirms it into a place that is still free rather than refusing it. The backoffice journey shows "termen depășit, locul se ține cât nu așteaptă nimeni" for such a hold — the condition in the words, because the row is rendered in a list that spans many events and reads no queue of its own.
9. Given a minor's registration confirmed on paper at the desk, when the declaration in effect in the registration's language names `{{participantIdDocument}}`, then the acceptance records the parent or guardian as `typed_name` and the minor as `minor_typed_name`, and the desk row, the registration's page and the list's confirmation say before the press that the paper carries both signatures, each with its own document, and the desk row offers the minor's paper form; otherwise the acceptance records the parent or guardian alone and nothing is said about a second signature (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §330).
10. A staff verb's confirmation dialog states an email count that matches what the send actually queues for that row's kind — never a participant count for a TEST row, which the club is told nothing about elsewhere. (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §384.)
11. Given a desk entry whose duplicate check found the address empty, when a public submission on the same address lands before the entry takes the event's lock, then the entry is refused under the lock with "this address already has a registration for this event", rolled back whole, and confirms nobody: no re-send, no audit row in the staff member's name, and the other runner stays as the public form left them. Proven on two real connections in `tests/concurrency/desk-race.test.ts` (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §420).
12. Given an event with reserved spares, when the desk renders a walk-in (a real runner with no number, settled or provisional), then the next free spare is suggested beside «Confirmă aici», in the number given by hand and in the walk-in form's box. When every spare has been given, the box is empty and the row says so. Given an online runner holding a provisional number, then no spare is suggested and the confirmation adopts their own number (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §444).
13. Given a number handed at the desk with the paper or typed by hand, then it is a whole number from 1 to 99999, refused when another registration of the event wears or holds it (settled, provisional or erased), and checked again under the event lock. When the number is a desk spare it becomes the runner's final number and is marked printed at once. When a walk-in's handed spare went to somebody else in the meantime, the entry stands unconfirmed and the desk is told why (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §444).
14. A paper confirmation at the desk gets its number at that press, in the order of confirmation, or the spare the volunteer hands, checked again under the lock (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
15. Criteria 12 and 13 above describe the provisional numbers of §214/§220/§444 and are superseded by this decision: a number exists only once the registration is confirmed (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
16. «Dă-i un loc» refused because no place is free changes nothing and says so with the allocator's own counts under the event lock — the event's places, the confirmed, the declarations to sign, the open offers and a family's reserved places — in the backoffice's language, never «Verifică datele introduse»; the same press confirms the registration, with a number and the confirmation email, as soon as a place is free, including one a family's lapsed reservation released with no job run since (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §589).
17. On a full race «Dă-i un loc» stays visible on the registrations list, the registration's page and both desk pages. It carries a keyboard- and touch-reachable tooltip whose words are the refusal banner's own (`Admin.errors.NO_FREE_PLACE`) with the allocator's counts, read once per event per request. With a place free there is no tooltip (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §592).
18. The refusal of «Dă-i un loc» on a full race ends «Mărește întâi capacitatea evenimentului.» / "Raise the event's capacity first." The press never takes a place beyond capacity or one promised to somebody else (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §592).
19. «Trimite-i oferta» is drawn on every waiting registration (its page and each waiting row of «Coada de înscrieri») at any moment before the start and is the Administrator's. Under the event lock it refuses a cancelled or finished event, a started one and a registration that is not `WAITLISTED` (a second press for the same person included); it no longer refuses a closed registration — the offer's deadline is the club's offer window from the press, capped by the start alone, its email's send re-bases it with the start as its one cap, and the person accepts it by signing — while the automatic offers keep the close as their cap and make none after it. When no counted place is free on a capped event (a lapsed declaration hold the line wants is released first) and the press carries the capacity its dialog named (`addPlace`, exactly the locked capacity + 1), it raises that one event's capacity by exactly one (a series' other dates keep theirs) and writes `event.capacity_raised_for_offer` with the actor, from, to and the registration, shown on that registration's page (read through its event's index, never another event's rows); the chosen person's offer occupies the new place before the queue is filled, so on «Da» the first in line does not take it; should the chosen person not take it, the added place stays in the capacity, as any freed place. Without that confirmation — a page read while a place was free, or a capacity named before another raise — the press is refused with `SUPPLEMENTARY_PLACE_UNCONFIRMED`, said in both languages, and nothing is written; a confirmed raise no longer needed adds nothing. An uncapped event is never raised. Its dialog says beforehand «Nu e niciun loc liber: apăsând, adaugi un loc suplimentar peste limită, capacitatea devine {n}.» with the confirm button «Adaugă un loc și trimite oferta», and after the close «Înscrierile s-au închis: oferta e valabilă cel mult până la start.», each under 200 characters in both languages (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §642).
20. The backoffice journey's words for a declaration hold kept past its deadline are «termen depășit, locul se ține cât nu-l cere nimeni» / “deadline passed, the place is kept while nobody asks for it”, in place of criterion 8's «cât nu așteaptă nimeni»: since §348 a newcomer the waiting list has no room for can want the place too (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §635).
21. with no counted free place, «Dă-i un loc acum» is no longer refused when its question said so and the press carries the capacity it named (`addPlace`; otherwise `SUPPLEMENTARY_PLACE_UNCONFIRMED`, nothing written): it raises that one event's capacity by exactly one under the same lock and in the same transaction, writes `event.capacity_raised_for_place_now` (actor, from, to, registration), and the row takes the new place at once — never the waiting list's; a family's live reservation still adds nothing. Its question says beforehand «Nu e niciun loc liber: apăsând, adaugi un loc suplimentar peste limită, capacitatea devine {n}.» and its confirm button reads «Adaugă un loc și dă-i locul» (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §642).
22. Given a registration the desk's paper confirmation is offered on, when the staff member opens its page, the desk or the registrations list's row menu, then the registration page's «Ziua cursei» box carries an «i» beside its title and a caption, the desk carries one «i» beside the line that says what the row buttons do (never one per row), the list's «Confirmă pe hârtie» item carries the same text as its tooltip, the confirm window says the confirmation email follows, or, if there is no place, the waiting list with no email now, and the four lines say, in Romanian and English: that it is for the race-day desk with the declaration the participant signed on paper in front of staff, recorded on the staff member's name (the address too only if it was not confirmed); that a person without a place yet gets it through the allocator (the waiting list when full, a full list refuses); that if they get the place they receive the confirmation email with the QR code, the number and the declaration's copy and no declaration-to-sign email, and on the waiting list no email is queued by the press, the offer email comes when they are given a place and the declaration stays unrecorded, an unconfirmed address, and only that, being on the staff member's name; and that it is not a shortcut around the email confirmation before the race. No button, dialog, verb or allocator rule changed (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §640).
23. «Dă-i un loc acum» on a registration on «Lista de invitați speciali» gives it its place whatever the counts — it needs no counted free place, so a full event does not refuse it and no lapsed declaration hold goes for it — while a counted row on the same full event is still refused with §589's sentence (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
24. Choosing «Invitat special — nu ocupă un loc» for a waiting row or an open offer opens the «Pui înscrierea pe lista de invitați speciali?» dialog, which names the declaration's email «{message}» and the bold email line for a real row, and for a TEST row neither — its body names the declaration to sign and no email (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
25. Only an Administrator sends, resends or withdraws invitations — asserted in the action, the admin service and the service; a send refuses, by the person's name and writing nothing, an address already registered at the event, one already invited, or a line twice; no invitation creates a registration: the invited person registers and signs themselves from the link (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §647).
26. An invitation may be sent while the event is scheduled, published and not started, after the public close included, its deadline capped by the start; a typed address the club already knows is written to in its participant's language and its stored name and language are never changed, an address never seen in the language the Administrator chose; a member's invitation registers a club member whatever the form posts; and the registration's page says who sent the invitation it came from and when, for every staff role that reads it (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §647).
27. A refused send of invitations hands the island the server's current capacity and places free for invitations, so the next press's dialog names the raise the server will accept without a reload; «Limba invitației» and «Zile până expiră» keep the Administrator's choice after a refusal, and the confirmation dialog names both before anything is sent (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §647).
28. «Retrimite» with days typed never moves the deadline of an invitation that holds a counted place while anyone eligible waits: under the event lock it re-issues the link and the email and keeps the deadline, the dialog says so before the press, the result says the deadline was kept and the audit row records it; an invitation «Pe lista de invitați speciali», and one with nobody waiting, may still be moved later (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §647, §649).
29. A send of invitations «Pe lista de invitați speciali» to an event whose «Folosește lista de invitați speciali» is off is refused under the event lock with `HIDDEN_LIST_OFF` — the registration page's own sentence — before any invitation is written or any email queued, and the invitation form draws the tick only while the switch is on; the same send goes through once the switch is on (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §647, §649).
30. «Lista de invitați speciali» on a registration's page is two radios, «Se numără între locurile evenimentului» and «Invitat special — nu ocupă un loc», the stored state checked, under a heading with the incognito glyph and, beside it, a 44-pixel «i» whose three lines say who the list is for, what the person keeps and that an ordinary participant does not go there, a one-line caption under the radios, drawn while the event's «Folosește lista de invitați speciali» is on or for a row already on the list — then with the line «Lista de invitați speciali e oprită la acest eveniment: înscrierea poate doar ieși de pe ea.»; the Administrator's form posts the hidden `outside` value opposite to the stored state, a change opens the existing per-state dialog, «Da» posts it to `setOutsideCapacityAction`, «Anulează» sends nothing and shows the stored state again, and the Organizer sees both radios disabled, with no form (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
31. The registration page's «Lista de invitați speciali» block explains itself to every role that reads it: the heading's «i» (`InfoTip`, a 44-pixel button beside the heading, outside the radio group's name) holds three lines — «Pentru organizatori, voluntari, pacemakeri și sportivi invitați: sunt la start fără să ocupe unul dintre locurile anunțate.», what they keep and when the public count includes them, and «Un participant obișnuit nu se pune aici.» — and the caption under the radios says in one line that the person takes no announced place, keeps the declaration and the race number, stays on the public list if they ticked it and enters the public count only with the event's tick, each under 200 characters, in both languages (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §649).

**Verification:** integration `registrations/race-day.test.ts`, `registrations/staff-crud.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-037-08 — Race day: the desk code, its QR, check-in

- **Source:** BR-BUS-037, BR-BUS-036
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §15.11, §16.3, §12.6, §8
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1 — 2026-09-18, `DECISIONS.md` §67.
- **Status:** built.

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a registration reaching `CONFIRMED` by any path, when it is confirmed, then `registrations.checkin_code` is set: ten characters from an alphabet without 0/O/1/I, unique across every event, stored in clear — an identifier that confers nothing, not a credential; a confirmed registration from before codes existed receives one the first time it is needed.
2. Given the `REGISTRATION_CONFIRMED` message, when it renders, then it carries the code as text in both bodies and, in the HTML body, a hosted PNG of the QR at `/api/registrations/qr/<code>.png` — never a data URI. The QR encodes the address of `/admin/checkin/<code>`, so a phone's camera opens the desk page for that runner. The route answers 404 for a code nobody has and for anything that is not a code; no other message type carries a code.
3. Given `/admin/checkin` and `/admin/checkin/<code>`, when requested with any staff session, then they render; when requested without one, then sign-in — a participant scanning their own code sees the sign-in page and nothing else. The desk shows for each runner the registered name, the state, the number and the check-in state, and never an email address or any other detail.
4. Given the desk, when a code is scanned (the browser's own `BarcodeDetector`, where it exists) or typed, then the one registration it names is shown from any event; when a name fragment is typed, or nothing, then the chosen event's registrations that are not cancelled or expired are listed, pending ones included, matched with the same diacritics-blind search as the list; when a race number is typed, then the registrations of the chosen event wearing it as a settled number are listed in any state — a settled number is never reused, so a cancelled or expired runner is found by it — and a provisional number finds only a registration that is not cancelled or expired; and the event's counts — confirmed, checked in, without a number, not yet confirmed — are on the page (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §311).
5. Given a confirmed registration, when staff mark it here, then `checked_in_at` and `checked_in_by_staff_user_id` are set, idempotently; undoing clears both; anything not confirmed is refused; each writes an audit row. Given the participant's own manage link from twenty-four hours before the start, when they press "I am here", then `checked_in_at` is set with no staff id, the token is read and not spent, and the same page shows their code and QR.
6. Given a walk-in on the desk, when "add somebody" is used, then the staff entry form opens with the fast track ticked and returns to the desk with the outcome (BR-REQ-037-07 criterion 4).
7. Given the whole of race day, when a volunteer opens the desk, then the steps — before, at the table, after, and what to do when each thing goes wrong — are on the page itself, and the guide at `/admin/guide` says the same for every role.
8. Given a scanned code or a typed settled race number that names a `CANCELLED` or `EXPIRED` registration, when the desk renders the row, then it says so first, in the error colour — the state and the date it left the live states — and, when a printed bib with that number exists, that the number was printed, is not to be handed out and is never reused; the row offers no verb, no number field and no bib picture, and every staff role sees it as a name, a state and a number and never an address. The desk's own how-to names the red box and what to do (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §311).
9. "Am ajuns" opens the club's check-in lead before the start, in the event's zone, and the manage page says from when, starting with "începând cu … înainte de start" (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §377).
10. Race week, for both the homepage countdown and the editor's bib-printing card, counts whole calendar days on the event's wall clock with the start still ahead. 0 means race day only, and the two always agree (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §377).
11. Check-in at the desk, in the list menu and on the registration page asks nothing: it emails nobody and is undone from the same row.
12. A print of 1 to 50 spares reserves the numbers after the highest number any registration holds (settled, provisional or erased) on a first print — below the hidden list's first number when its own series sits above the race's — and the next free numbers after the reservation on a second, neither reaching the hidden list's first number. A number an extension steps over stays its holder's and is never a spare. The reservation is at most 500 numbers and within five digits (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648).
13. No automatic draw (provisional, final or at close) ever hands out a reserved spare number. A spare reaches a runner only when a staff member hands it at the desk (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §444).
14. The desk's «Confirmă aici» offers the next free spare only to a walk-in: a real registration entered by staff (or holding no number at all) with no settled number and no printed bib. A registration made online keeps its provisional number, and the confirmation adopts it (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §444).
15. `confirmRegistrationByStaff` refuses a handed number (VALIDATION_ERROR on `bibNumber`, nothing written) for a registration that is not such a walk-in. It refuses a number someone else holds, settled or provisional, with CONFLICT on `bibNumber`. It checks both again under the event lock (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §444).
16. A spare handed at the desk, or typed by hand, is marked printed, so the unprinted sheet never prints it a second time and a cancellation lists it among the existing bibs. When every spare is given, the desk says so in words (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §444).
17. A walk-in whose handed spare was taken between the desk's check and the confirmation stays entered and unconfirmed, and the desk is told to confirm them with another spare. A walk-in the allocator puts on the waiting list takes no number, and the spare stays free (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §444).
18. Beside every QR — the confirmed email's caption, «Gestionează înscrierea» and «Înscrierile mele» — the person's name and race number are written, «—» while no number is given and never a word that calls it provisional; the club's copy, which carries no QR, carries the same name-and-number line in its place (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §547).
19. Each person of a family on one address gets their own number at their own confirmation, shown beside their own QR; a person still at the declaration shows none (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
20. Criteria 4, 12, 13, 14 and 15 above describe the provisional numbers of §214/§220/§444 and are superseded by this decision: a number exists only once the registration is confirmed (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
21. Given a race number typed at the desk, with a paper confirmation or on its own, when it is saved, then only digits count: «12.7», «1e3», «0x10» or «-5» is refused naming the box, never rounded and never read as another number, and «007» is 7 (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §553).

**Verification:** integration `registrations/race-day.test.ts`; unit `registrations/checkin-code.test.ts`, `notifications/templates.test.ts`; e2e `race-day.spec.ts`; integration `registrations/void-bibs.test.ts` (4, 8); e2e `race-day.spec.ts` (8)

#### BR-REQ-071-01 — Participant export

- **Source:** BR-BUS-071, BR-BUS-070
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §15.10
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M2

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given an Author or Editor, when an export is attempted, then it is refused.
2. Given an administrator, when an export is produced, then it contains only the fields needed for the stated organizer purpose.
3. Given a cell value beginning with `=`, `+`, `-`, or `@`, when it is written to CSV, then it is neutralized.
4. Given an export, when it completes, then it is delivered through a short-lived authorized response, is not written to public storage, and is audited.
5. Given any registrations export (CSV or spreadsheet), when it is produced, then an audit row records who exported, which event, the format and the row count, never a row. Given the Administrator's JSON of everything held about an address, when it is downloaded, then it is recorded as participant.data_exported with counts only.
6. Given registrations that said yes to offers and benefits, when «Descarcă lista pentru sponsori» is downloaded for one event (`GET /api/admin/registrations/sponsor-list?event=<id>`) or for every event (no `event`), then both come from the one query and the one CSV — Prenume, Nume, Email, Eveniment, Data acordului, a BOM, CRLF, every cell neutralized, named `sponsori-<slug>-<day>.csv` or `sponsori-toate-<day>.csv` — holding only real registrations whose address is proved and which still stand, of participants who are 18 or older on the day of the download, never a no, a cancelled, an expired, an unproved-address, a test registration, a minor or a registration without a birth date, and never the birth date, the phone, the health note or the declaration's facts; the count beside the button is the file's row count (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §570).
7. Given a download of the sponsor list, when it is made with or without «Cui dai lista», then it writes one audit row `registrations.sponsor_list_exported` with the event (or null), the count, the ids of the registrations in the file and the recipient (trimmed, control characters dropped, at most 120 characters, or null) — never a name or an address — and the page of each registration in the file lists it under «Dată partenerilor» with its moment, its recipient (or «destinatar nenotat») and the staff member who downloaded it, while a registration in no file lists nothing (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §570).
8. Given registrations on the sponsor list, when «Descarcă lista pentru sponsori (Excel)» is downloaded (`GET /api/admin/registrations/sponsor-list?format=xlsx`, for one event or every event), then it holds exactly the rows of the CSV of the same moment — a yes given under a notice that describes the sharing, of an adult, on a real registration that still stands, never a yes under an older notice, a minor, a no, a cancelled, an expired or a test registration — in one sheet «Sponsori» named `sponsori-<slug>-<day>.xlsx` or `sponsori-toate-<day>.xlsx`, with the CSV's five columns first and then the notice the yes was given under, the public list & results and the socials (yes or no), the terms' and the declaration's versions and moments and the registration's state, headed in the reader's language, a blank version or moment left unwritten, and never the birth date, the phone, the identity document or the health note (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §581).
9. Given a download of the sponsor list in either format, when it is served, then it writes one audit row `registrations.sponsor_list_exported` naming its `format` (`csv` or `xlsx`) beside the event, the count, the recipient and the registrations' ids, and a registration in it lists that download under «Dată partenerilor» (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §581).
10. Given the registrations list with «Doar cu oferte și beneficii» ticked (`promo=1`), when it renders and when «Exportă Excel» or «Exportă CSV» beside it is followed, then the list, its count and both files hold exactly the registrations `promoListed` names — a yes to offers and benefits on a real registration waiting for its declaration, on the waiting list, offered a place or confirmed — and no other; and the full export carries «Public list & results» (the public-list tick) right after «Socials on the public list» in the CSV and the spreadsheet (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §581).

**Verification:** integration `backoffice/export.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-038-01 — Race numbers, assigned as a batch and printed as a sheet

- **Source:** BR-BUS-037
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §12.6, §9.2
- **Priority:** SHOULD
- **Release:** M1 — pulled forward from M2 on 2026-09-17 (`DECISIONS.md` §65); the rest of M2's
  bib story (one number per race across its distances, results keyed by bib) stays M2.
- **Status:** built.

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a registration of a real kind, when it is confirmed — by the participant's signature or at the desk — then it receives a number drawn at random from those never worn at that event (three digits while they last, four after), inside the transaction that holds the event row; and given an Administrator on an event that takes registrations here, when they assign race numbers, then every confirmed, real registration without one receives such a number, in order of confirmation, and the outcome states how many were assigned and how many the event has (2026-09-18, `DECISIONS.md` §87, §94).
2. Given a registration that already has a number, when numbers are assigned again, then its number is unchanged; a number once worn at the event — by any registration, cancelled included — is never drawn again.
3. Given a test registration, a waitlisted, pending, cancelled or expired one, when numbers are assigned, then it receives none; a cancelled registration that had a number keeps it, so the number is never given to somebody else.
4. Given two registrations of one event, when both would carry the same number, then the database refuses the second.
5. Given assigned numbers, when the sheet is requested — all of them, or a range `from`/`to` for a reprint or a late batch — then the response is a PDF, Administrator only, of A4 pages with two bibs each and a dashed cut line between them, or, with `layout=one` ("câte unul pe pagină"), the same A5-sized bib centred one per A4 page (2026-09-18, `DECISIONS.md` §79); each bib carries the club's lockup, the number in the club's blue as large as the paper allows, the participant's registered name, and the event's title and date in the requested language. Only confirmed, real registrations are printed.
6. Given the assignment, when it completes with at least one number given, then one audit row records who, for which event, and the range assigned — never a participant.
7. Given one confirmed registration, when any staff role types a number for it — or clears it — then the number is a whole number from 1 to 99999, a number already worn at that event is refused with a sentence rather than a stack trace, and an audit row records the number before and after (2026-09-18, `DECISIONS.md` §67); the registration's page lists the first free numbers beside the field, and a number given or changed by hand on a confirmed registration queues `BIB_ASSIGNED` to the participant — the number, the QR code and the manage link — while clearing one sends nothing (`DECISIONS.md` §105).
8. Given a registration for which Mailgun reported `permanent_fail` or `complained` on any message (BR-REQ-080-04), when the desk row or the registration page renders, then it carries an "email respins" chip with the provider's short reason, so the organizer knows before race day who never got the email and calls them; the participant sees nothing (2026-09-18, `DECISIONS.md` §76). The registrations list filters to those rows ("doar email respins"), shows the same chip, and its CSV export carries `Checked in` and `Email bounced` columns; the events list shows confirmed and checked-in counts beside an event within a day of its start (`DECISIONS.md` §83).
9. Given numbered registrations, when an Administrator opens the event page, then each confirmed, real registration's bib can be seen as a picture — the club's lockup, the event's title and date, the number in the club's blue, the registered name — drawn on request at `/api/admin/events/<id>/bibs/preview?registration=<id>`, Administrator only (2026-09-18, `DECISIONS.md` §94).
10. Given an event, when a registration is confirmed or the batch runs, then it is given the lowest free race number in its own series — the race's, from the event's own `bib_start_number` (1 by default), or, for a row on «Lista de invitați speciali» of an event whose switch is on and whose hidden list has its own series (`hidden_list_bib_start`), the hidden list's, from that start; the race's series ends at the number before the hidden start when that sits above it, and the hidden list's at the number before `bib_start_number` when it sits below it — in order, never at random, the desk's spares and retired numbers skipped; a series with no free number left takes the next free number after it, in the other series if need be, never a duplicate; under the same event-row lock capacity uses; a number once given is never renumbered, stays when the row changes list, and a cancelled registration keeps it (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
11. Given a confirmed registration that already has a race number, when a staff member tries to change or clear it, then it is refused: the runner has been emailed it and it may already be printed. Given a confirmed registration with no number, then one may still be given by hand and the runner is told. Given a registration that is not yet confirmed, then a preferential number may be set among the free ones, and no email goes out until confirmation carries it (2026-09-20, `DECISIONS.md` §173, §105).
12. Given an event, when an organizer sets where its numbers start and what colour they print, then both are stored on the event and checked at the database — a start a four-digit bib can reach, and a colour that is six hex digits after a hash — and the free-number suggestions count from that start rather than from 1 (2026-09-20, `DECISIONS.md` §173).
13. Given an event's page, when an organizer opens "cum arată numărul de concurs" and saves, then the event stores what is printed (the runner's name, the event's title, the date, the club's logo), the number's size, where the name sits, a header picture, a sponsors' strip and whether the sheet carries cut marks; the A4 sheet and the preview picture both render exactly that, and a form that does not carry the panel — the create form, an older caller — leaves the stored design untouched (2026-09-21, `DECISIONS.md` §249).
14. Given a header or sponsors' picture, when it is chosen, then it must be one this site stored — its own WebP variant, on the store's public host or the local media route — and anything else is refused or dropped; when the sheet prints, each picture is fetched with a five-second deadline and a four-megabyte ceiling, and one that cannot be fetched prints the coloured band rather than failing the sheet. The band's own text is white or ink, whichever can be read on the chosen colour (2026-09-21, `DECISIONS.md` §249).
15. Given the event editor's bib-design panel, when it renders, then it shows a picture of the bib drawn by the same renderer that prints — a placeholder runner, the event's start number, its real title and date, the unsaved design read from the picture's own address through the schema the save uses — and redraws it about 300 ms after any design box, the band colour or the start number changes, before a save; the sample is a staff-only GET that reads no participant row, mutates nothing and is never cached; a garbage design value falls back field by field and a third party's picture is refused (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §301).
16. Given a real registration with a settled, printed race number, when it is cancelled — by an Administrator, in bulk, or from the participant's own link — or expires, then the number stays retired and the printed mark stays on the row; `voidBibsFor(eventId)` lists it — number, registered name, status and the row's own `cancelled_at` or `expired_at` — sorted by number, and lists no unprinted, provisional, test or confirmed row; the registrations list's bibs panel shows one visible line per such bib, the whole line a link to its registration naming the number, the name, the state in the page's language and the date, and its "printed / total" figures count confirmed registrations only and say so; the sheet prints none of them, in the batch or in a range (1–50 around a cancelled printed 27 renders 49); the printing mark is offered only on a confirmed row; the registration's page shows a "BID N tipărit — de retras" chip and the timeline's cancelled or expired line says it; a staff cancellation's audit row carries `bibNumber` and `bibPrinted: true`, and an erasure's carries the event, the number and the printed fact — never the name; the single cancel's confirmation names the printed number before the press, the bulk cancel names the printed numbers among the rows it shows before the press, and its saved banner names the printed numbers that press retired (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §311).
17. Given a registration that is `CANCELLED` or `EXPIRED`, when any staff role — through the desk action or otherwise — tries to clear its number, replace it or give it one, then the server refuses it and nothing is written; given a registration whose settled number is printed, in any status, then a change or a clear is refused the same way. Given a registration that was erased while wearing a settled number, printed or not, then that number stays taken at its event for as long as the erasure's audit row is kept: the confirmation's draw, the batch, the provisional draw, the settle at the close and the free-number suggestions never produce it, and typing it by hand is refused with nothing written (an erased printed 27 in a band from 1: thirty draws give 1–26 and 28–31) (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §311).
18. Given the registrations list, when the race-number column renders, then its header carries a tap-friendly hint saying that a number with an asterisk is provisional (it can change until registration closes and is emailed and printed only once settled), that a bold number is final and that a tick means printed; the asterisk is hidden from a screen reader and followed by the word "provizoriu" (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §313).
19. Given an event's bib design, when an organizer switches the footer's pieces — the club's mailbox, the partners, the site's bare host from `APP_BASE_URL`, the event's title and date — and types a line of the club's own, then the A4 sheet and the preview picture print them in that order: event, partners, the club's line, site, mailbox. The club's line is stored as plain text on one line, normalised to composed characters, with only characters the bib's font can draw, at most 120. The event's title or date is printed only where the header actually drawn does not show it; a sheet whose header picture could not be fetched prints the band and does not repeat them. The platform's own design, and any design stored before these keys existed, prints exactly the partners, then the mailbox. The footer takes one line, or two with whole pieces moved to the second, the second cut with "…" when even two are not enough. It never takes a third line and is never shrunk. Both renderers break it at the same characters. No footer line is wider on the paper than its 503.28-point box by pdfkit's own measurement, kerning included, and the sheet gives pdfkit no width to wrap in. Nothing about a participant is ever printed in it (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §317).
20. Given assigned numbers, when the sheet is requested — all of them, or a range from/to for a reprint or a late batch — then the response is a PDF, Administrator only, of A4 pages with two bibs each and a dashed cut line between them, or, with layout=one ("câte unul pe pagină"), the same A5-sized bib centred one per A4 page (2026-09-18, DECISIONS.md §79); each bib carries the club's lockup, the number in the club's blue as large as the paper allows, the participant's registered name, and the event's title and date in the requested language. Only confirmed, real registrations are printed. (Criterion 5 — this fix restores compliance: the branch had drifted `layout=one` to an upper-half, cut-lined bib before this pass.)
21. Criterion 5 (addition): with `layout=one` the A5 bib stays centred on its A4 page with no dashed cut line and no frame; when the design's cut marks are on, the page carries the trim guide — short solid rules at each end of the bib's top and foot — and with them off it carries no line at all (§79, §340). Verification: unit `registrations/bibs-pdf.test.ts`.
22. Given a lapsed declaration hold released by the sweep, then its provisional race number is released like every other sweep's (and migration `0080` clears the rows left set). A desk confirmation after the numbers settle then gets a free final number, never a duplicate-key error (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §420).
23. Given registration closes, when the numbers settle, then only confirmed, held and offered places get a final number and BIB_ASSIGNED. An unconfirmed address gives its provisional number back and gets none (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §420).
24. Given a waiting-list offer, from the queue or the desk's promotion, then it draws a provisional number under the event's lock like every other place. After the close, the desk's promotion adopts that number as the final one (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §420).
25. Given an event that takes registrations here, when an Administrator types how many spares (1 to 50) and presses «Tipărește», then, asked first with the first number named, the event's reservation of desk spares is made under the event-row lock. The first print takes the numbers right after the highest number anybody has, settled, provisional or erased — when the hidden list's own series sits above the race's, the highest below the hidden list's first number. A later print extends the reservation after its end, stepping over any number somebody holds. Neither reaches the hidden list's first number: a print with no room left before it is refused with `SPARES_BEFORE_HIDDEN_LIST`, naming «Numerele invitaților speciali încep de la». A start that moved since the question is refused and nothing is written. The reservation is stored as a start and a count, both or neither, at most 500 numbers and within five digits (checked at the database), and audited with the range, never a name (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
26. Given an event with reserved desk spares, when a number is drawn automatically, then it is never a reserved number. This covers the provisional number at submission, the recompaction at the close, a confirmation after it, the batch and the free-number suggestions (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §444).
27. Given a print whose extension reached numbers online runners already held, then each such number stays its runner's for good. The close keeps a provisional number inside the reservation as that runner's final number and never moves them out of it. The print records the numbers it stepped over, and none of them is ever counted, suggested or printed as a spare, even after it is released. No number is worn twice (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §444).
28. Given reserved spares, when the sheet is requested with `spares=1`, optionally within `from`/`to`, then it prints the free spares only, each bib with an empty name line and «înscris la fața locului» / "on-the-spot entry" under it, on the sheet and in the preview picture. Given the redirect after a print carries a numeric `from`/`to`, then the event page shows a banner naming that range with a link to its blank sheet; a range that is not two numbers of one to five digits, lowest first, shows no banner (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §444).
29. A registration has no race number at the form, at the address's confirmation, with a declaration hold or with a waiting-list offer; the number is drawn when the registration is confirmed, under the event row's lock (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
30. Three registrations confirmed in a different order than they were sent are numbered in the order of confirmation, from the first number of their own series — the event's own first number, or, for rows on «Lista de invitați speciali» of an event with the hidden list's own series, that series' first number (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
31. A cancelled confirmed registration keeps its number retired, and the next confirmation takes the next number; a test registration gets none at its confirmation (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
32. Every surface shows a number only for a confirmed registration (a cancelled or expired one keeps its retired number): «—» in the backoffice list and page before it, an empty cell in the CSV and the Excel export (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
33. No message prints a number for a registration that is not confirmed, whatever the row holds, and the confirmed email carries the number; no «provizoriu» / «provisional» is left for numbers in either catalogue (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
34. The first maintenance run after the release keeps an old held number on a confirmed registration as its race number and emails it once if the race is ahead, clears every other held number, never keeps a number another row wears, and changes nothing on a second run (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
35. A preferential number is typed on a confirmed registration only, and replacing a number the registration already wears is the Administrator's — any other staff role is refused with FORBIDDEN and nothing written, while any desk role may still give a number to a confirmed registration that has none; the number it replaces is retired and never drawn again (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
36. A confirmed registration's number is never cleared; a clear is refused with nothing written (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
37. Criteria 11, 18, 22, 23, 24, 26 and 27 above, and criterion 7's clause that any staff role may type a confirmed registration's number or clear it and that clearing one sends nothing, describe the provisional numbers of §214/§220/§444, §173's lock on a confirmed number or a clear this decision refuses, and are superseded by it: a number exists only once the registration is confirmed, only an Administrator replaces one, and nobody clears it (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §548).
38. Given the desk's single-bib picture, when it is asked for a registration that is not a confirmed real one — restarted with a number, unconfirmed, waiting, cancelled or a test — then it answers 404 and draws nothing (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §553).
39. The bib designer's «Imagine în locul benzii colorate» and «Bandă cu sponsori» each take an upload, with the quality choice, or a picture «Din galerie», then a crop held to the place's one shape: the card's width by the band's height (about 9 : 1) and the band's width by its height (about 22 : 1). The crop is saved with the event's bib design on «Salvează»; with none, the header covers its strip from the middle and the sponsors' band fits the whole picture (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §560).
40. The sheet, the picture of one bib and the editor's preview draw a picture's crop through one rule, so the same part of the picture fills the place on all three; the preview redraws as soon as a picture or its crop changes, before any save (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §560).
41. The bib's design, its pictures and crops included, and the band's colour go with the race: to every date a series makes, by a series edit's scope, and to a duplicate (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §560).
42. Given the backoffice in either language, when it names the race number's printing mark, the desk guide's numbers or the start list's toggle, then it spells the term «BIB» («BIB-ul», «BIB-uri», «BIB-urile» in Romanian) and never «BID»; the chip of criterion 16 therefore reads «BIB N tipărit — de retras», superseding its spelling «BID N tipărit — de retras»; the short forms «nr.» and «no.» are unchanged (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §630).
43. With the hidden list's own series set (`events.hidden_list_bib_start`) and the switch on, a confirmed row on the hidden list draws the lowest free number from that start and a counted row from `bib_start_number` up to the number before it, each in confirmation order, at the confirmation and in «Alocă numerele»; a number once given stays when the row changes list; without a series, or with the switch off, every row draws from the race's series; a series with no free number left takes the next free number after it — in the other series if need be, never a number already given — rather than refusing the confirmation, and the box's help asks for room above the race's series while its «?» says the room is for supplementary places and the desk's spares and states this fallback (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648).
44. The event save refuses a hidden-list start inside the race's capped series (`bib_start_number` to `bib_start_number + capacity − 1`), or at or above the race's first number on an uncapped event, naming «Numerele invitaților speciali încep de la», and one whose series (from the start to the race's first number less one when it sits below it, otherwise to the ceiling) would hold any of the desk's reserved spares — a start below spares that sit above the race as well as one inside them — naming the same box (`fields: ["event.hiddenListBibStart"]`), the spares read from the event row locked inside the save's transaction (on an uncapped event too); it judges neither while the switch is off, and judges only a save that moves the hidden start, switches «Folosește lista de invitați speciali» on, or moves the race's first number or its places, and every new event — a save posting all of them as stored passes, so a place «Trimite-i oferta» added (§642) that carries the race's series up to a hidden start set just above it (race 1–150, hidden 151, capacity now 151) never refuses the event's next ordinary save, while a save moving the places to 152 is refused; a scoped series save judges every other date it reaches the same way, against that date's own row — its places and its spares, read from that date's row locked inside the save's transaction — and a refusal names the date and writes nothing on any date; a duplicate and «Repetă evenimentul» judge the band on the source's values and refuse before writing anything or storing the rule, while the maintenance job skips such a source and goes on with the others (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
45. A refused hidden-list start reaches the Administrator as its own sentence, never «Verifică datele introduse»: `Admin.errors.HIDDEN_LIST_IN_RACE_SERIES` (inside the race's series, or its places have reached it) or `HIDDEN_LIST_ON_SPARES` (through the desk's spares), each naming «Numerele invitaților speciali încep de la» / «The special guests' numbers start at» — on the editor's save, with the box linked, on «Repetă evenimentul», with no box linked, and on the events list after a refused duplicate; a scoped series save refused on another date's places or spares answers `HIDDEN_LIST_IN_RACE_SERIES_DATED` or `HIDDEN_LIST_ON_SPARES_DATED` with `{date}` that date in the reader's language, while a copy's sentence names no date (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
46. A standing series whose source the hidden list refuses makes none of its dates while every other series still gets its own; `materializeStandingRepeats` returns that source's id among `refused`, the maintenance run logs it and adds one to its `errorCount` for each such source on every run until the start is moved, and any other refusal of a series still fails the step (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648).
47. With the hidden list's series above the race's, the desk's spares stay below it: the first print reserves after the highest number below the hidden start — invitations holding 900–902 never push the spares to 903 — no print or extension reaches the hidden start, both series' draws skip the spares, and a print with no room left before it is refused with `SPARES_BEFORE_HIDDEN_LIST`, whose sentence names «Numerele invitaților speciali încep de la»; a hidden series below the race's bounds nothing (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §648, §649).
48. Given the registrations list, when an Administrator ticks «Doar cine nu a primit emailul» (`bounced=1`) — the tick that replaces criterion 8's «doar email respins» option — then the list keeps the registrations with a bounced or complained message, a club copy excepted, and every list row of such a registration, filtered or not, carries the same «Email respins» chip the desk row and the registration's page draw, the provider's short reason as its hover text (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §650).
49. Replaces criterion 48's hover text. Given a registration whose newest email (a club copy excepted) was bounced or marked as spam, when the registrations list, the registration's page or the desk row renders, then «Email respins» opens on hover, keyboard focus and a tap a tooltip that names the email by its «Emailuri» name, the instant it left in club time («pus în coadă» rather than «trimis» when the provider refused it at the send), why in plain words (the address does not exist or the mailbox is full; or marked as spam), whether the address had been confirmed before it, after it or never, and that the person is to be phoned — the provider's reason in small print, never a `title` attribute, and a screen reader hears the words once, as the chip's name, never again as its description; the registration's page says the same under the address, and the desk shows no address (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §663).
50. Given an event whose bib design has «Membrii primesc un număr cu design propriu» on, when the sheet or the preview draws a confirmed real registration that asked «Vreau numărul de membru» and whose canonical address is a live account on «Echipa», then it draws the members' header (their picture, else their colour, else the event's band) and the label «Membru {club}» or the club's own, at most 24 characters, at the geometry both renderers share; the number, its band and its order of confirmation, the name and the small print are those of the ordinary bib; a declared-only row, a row that did not ask, a spare and any row while the switch is off print the ordinary bib, and a TEST row stays off the sheet (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §664).
51. A bib design stored before the members' key existed, or with a malformed `member` object, reads as the members' bib off with no colour, no picture and the default label, and prints exactly as before; a members' colour that is not a hex triplet reads as none, a crop without its picture is dropped, and the label is normalised like the footer's line and cut at 24 characters (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §664).
52. Given the event's members' switch on, the bibs page shows a sample of the members' bib and, when confirmed real registrations with a number asked for it with an address that is no member account's, one line with their count and a link to the registrations list filtered to the same rows (`memberBib=asked`), whose list, count and summary all answer with that filter on; the export carries «Member bib» = yes / asked / empty (2026-10-04, `DECISIONS.md` §664).
53. Replaces criteria 48 and 49's «newest email». Given a registration, then «Email respins», the page's line under the address, the filter «Doar cu un email respins» and its count, and the export's `Email bounced` read one email state chosen from the participant's own messages only — never the club's archive copy, its confirmation notice or a club copy: the address refuses the club's mail (the registration's own refusal, or a family member's at the same event), the club's account refused it, the address works again but this email is owed, or it was sent again — in that order, and within each the registration's own refusal before a family member's, then the newest; they speak only for the first three, which ask somebody to act, and say nothing of an email sent again; the state ends when the same email, or one that carries it, is delivered, or for the account's refusal when such an email leaves (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §670).
54. Given the desk, the registrations list or the export, then none carries the provider's words or an address; only the registration's page reads the code and the redacted words, in small print (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §670).
55. Given the desk, then «Email respins» says which email did not arrive, why and — for an email owed or sent again — since when, and asks every staff role at the desk to ask an Administrator to press the button that clears it; the registration's page and the list tell the Administrator that button — «Retrimite QR» (on the page «Retrimite emailul») for the race number, the signed declaration or the confirmation, «Trimite reminderul» for the reminder, and for a family member's refusal the button on that person's registration — never one that cannot clear it; the Organizer is asked to ask an Administrator to press it; for an address that refuses the club's mail and for «Detalii actualizate» and the cancellation, which no button sends again, the list and the page tell the Administrator and the Organizer alike «Sună persoana: …» — for the update «nu a primit „Detalii actualizate” — ce s-a schimbat e pe pagina evenimentului», never what changed — and the desk, which has no phone, «Spune-i persoanei: …» what to say to the person in front of it, for an address that refuses mail «Spune-i persoanei că emailurile clubului nu ajung la ea; un administrator o sună.»; every reader × kind × type is held to this by a table test; none says the address refused an email that is merely owed (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §670).
56. Given a registration's page, then its email history reads newest first, each row's role (the participant, the club's archive copy, the club's confirmation notice, a club copy) and never a club mailbox's address, the road, the delivery, the refusal with its cause and — on a refused row only — the code and the redacted words, what came after it, and the staff member who asked for a manual resend (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §670).
57. Given a participant's refused message and a later message of the same registration that carries what it carried — the confirmation («Retrimite QR») for a refused race number (`BIB_ASSIGNED`) or signed declaration (`DECLARATION_SIGNED`), the reminder for a refused race number, and the confirmation, the reminder, the declaration request or a later «Detalii actualizate» for a refused «Detalii actualizate» (`EVENT_UPDATE_NOTICE`), each sent no earlier than the refused one was queued, what it carries being read at its send — when the later message leaves, then the refusal reads sent again (and, for the club's account's refusal, is over), and when it is delivered the refusal is over; whichever event is processed first, written on the refused row so it stays over after the 90-day sweep deletes the later message; every other message type answers only its own refusal; migration 0131's backfill reads the same pairs for the rows sent before it (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §670).
58. Given a refusal the club's account caused, or one owed to an address that works again, or one sent again, then it is part of the registration's email state only while the registration it was for still needs the refused message — the one the page sends again for its status now, or one that message carries; on a confirmed registration of an event not yet started the reminder; on a registration the event's notices go to (owing the declaration, offered a place, confirmed, waiting), «Detalii actualizate» while the event is on and not started and the cancellation while it is cancelled and not started; a verification after the address was confirmed or the registration left «Așteaptă confirmarea emailului», a declaration link after the signing, a waiting-list email after the list, an informational notice, or anything but where the registration stands and the cancellation on a cancelled event shows no chip, is not kept by «Doar cu un email respins» or its count, is not «Yes» in the export and says nothing in «Ce îi spui», and stays in the registration's history; a refusal of an address with nothing delivered to it since is shown whatever the message and the registration's status, until the event ends (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §670).
59. Given a family member's refusal at the same event on the same address, then it is read on every registration of the address at that event with the same kind and the same still-needed rule, against its own registration's status: sent again, it waits on both; answered by a message that carries it, it is silent on both; owed, it is owed on both, the words saying it is sent again from that person's registration (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §670).
60. Given a registration of an event that has ended — its `ends_at` passed, or without one the end of its start day in the event's own time zone — then no refusal of any kind, an address that refuses the club's mail included, shows a chip on the list or at the desk, is kept by «Doar cu un email respins» or its count, is «Yes» in the export, says anything in «Ce îi spui» or counts for its race on the club's side; the registration's history keeps every row; on race day, after the start too, every one of them still shows (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §670).
61. Given «Detalii actualizate» or the cancellation that the club's account refused or that is owed to an address that works again, on a registration the event's notices go to, then it shows while the event is ahead — the update on an event that is on, the cancellation on a cancelled one — with no button named, the Administrator and the Organizer told to phone the person and the desk told what to say to them — that «Detalii actualizate» did not arrive and what changed is on the event's page, or that the event was cancelled; the confirmation, the reminder, the declaration request or a later update notice that leaves or is delivered answers the update's refusal; from the start, or once the registration is checked in, neither shows (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §670).
62. Given «Email respins» in words, then an email that never left — the club's account refused it, whatever its row says of the send, or the provider refused the address at the send — is never said «trimis» or «Respins la aceeași adresă»: it is said «refuzat la trimitere» at the refused attempt's instant, never «pus în coadă»; a family member's reads «Nu a plecat, pentru altă înscriere de la aceeași adresă și acest eveniment», and the address's refusal at the send «Nu a plecat: furnizorul a refuzat adresa.», never the recipient's server; a complaint is always said as an email that left; every combination of own, sent, kind, status, cause and reader is held to this by a table test (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §670).
63. Given a registration checked in at the desk, then a refused «Detalii actualizate» or cancellation that the club's account refused or that is owed to an address that works again shows no chip, is not kept by «Doar cu un email respins» or its count and is not «Yes» in the export — race day before the start included; an address that refuses the club's mail still shows (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §670).
64. Given the registrations list, then a row whose email state asks somebody to act carries one line under the name, a 44-pixel link to its «Emailuri» with the filter's glyph — red «{cause} · {short name} · {day}» for an address that refuses mail, amber «{short name} lipsește · adresa merge acum» or «Nu a plecat · {short name} · {day}» — each part (label, short name, date) unbreakable, so the line wraps only at « · » — the common line («Adresa nu există · Confirmarea cu QR · 3 oct.») on one line at 400 px, the longest realistic one («Adresa nu mai există» or «Mailgun nu mai trimite» with «Numărul de concurs» and a two-digit day) on two lines at most at 360 px and at 400 px — the day written as the day and the month alone (no weekday, year or hour), no provider words and no address; an email sent again draws nothing; «Doar cu un email respins (n)» says how many it keeps under the other filters (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §671).
65. Given the words of an email state, then every cause has a label of at most four words, a why and a to-do for each reader, every sentence under 200 characters: the Administrator told the press by its button's name, the Organizer to ask an Administrator for it and to phone where no press helps, the desk what to say to the person and never to phone; «Detalii actualizate» and the cancellation, which nothing sends again, end with the call whatever the cause (at the desk, what to tell the person); on a confirmed registration the to-do opens with «Locul rămâne confirmat; la masă îl găsiți după nume.» and never names a new registration; a refusal whose instant was not stored is said «pus în coadă», never «refuzat la trimitere» (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §671).
66. Given the desk, then a row shows «Fără QR pe email — caută după nume» only while its email state asks somebody to act and the refused email is the QR confirmation or the race number's email it carries — this person's own, or any at an address that refuses mail — its tooltip saying it does not stop the number being handed out and what to tell the person, for an email owed «Spune-i persoanei: emailul cu QR nu i-a ajuns; numărul îl ridică după nume.» and never a press; a family member's owed one and every other email show nothing, and the chip carries no address and no provider words (2026-10-08, `DECISIONS.md` §671).

**Verification:** integration `registrations/bibs.test.ts`, `registrations/race-day.test.ts`, `cms/bib-design.test.ts`; unit `registrations/bibs-pdf.test.ts`, `registrations/bib-design.test.ts`; integration `registrations/void-bibs.test.ts` (16, 17); unit `registrations/race-number.test.ts` (16); unit `registrations/bib-footer.test.ts` (19); e2e `bib-design.spec.ts` (19)

### 4.8 Public runner profiles

#### BR-REQ-038-01 — Profiles are private by default

- **Source:** BR-BUS-038, BR-BUS-070
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §10.9, §15.9
- **Priority:** SHOULD
- **Release:** M4

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a newly created profile, when its public URL is requested, then the response is 404 until the participant explicitly publishes it.
2. Given a published profile, when it renders, then it shows only display name, biography, and allowlisted links, and never an email address or registration history.
3. Given a published profile, when the page is served, then it carries `noindex, nofollow` and does not appear in the sitemap or any directory.
4. Given a participant who is not verified, when profile management is attempted, then it is refused.

**Verification:** integration `profiles/visibility.test.ts`; e2e `runner-profile.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-038-02 — Social links are validated
