The server narrows by that place's own references (kind and id), never by the whole list in the browser. An event's and a page's pickers open on it, falling back to «Toate» when the place uses no picture yet. An album's picker opens on «Toate» (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §485).
41. A film's poster may come from the gallery and is cropped to the part its 16∶9 box shows, over the club's poster or YouTube's own thumbnail. A film stored before the change keeps its exact JSON and is still drawn covered and centred (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §485).
42. Since migration 0092, `events.video_poster_url` is neither written by the older pictures' button nor counted as a reference by the orphan sweep. A poster the description's youtube node names is kept (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §485).
43. A team card carries at most twelve typed https links; the save and the database CHECK team_members_links_is_a_short_array_of_https_links hold the same number (MAX_TEAM_LINKS).
44. «Din galerie» in a rich text finds a stored picture by its file name with accents and case ignored, narrows by the «Folosită în» chips, and inserts the same stored file (the same /api/media address), telling the editor «Din galerie: <name>, W × H px.» — tests/e2e/gallery-picker.spec.ts, mobile and desktop (§485).
45. A saved page's or event's picker opens on its own place («Această pagină» / «Acest eveniment») listing only the pictures that place uses; an album's picker opens on «Toate» with «Acest album» beside it and takes a stored picture once, saying «… era deja în album.» on a second press — tests/e2e/gallery-picker.spec.ts (§485).
46. «Întrebări frecvente» is a standard page. It is a 404 while DRAFT. Once published it lists only the questions shown on the site, in the page's own language, grouped under their «Categorie» with uncategorised questions first, under the club's introduction when one is written in both languages. Each question is a native fold with a glyph and a #q-<id8> anchor that opens it. The same questions are published as FAQPage JSON-LD.
47. The FAQ page is edited and saved as one form: the introduction and every question card, in order, against the page's version. A stale version is a CONFLICT. A question and its answer are required in both languages; a category and the introduction are both languages or neither. An answer may hold pictures but no table.
48. A card of «Echipa» takes its photo through the same upload as a picture in a text: the quality choice, «Din galerie», the chosen file's size and what it was stored as. After the choice comes the crop box with the five shapes, 1:1 pressed first. The crop is saved with the person as four fractions of the photo, is kept only with a photo, and one the box could not draw is refused on the photo box. The public page, the backoffice list and the preview all draw that crop in its own shape, with `srcset` and `sizes`. A card with no crop draws the covered square with the face near the top, as before. Verification: unit `content/team-photo-crop.test.ts`, integration `cms/team.test.ts`, e2e `gallery-picker.spec.ts` (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §541).
49. The bib designer's two picture places join the places that take a picture «Din galerie»: a picture chosen there, or uploaded there with the quality choice and its shown dimensions, opens the same crop box, held to the place's one shape with no row of shapes to choose from (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §560).
50. A picture named by an event's bib design counts as in use: the orphan sweep never takes it, a delete from «Poze» is refused, and the pictures page names the event (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §560).
51. A published standing page and «Echipa» end with one secondary line «<legal name> (<site name>) · CIF <CIF>», composed from CLUB_LEGAL_NAME, CLUB_REGISTRATION_NUMBER and the site's name constant, wrapping inside 320 px. A label the variable carries itself («CIF …», «CUI …») is written once. Without the CIF it reads «<legal name> (<site name>)», and without the legal name it is absent. The registered seat is never shown. Tested in tests/integration/contact/club-identity-render.test.ts and tests/e2e/pages.spec.ts (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §565).
52. An Administrator orders every entry of the site menu — «Evenimente», «Calendar», «Contact», «Galerie», «Echipa», «Întrebări frecvente» and each custom page (never «Membri», which is a link in the footer's fold) — in one list on «Pagini» → «Paginile clubului» → «Ordinea meniului», with «Sus» / «Jos» and one confirmed «Salvează ordinea»; the order is one `platform_settings` value of keys (`page:<id>` for a custom page), an entry it does not name (a fresh site, a page written after the save) falls to the end in the default order, a deleted page's key is dropped, and an entry not on the site keeps its place, greyed «nu apare încă» (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §571) (amended 2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §591).
53. The page editor has no order box and the custom pages' list no ↑ / ↓: a new page is written after every page there is, so it is last in the menu until it is moved, and the list shows each page's place in the whole menu (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §571).

**Verification:** integration `cms/pages.test.ts`; integration `cms/boundary.test.ts`; integration `cms/media-references.test.ts`; unit `content/rich-text.test.ts`; e2e `pages.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-054-01 — The photo gallery: albums on R2, light by construction

- **Source:** BR-BUS-050, BR-BUS-052
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §12.10, §17, §9.2, §8
- **Priority:** SHOULD
- **Release:** M1 — pulled forward from M5 on 2026-09-17 as the small version (`DECISIONS.md` §66); the media library for other content, captions and the Tiptap image node stay M5.
- **Status:** built; needs the R2 bucket of `SETUP.md` §32 on a deployed environment.

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given an editorial role, when they create an album, then it has a date the photos were taken, an optional event it is from, and a title, address and optional description in every language; it is a draft with no photos.
2. Given an album, when a photo is uploaded, then the browser first shrinks it to at most 2000px on its long side and posts it as WebP, one request per photo; the server decides the format from the bytes, refuses anything but JPEG, PNG and WebP (SVG included), refuses a file over 6 MB or under 200px on a side, rotates it upright, drops every EXIF field including the GPS position, and stores exactly two WebP variants — `web` (≤1600px) and `thumb` (≤480px) — under an opaque key prefixed with the environment. The original is not kept.
3. Given an album's first photo, when it is stored, then it becomes the album's cover; any photo can be made the cover later; removing the cover moves it to the first remaining photo.
4. Given an album with no photo, when publication is attempted, then it is refused with a reason. Given one with photos, when it is published, then both languages go live together and its address stops changing (§11.5).
5. Given the public gallery, when it renders, then it lists published albums newest first with cover, title, date and count, and an album page shows its photos as thumbnails in upload order, each a plain link to its `web` variant, every image with its dimensions and lazy loading, and no script — a locale with no translation is a 404, never the other language (BR-REQ-040-02). The "Galerie" section appears in the site navigation only while a published album exists.
6. Given a photo or an album that is removed, when the removal completes, then its rows are gone first and its objects are deleted from storage afterwards — an object without a row is a cost nobody notices, a row without an object is a broken image somebody does.
7. Given a deployed environment without the five `R2_*` variables, when it boots, then it boots; albums can be created, uploads are refused with a sentence, `/admin/tasks` shows the storage task open with the steps, and the site's public gallery is simply empty. Locally photos live under `.media/` and in tests in memory, so neither needs a bucket.
8. Given the images the site serves, when they are loaded, then they come from `R2_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` (Cloudflare, egress free) and never through a function; a hostname appears nowhere in `src/` — the S3 endpoint and the public base are configuration.
9. Given a photograph uploaded through the editor or the gallery, when it is stored, then it has been encoded lossily once and not twice — the browser sends the original whenever the server will accept it — and the web variant is 2400px at quality 88, which is what a full-width picture needs on a 2× screen (2026-09-20, `DECISIONS.md` §176).
10. Given the album form, when it renders, then the album's day is the backoffice date picker (`30.09.2026`), posting `takenOn` as `YYYY-MM-DD` exactly as before and coming back after a refused submit (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §345).
11. Given the album editor, when an album is saved with its description in one language only, then the save is refused on the empty box and the rest is kept. Both or neither is accepted (§354). Verification: unit content/bilingual-everywhere.test.ts; integration cms/gallery.test.ts; e2e gallery.spec.ts.
12. Given a photo uploaded to an album, when it is stored, then it is stored at the quality chosen beside the upload button: «Normală» (the default, and what a request without a choice gets) keeps at most 2400 px on the long side; «Înaltă» keeps at most 4000 px, lossy at quality 90 or near-lossless when that costs at most twice as much, which is how posters and lettering come out. A value that is neither is refused with nothing written. The browser sends at most 3000 px for «Normală» and 4000 px for «Înaltă», sends a file that fits as it is, and keeps every request under 4 MB. Besides the master and the thumbnail, the photo is stored at every width of 480, 640, 960, 1280, 1600, 1920 and 2400 that is narrower than 0.9 of the master, all of them removed with the photo. The album page's tiles and the albums page's covers offer those widths as `srcset` with the drawn width as `sizes`, so a 390-px phone at 3× takes a rung and never an enlarged thumbnail. A photo stored before the ladder has no `srcset` and keeps its thumbnail. The choice is remembered for the tab, in the page's memory when the browser refuses the store, and after the upload the page states the last photo's size, quality, bytes and number of files. Verified by unit `media/images.test.ts`, `media/ladder.test.ts`, `media/browser-shrink.test.ts`, `media/image-quality-choice.test.ts`, integration `cms/image-quality.test.ts` and e2e `gallery.spec.ts` (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §414).
13. Every event page, whether a race or a group run, carries the photographs notice from the counsel review's amendment, word for word in both languages, with a way to ask for a photo to be taken down and a link to the privacy notice. The gallery's upload help ends with the upload rule, word for word, in both languages (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §421).
14. Criterion 14 — a press of the older-pictures button that fails a picture (missing master, undecodable master, a store refusal, or a transient per-picture exception) logs which asset id failed and records up to `perPress` of those ids on the audit row's metadata as `failedIds`, alongside the existing `converted`/`failed`/`left` counts (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §430).
15. Criterion 14 — the task board's failed-picture copy, in both Romanian and English, states that the file is usually missing from storage or not a picture, and hedges that if the picture is still failing after one more press it should be re-uploaded where it is used — it no longer asserts unconditionally that pressing again never helps (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §430).
16. An album may be linked to an event or be free (no event). The public gallery lists both kinds in one list, newest first by the day taken. An event album's card names its event only while that event is published in the reader's locale; a free album's card names no event.
17. The backoffice album list groups albums into event albums and free albums, each newest first, and each group says when it is empty.
18. Criterion 12 (extended from two levels to four): every album photo upload offers «Calitate: Minimă / Medie (recomandat) / Mare / Originală», with Medie as the default. Minimă keeps a master of at most 1280 px, Medie 2400, Mare 4000, and Originală the file's own pixels up to 6000. Nothing is enlarged. The 3200 rung exists only under a master wider than 4000, and a stored master of 4000 px or less never has a 3200 file in its srcset.
19. 14 (addition). The older-pictures press also rewrites the picture's address in the «Echipa» bios, both languages, bumping each changed row's version, and in the team page's introduction setting (§483). Verification: integration `cms/older-pictures.test.ts`.
20. Removing a photo from an album, or deleting an album, deletes the picture itself only when nothing else uses it: a text, a card of «Echipa», another album or a cover (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §485).
21. A picture the club already stored can be reused from «Din galerie» in a page's text, an event's summary and description, a film's poster, an «Echipa» card and an album. It is linked by the same stored file, never copied. Removing it from an album keeps it while a page still uses it.
22. Given a multi-file album upload whose last file fails, when it ends, then the stored-facts line does not show the facts of an earlier photo under the failed file's «Fotografia aleasă».

**Verification:** unit `media/images.test.ts`; integration `cms/gallery.test.ts`; e2e `gallery.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-051-01 — Editorial workflow and permissions

- **Source:** BR-BUS-051, BR-BUS-060
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §11.2, §13.1
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M5
- **Status:** built for events during M1 (`DECISIONS.md` §25); it applies to articles and pages
  when those exist. Criterion 2 changed with `DECISIONS.md` §28: publication is one state for
  the whole event rather than one per language (`DECISIONS.md` §28).

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a copywriter (`COPYWRITER`), when they work in the CMS, then they can edit the text of any event or page at any status — with the live-edit acknowledgement on published content — create page drafts and submit any draft for review, and cannot publish, configure an event, or touch the gallery; given a volunteer (`CONTRIBUTOR`), then every text is refused and their backoffice is the desk and the guide (`DECISIONS.md` §103).
2. Given an **Administrator**, when they review a submission, then they can publish, unpublish, and archive the event, and both languages go live or come down together; given an Organizer (`MODERATOR`), then those four moves are refused — they prepare, submit and return work, and archive what was never published — because every move that crosses into or out of public view belongs to the Administrator (2026-09-20, `DECISIONS.md` §201). *Editing the text of an already-published page is not yet part of this and remains the copywriter's with the acknowledgement of criterion 4: raising it would reverse criterion 3, and §201 records why that is the club's decision rather than the platform's.*
3. Given published content, when a volunteer attempts to edit it, then it is refused; a copywriter's live edit is accepted with the acknowledgement of criterion 4.
4. Given a save that affects live content, when it is submitted, then the interface warns before it takes effect.
5. Given two editors saving the same record, when the second save carries a stale version, then it is rejected as a conflict, and the first editor's save survives intact. This is verified with two real database connections, not the in-process test database, which is single-connection and cannot express the race. The event row carries a version of its own, so a publish that races a change to the event is a conflict too.
6. Given an event where any language is missing a field the public page renders, or has no translation at all, when publication is attempted, then it is refused with the language and the missing fields named, and nothing goes public.
7. Given a save refused as a CONFLICT, with JavaScript on or off, when the form renders again, then each version guard (the event row, each translation, a page, an album) is recalled with the boxes rather than re-read from the database, so a second press is refused as well and the colleague's save survives; the summary says to copy what is needed and reload rather than send again (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §315).
8. Only the chosen registration mode's fields are shown in "Participare și înscrieri"; a capacity, declaration or public list posted with a mode other than on-site registration, and an organizer name or link posted with a mode other than elsewhere, are saved as none rather than refused.
9. A refusal of the event form — by the browser or the server — opens every closed box and card around each field it names and brings that field's language tab forward, with every value kept (e.g. a missing declaration on an on-site registration opens "Participare și înscrieri" › "Condiții de participare și declarația").
10. Given a role without settings rights (Redactor) on the event editor, when "Ce fel de eveniment" renders, then "Setările le schimbă Administratorul." appears exactly once, in place of the type. Its three cards are shown as their headings and closed lines with nothing to open, and no `event.*` field is posted. Verification: unit `content/editor-first-card.test.ts`; e2e `cms-publish.spec.ts` (2026-09-24, `DECISIONS.md` §358).
11. Migration `0072_family_registration` is expand-only: it adds the name key and its unique index beside the one-registration-per-address constraint. The family flow stays closed, and no link is offered, until a later contract migration drops that constraint. `family-gate.ts` reads the catalogue, so the flow opens with no flag and no deploy of its own (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §389).
12. On a draft event's editor, a role that may publish is offered «Publică» beside «Trimite spre verificare». One press walks DRAFT → IN_REVIEW → PUBLISHED under the same guard and version check as «Creează și publică». A refused publication leaves the event a draft, and a role that may not publish is refused before anything moves (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §423).
13. «Copiază și tradu tot: RO → EN» is visible at the top of every editor; with the English empty it translates in one press, with English text present it asks naming how many and which boxes and offers «Înlocuiește tot» or «Doar cele goale», and nothing is saved until «Salvează» (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §482).
14. The confirm title's count is phrased through countForm's one / few / other keys, never an ICU plural (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §482).
15. The backoffice guide's «Engleza dintr-o apăsare» is one four-step list per role (write the Romanian, press, read and correct, «Salvează»), and the Organizer's version does not contradict the rule that the title and description are the Administrator's (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §482).
16. Given a translation press within the day's budget, when DeepL's credit as last read is spent, then the press is refused as `quota` and nothing is sent. When the credit is smaller than the press's characters, it is refused as `credit`, naming the characters left, and nothing is sent or metered. When the credit could not be read, the press goes to DeepL as before (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §497). Verification: integration `translate/translate.test.ts`.
17. The backoffice events list reads its search, state and order from the address, applies them with one «Aplică» / "Apply" button, and shows «N din M evenimente» while it is narrowed (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §527).
18. A bulk or row action taken from the events list redirects back to the same search, state, order and page. The `back` field is re-parsed so that only the list's own keys survive (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §527).
19. Given the backoffice events list with no `state` in the address, when it renders, then it shows only the dates still to come («Viitoare» selected in «Starea») and the count line «N din M evenimente»; «Toate» posts `state=ALL` and shows every date; an unknown value falls back to «Viitoare» (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §555).
20. On «Membri», with «Beneficiile membrilor» or the members' zone written in Romanian and its English empty, «Copiază și tradu tot: RO → EN» translates it in one press and the English editor shows the translation. It reads the Romanian as typed at the moment of the press, and says «Nu e nimic de tradus» only when that Romanian is empty. `benefitsEnBody` and `zoneEnBody` are on the translate allowlist as rich texts, with `benefitsRoBody` / `zoneRoBody` as their twins (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §572).
21. Each members' text shows its two editors as tabs «RO» | «EN» in one strip, one editor at a time, with «Copiază și tradu tot» above the tabs. The hidden language's editor stays in the form, so the save posts both. Each tab carries the header's flag beside its code, the flag never being the tab's name. The English tab reads «gol» («empty») while its editor is empty, re-read as the person types. With both languages empty the row fits at 320 px without scrolling (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §572).
22. After a translate press fills a box in a strip's English panel, in the form that pressed, that strip brings its English tab forward and the tab reads «tradus — verifică» («translated — check it», `Translate.tabTranslated`) until the person types in it, on every strip with no word passed. The press announces only the boxes it filled: a strip in another form, one whose English holds no filled box, or an answer for a name the form has no box for moves no tab (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §572).

**Verification:** integration `cms/workflow.test.ts`; concurrency `cms-conflict.test.ts` (`yarn test:concurrency`); e2e `cms-publish.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-051-02 — Protected preview

- **Source:** BR-BUS-051
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §11.5
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M5
- **Status:** built for event translations during M1 (`DECISIONS.md` §25).

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a preview URL, when it is opened without staff authorization, then access is refused before any row is read, so the response does not reveal whether the draft exists.
2. Given a preview page, when it renders, then it carries `noindex`, is absent from the sitemap, and is not publicly cached.
3. Given a preview URL, when it is opened by staff, then it renders the translation of the locale in that URL, whatever its editorial status.
4. Given the event editor or the create page, when a staff member who may preview presses «Previzualizează», then the listing card and the event page are drawn from the form's unsaved values by the listing's and the page's own components, in the chosen language, at a phone's 360 pixels or a desktop's width, and nothing is written — no row, no version, no audit, no email — and no public cache entry is read, filled or expired (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §579).
5. Given a value the save would refuse, when it is previewed, then the preview names the boxes the save names and draws no page; given a language that lacks what publication needs, or an optional text written in the other language only, then that language is drawn and marked «incomplet» with the boxes it lacks (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §579).
6. Given the Tehnic, the volunteer, a member or a stranger, when an event's preview before saving is asked for, then it is refused before any row is read; given a preview's registration door, then its button is shown disabled with «previzualizare» and links to no form (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §579).
7. Given the event editor's «Previzualizare», when a staff member who may preview chooses «Formular» and presses «Previzualizează», then the public registration form is drawn from the unsaved values by the register page's own parts, in the chosen language: the T-shirt size only when the kit gives a shirt, the health note's fold only when the event asks it, the event's minimum age in the age line and the birth date's bounds, the account's address named and not asked on a members' event, the club's limit per address under a typed address, the public-list tick (with its states and the socials) and «oferte și beneficii» only under a notice in force that describes them, and the terms by the version in force, or the form's own warning while none is approved (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §586).
8. Given the preview's registration form, then it has no action, no field the service reads (the event, the timing check, the honeypot, the terms version shown, the second try), no Turnstile widget, script or token field, and its send button is disabled and marked «previzualizare»; and drawing it writes no hold, registration, email or audit row and reads, fills or expires no public cache entry (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §586).
9. Given a draft whose registration window is not open, then the preview's form is drawn under one line with the event page's own sentence; given an event that takes no registration on the site or whose date is to be announced, then no form is drawn and one line says why; given a run that offers its group-run declaration, then one line says the run's page offers it (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §586).

**Verification:** integration `cms/preview.test.ts`; unit `seo/private-paths.test.ts`; e2e `cms-publish.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-052-01 — Publication quality gates

- **Source:** BR-BUS-052
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §11.6, §20
- **Priority:** SHOULD
- **Release:** M5

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given an image without alternative text, when publication is attempted, then it is blocked or clearly warned.
2. Given content generated with AI assistance, when it is created, then it remains in Draft until a human publishes it.
3. Given a published article, when it renders, then it carries a title, summary, SEO description, and locale metadata.

**Verification:** integration `cms/quality.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-052-02 — Structured data for events, articles, and the club

- **Source:** BR-BUS-052, BR-BUS-010, BR-BUS-034
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §18.1
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given the homepage, when it renders, then it contains one `SportsOrganization` JSON-LD block with a stable `@id`, the club name, logo, URL, and `sameAs` entries for the club's official profiles. `sameAs` comes from `CLUB_FACEBOOK_URL`, `CLUB_INSTAGRAM_URL` and `CLUB_STRAVA_URL` and is omitted where none is configured; `logo` is still absent and needs an approved raster (`DECISIONS.md` §58).
2. Given a published event page, when it renders, then it contains a `SportsEvent` block whose start and end times carry the event timezone offset, whose `organizer` references the club `@id`, whose `location` includes a postal address, and whose `image` lists the event's two cards (2026-09-19, `DECISIONS.md` §155).
3. Given a capped event, when the block renders, then `remainingAttendeeCapacity` equals the free-place count displayed on the same page.
4. Given a cancelled event, when the page renders, then the block is still present with `eventStatus` set to cancelled.
4a. Given a race with a gun time distinct from the event start, when the block renders, then `startDate` is the race start and `doorTime` is the event start; with no distinct gun time, `startDate` falls back to the event start.
5. Given a published article (M5), when it renders, then it contains an `Article` block with `datePublished` and `dateModified`.
6. Given any structured data block on any page, when it is inspected, then it contains no participant name, email, registration list, or declaration content.
7. Given the test suite, when it runs, then it parses the emitted JSON-LD and asserts the required properties are present.
8. Given a published event page, when it renders, then its `og:image` is a 1200×630 card drawn on the server from the event's own facts (title, date and time, meeting point, distance), absolute under `APP_BASE_URL`, with `twitter:card = summary_large_image`; every other public page carries the site's card; and the event page offers, as 44-pixel buttons, the phone's own share sheet where `navigator.share` exists, Facebook, WhatsApp and, under "Instagram", the same card shared through the phone's own sheet where a file can be shared from a coarse pointer — otherwise offered as a download and labelled as one — with "add to calendar" as its own row; and the footer's three social marks are 44×44 items of one wrapping row, never drawn over the text or the build badge at any width (2026-09-18, `DECISIONS.md` §90; 2026-09-19, §140; 2026-09-23, §299).
9. on an EXTERNAL-registration, PAID event, offers.url is the organizer's own externalRegistrationUrl rather than the club's cost_url — the place where the offered thing is actually taken — and isAccessibleForFree stays false. The .ics description's cost line carries the same «Cost: <amount>, la organizator» wording, with the discount note appended when the club stated one (2026-09-25, DECISIONS.md §395).
10. Given a published event that the club registers itself (registration mode INTERNAL, not a group run) with a minimum age N above 0, when its block renders, then it carries `typicalAgeRange: "N-"`; with N = 0, or for an event registered elsewhere or not at all, the property is absent (2026-09-23, `DECISIONS.md` §329). Verification: unit `events/structured-data.test.ts`; e2e `registration-form.spec.ts`.
11. criterion (new): the SportsOrganization and SportsEvent organiser name, the page title, the header link's and wordmark's accessible names, the share pictures, the calendar's PRODID and feed file name, the legal PDF's Author and the EMAIL_FROM_NAME default are all `CLUB_NAME`. No string under `src/` outside `theme/brand.ts` and the seeds names the club, and a test fails otherwise (§369).
12. Given an event whose map link carries coordinates in its address, or which has typed «Coordonate», when its JSON-LD is built, then its `Place` carries `geo` as `GeoCoordinates` with that point. Given only the club's fallback place, or a place to be announced, then no `geo` is published (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §416).
13. The event page's «Vârstă» fact row and the JSON-LD `typicalAgeRange` state the event's minimum age for every event type and registration mode whenever it is above zero. The parent's clause is added only where the club takes the registrations (INTERNAL). With no minimum and no registration here, the page has no age row and the JSON-LD has no `typicalAgeRange`. Covered by `tests/unit/events/age-every-type.test.ts` and `tests/unit/events/structured-data.test.ts`.
14. The event page's «Vârstă» fact row and the JSON-LD `typicalAgeRange` state the event's minimum age for every event type and registration mode whenever it is above zero. The parent's clause is added only where the club takes the registrations (INTERNAL). With no minimum and no registration here, the page has no age row and the JSON-LD has no `typicalAgeRange`. Covered by `tests/unit/events/age-every-type.test.ts` and `tests/unit/events/structured-data.test.ts`.
15. Given a published event the club registers itself, when its block renders, then `typicalAgeRange` is "N-" with N the minimum age that binds, never under 14. An event stored with 0 states "14-" (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §515, amending criterion 10). Verification: unit `events/structured-data.test.ts`; e2e `registration-form.spec.ts`.
16. Amends criterion 8. Given a published event, when its card is drawn in either shape, then it is drawn from one design object with platform defaults (palette, accent, which elements show, an optional tagline and background picture) that nothing stores yet; by default it carries the club's logo (150 pixels tall on the square, 100 on the wide card), the type pill as a quiet tint (a cancelled event's pill is the solid accent whether the type is shown or not, so the two differ in colour as well as in word), the title (at most three lines, cut at a word with «…» past 110 characters), the date with the time as the headline, the meeting point with a pin on one line (cut at a word with «…» past 60 characters or where its line ends), the distance and the climb as chips, and a paper band with the site's host; a switched-off element leaves no gap, and a card with a background picture keeps its white words legible under a veil — at least 4.5:1 behind the logo and the pill over a near-white photograph (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §609).
17. Amends criterion 8. Given any title, place and tagline, when the card is drawn in either shape, then every fact stays on it: the title takes the largest size at which its lines, three at most, fit the height the rest of the card leaves, so the paper band fills exactly the card's last 104 rows on the square and 80 on the wide card and the logo keeps its place; on the square a sentence-case title of up to 110 characters is drawn at 50 pixels or more (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §609).
18. Amends criterion 8. The card's background picture is fetched only from an `https:` address — never taken as a `data:` address, never through a redirect — within five seconds and four megabytes (refused on the announced length before the body is read); whatever design a caller hands over is read through `readShareCardDesign` first, and a picture that cannot be had draws the card without it (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §609).
19. Amends criterion 11. The share pictures carry the club's name as the lockup's artwork, drawn from `LOGO` in `@/theme/brand`; `CLUB_NAME` is not typed onto them, and a card whose logo is switched off names the club only through the host in its band (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §609).

**Verification:** integration `seo/structured-data.test.ts`; e2e `event-page.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-070-03 — Public content is machine-readable and crawler policy is explicit

- **Source:** BR-BUS-052, BR-BUS-070
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §18.4
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given any public page, when its server HTML response is fetched without executing JavaScript, then the page's substantive content is present in that response.
2. on an EXTERNAL-registration, PAID event, the cost text reads «Cu taxă, la organizator» / «Paid, to the organizer» with the amount — never the wording an INTERNAL paid event uses, which would read as the club's own fee — and the club's discount note, when stated, follows it in the reader's language, both languages or neither (2026-09-25, DECISIONS.md §395).
3. Given a cancelled or full event, when its text is extracted, then that status is stated in words.
4. Given production `robots.txt`, when it is fetched, then admin, API, participant action and manage paths, declaration pages, preview, and runner profiles are disallowed for every user agent.
5. Given production `robots.txt`, when it is inspected, then the training-crawler policy recorded in `DECISIONS.md` is reflected, with the verification date of the user-agent names recorded.
6. Given a request identifying as a crawler, when a public page is served, then the content is identical to what a person receives.
7. Given production `robots.txt`, then the following are disallowed: every query permutation of the listing and the calendar, an event page asked `lista`, `interest`, `since` or `declaratie`, the live twins under `/<locale>/live/`, the registration and declaration forms, the token pages, the newsletter's pages and the share picture asked for a shape. The canonical listing, calendar and event pages stay crawlable, as do an event page with a key it does not read (`?fbclid=`), every Open Graph picture with or without Next's content-hash query (`/en/events/<slug>/opengraph-image?<hash>`), and the bare share picture named in the JSON-LD (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §549).
8. Given robots.txt, when it is read, then it disallows `/ro/calendar/` and `/en/calendar/` (every period's own path) and still allows the bare `/ro/calendar` and `/en/calendar`; every period page's canonical is the bare calendar (2026-09-30, `DECISIONS.md` §574).

**Verification:** integration `seo/machine-readability.test.ts`; e2e `robots.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-053-01 — Legal documents are versioned and immutable

- **Source:** BR-BUS-053
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §12.5, §11.1
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given a legal document key, when a version is approved, then it carries a version number, an effective date, an approval record, a content hash, and Romanian and English bodies.
2. Given a version that a participant has accepted, when any edit is attempted, then it is rejected.
3. Given a new version, when it becomes effective, then earlier acceptances continue to reference the version that was accepted.
4. Given a version that is approved or referenced, when any interface attempts to change its text, then it is refused — editing is confined to unapproved, unreferenced drafts (BR-REQ-053-02). An approved version is also never deleted and its approval is never withdrawn (`DECISIONS.md` §53) — which is not the same as taking a version out of circulation: one that nothing relied on, and that is not the text in force, may be **withdrawn**, keeping its row, its number and its words (BR-REQ-053-02 criteria 16–19, `DECISIONS.md` §181).
5. Given the public site, when any page renders, then the privacy notice and terms are reachable in the current locale.
6. Given any environment other than production, when it is seeded, then a clearly marked sample version of each key exists, whose own rendered body opens — in both languages — with a banner saying that it is sample text, is not approved by the club, is not legal advice, and must be replaced before a real participant registers.
7. Given `APP_ENV=production`, when the sample text is seeded, then it is refused outright rather than skipped quietly; the club's approved wording is written in the backoffice (BR-REQ-053-02) or, where a migration is preferred, per `docs/RUNBOOKS.md` § Legal document version.
8. Given a sample document, when it is read, then every club-specific fact — the controller's legal name, address and contact, any representative, retention periods, and the lawful basis for each purpose — is an obvious placeholder rather than an invented value.

9. Given a legal text, when a paragraph carries `[the words](https://…)` or `![what it shows](https://…)` on a line of its own, then the page renders a link (https, mailto or a path on this site; anything else stays words) and a picture (https only, lazy, at most the column's width, its words as the caption); the PDFs render the link as "the words (address)" and the picture as its words; the stored body, its hash and the merge fields are the plain text as typed (2026-09-19, `DECISIONS.md` §127).
10. Given the approved privacy notice or terms, when its public page renders, then it says "Versiunea N, în vigoare din <date>" / "Version N, in force since <date>" under the title, and every section heading carries an id (#s1, #s2, …, prefixed per language where both languages are shown side by side) (§323).
11. Given the terms or the privacy notice served from the public cache, when a version is approved, withdrawn or deleted — or a version approved for a later date reaches its effective date with nothing saved — then the next visitor reads the version in force: the approval expires the cached text, and the text is keyed by the stretch between effective dates (§333). Verification: integration `public-cache/revalidation.test.ts`; unit `public-cache/clock.test.ts`.
12. Given the platform's declaration template, when it is read in either language, then it names the risks the runner accepts — terrain and falls, wild animals and dogs with what to do on meeting one, weather and darkness, the runner's own equipment including a working headlamp after dark, their own pace and decisions, protected areas and personal belongings — and every sentence in which the organiser does not answer for something is limited "în limitele permise de lege" / "to the extent the law allows"; none promises immunity.
13. Given any of the platform's legal templates or a seeded sample, when it is read, then it names no event, place, date, time, distance, amount or club fact as words: an event's facts are merge fields and a club's facts are placeholders; only the laws cited, the supervisory authority's statutory contact, 112 and the platform's own fixed periods are written in.
14. Given the sample seed or "start from the platform's text", when a text is produced, then every merge field of the template stays a merge field and every club fact the environment does not know stays its placeholder; re-seeding a changed template inserts the next version, and an unchanged one inserts nothing.
15. Given any string, template or JSX text under `src/` outside `theme/brand.ts` and the seeds, when it is read, then it does not name the club. The default sender name of every email and of the contact form, the share picture's heading, the calendar's product id and feed file name, and every PDF's Author read `CLUB_NAME`, and `EMAIL_FROM_NAME` still overrides the sender's name when it is set (2026-09-24, `DECISIONS.md` §369, §357). Verification: unit `notifications/no-hardcoded-values.test.ts`.
16. The notice on `/admin/legal` states the two refusals the service enforces, separately and without more: registration is refused while no approved privacy notice is in effect, and no place can be confirmed while no approved declaration is; a version somebody relied on is frozen and a correction is the next version; a version nobody relied on may be deleted. The notice folds closed by default and opens from `/admin/legal#legal-versions` (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §376).
17. The platform's templates of the terms and the privacy notice state the email link, the hold, the offer and the reminder through `{{confirmationHours}}`, `{{holdMinutes}}`, `{{offerHours}}` and `{{reminderClause}}`, merged from the setting when the text is shown. The approved text and its hash are untouched (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §377).
18. `{{reminderClause}}` never promises what one event can break. With a club default above zero it reads ", un memento cu <lead> înainte (sau cât alege evenimentul)" / ", a reminder <lead> before (or as the event chooses)". With a default of none it reads ", un memento înainte de start, dacă evenimentul trimite unul" / ", a reminder before the start where the event sends one", with no number and no dotted blank. A value given as "" leaves nothing behind (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §377).
19. The token legend in `/admin/legal` lists the four deadline tokens with an example in each language, written by the same function that fills the text in that language, and describes the reminder clause as it behaves (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §377).
20. Two optional legal documents exist beside the three a registration rests on: the group run's self-declaration for asphalt and for trail. They are approved, versioned and deleted like the others, and gate no registration: an approved one stands in for none of the three, and its absence stops none of them (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §393).
21. A group run's self-declaration is refused while no approved privacy notice is in force in the signing language. Nothing is written and nothing is queued. The run's page shows no offer and the signing page shows no form meanwhile (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §393).
22. A signature counts as reliance on its version: deleting that version and editing it are refused. Once the retention sweep has removed its last signature, a superseded version may be deleted again (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §393).
23. A group run's declarations and their outbox messages are deleted by the retention sweep 7 days after the run's start. The window is one constant, and the run's page, the signing page, the backoffice fold and both emails state it through that value, never as typed words (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §393).
24. The platform's terms template says: registration on the site is free, and on another organiser's event the club only publishes. §3, §4, §5 and §10 are accepted expressly, by a separate box. One address may register others with their agreement, and each person signs their own declaration; nobody signs for another adult. The courts are the ones competent under the Code of Civil Procedure, with a consumer's domicile as a choice, never the club's seat. The template points to ANSPDCP, not SAL-ANPC (2026-09-25, DECISIONS.md §418). Verification: unit legal-documents/no-hardcoded-values.test.ts.
25. The platform's privacy notice names {{publicListPeriod}} in §4 and in §7. It is filled with a period and its unit in the reader's language ('30 de zile', '30 days'), never a number written into the text. Merged with the deadlines, the list states and the list period, the notice carries no dotted blank (2026-09-25, DECISIONS.md §418). Verification: unit deadlines/duration-words.test.ts.
26. The platform's two group-run declarations name no identity document. The signing page therefore asks for none, and a signature under them stores none. The texts state the club's legitimate interest (art. 6(1)(f)), with art. 9(2)(f) for the health statement. They state the platform's seven days and the archive copy's three years. They list the rights of access, rectification, erasure, restriction and objection. A text that still names {{idDocument}} asks for it, and the club's copy masks it as before (2026-09-25, DECISIONS.md §418). Verification: unit legal-documents/group-run-declaration.test.ts, integration group-run-declarations/sign.test.ts.
27. A public registration, from the event's form or the family link, is refused while no terms version is in force, and refused without the terms tick. The tick is required, never folded, and names the version in force and the clauses accepted expressly (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §421).
28. The registration records the terms version in force and the moment of acceptance, and rewrites both when a cancelled registration is sent again. A staff entry records neither and is not refused, because the paper carries the terms (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §421).
29. A tick shown under a terms version that is no longer in force at submit is refused and names the tick. The form brings the box back unticked and says the terms changed. An empty or unreadable posted version counts as none and says nothing changed (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §421).
30. A terms version somebody accepted counts as relied on: its withdrawal and deletion are refused, as for a privacy notice with acknowledgements (2026-09-25, `DECISIONS.md` §421).
31. The registration's backoffice page shows the privacy notice version acknowledged and the terms version accepted expressly, each with its moment. A staff or desk entry says the terms are on paper, and a row with no recorded terms version says none was recorded (§425).
32. Given a legal text that names `{{minimumAge}}`, then the token is listed in the /admin/legal token legend with an example in both languages («16 ani» / "16 years"). The seed and «start from the platform's text» keep it as a field, and the approved version's hash covers the unmerged text, so leaving the sentence out at zero changes no hash (2026-09-26, `DECISIONS.md` §440).
33. When the club starts a legal text from the platform's text, the club-email placeholder is filled with the addresses in force: «a sau b» in the Romanian text and "a or b" in the English one. An approved text keeps the address it was approved with.
34. Every declaration document — the signed PDF, the club's archive copy, the event's bundle, the blank desk form and the group-run PDF — names the version of the text it carries and the day that version took effect («Versiunea N, în vigoare din <weekday, date>», on the club's clock) under each entry's title and in the footer of every page, each page naming its own entry's version; a signed page's footer also says when it was signed. Both signing pages show the same line over the text.
35. A group run's optional self-declaration (§393) is never deleted by the retention sweep. It leaves only through the Administrator's audited erase, on the signer's request, or with its event. The sweep clears only its identity document, seven days after the run.
36. The run's page, the backoffice fold, the signer's email, the club's archive email, both group-run declaration templates and the privacy-notice template say, in Romanian and English, that the platform keeps the declaration until the signer asks for it to be deleted. None of them states a number of days for it.
37. A group run's optional self-declaration (§393) is never deleted by the retention sweep. It leaves only through the Administrator's audited erase, on the signer's request, or with its event. The sweep clears only its identity document, seven days after the run.
38. The run's page, the backoffice fold, the signer's email, the club's archive email, both group-run declaration templates and the privacy-notice template say, in Romanian and English, that the platform keeps the declaration until the signer asks for it to be deleted. None of them states a number of days for it.
39. Given an event whose organizer chose a race-declaration version, when a participant signs, then they sign that version's kind (trail `EVENT_DECLARATION` or road `EVENT_DECLARATION_ROAD`). With none chosen, an asphalt course reads the road text and any other or unstated course the trail text. A road race falls back to the trail text while no road text is in force, and the trail text never falls back to the road one. The same resolver serves the page, the signature, the paper form, the desk and the emails (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §515). Verification: unit `legal-documents/race-declaration-keys.test.ts`; integration `registrations/race-declaration-kind.test.ts`.
40. Given the event editor of a race with no declaration chosen, when it renders, then «Declarația pe care o semnează participantul» starts on the newest version in force of the kind its course reads, and follows the surface select until the organizer picks a version by hand. A saved choice is kept, and a note says when its kind does not fit the course while the fitting kind has an approved version (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §515). Verification: unit `legal-documents/race-declaration-keys.test.ts`, `content/editor-declaration-card.test.ts`; e2e `forms-keep-values.spec.ts`.
41. A group run's self-declaration covers every run of its series (§113) from the signing on. The same person, by canonical address and name, signs one version once per series; a newer version is signed beside the older one, which is kept.
42. A signing press never deletes a stored group-run declaration. Only the Administrator's erase, with an audit row, removes one.
43. The signed PDF of a group-run declaration says what its blanks said at the signing. Moving or renaming a date later changes nothing in it.
44. A group-run declaration's text states the series (name, rhythm, usual place) for a run that repeats, and the run, its date and its place for a one-off run.
45. From the signer's own emailed link, the run's page says «Ai semnat deja…» with the version and the day, and shows no button, while that version is in force. After a newer version it asks for a signature again. Without the link the page says nothing about anybody.
46. The privacy notice says an older version's group-run signature is kept as evidence until the signer asks for its erasure, and promises no deletion the platform does not perform, in RO and EN (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §523).
47. The club's archive email for a group-run declaration states one retention rule: kept while the declaration is active, at most three years after a withdrawal, then deleted (2026-09-28, `DECISIONS.md` §523).
48. The platform's group-run trail template and the race's trail template ask for «încălțăminte adecvată terenului, cu aderență corespunzătoare (de preferat pantofi de trail)» and for «indicațiile generale de siguranță comunicate de organizator», and no declaration template names «recomandările autorităților». Both group-run templates and the privacy notice's retention sentence for them keep a copy «pe durata necesară constatării, exercitării sau apărării unor drepturi», with the three-year limitation period of art. 2517, and a document under a complaint, a dispute or proceedings until finally settled; both race templates add that dispute sentence after their three years. Both languages (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §556). Verification: unit `legal-documents/second-review-wording.test.ts`.
49. Given the platform's privacy-notice template, when it is read in either language, then section 5 names `{{promotionalMaterials}}` under «Oferte și beneficii» / “Offers and benefits” and says the purpose, that only the club sends and the partners never receive the address, that the answer is given per registration, the basis art. 6(1)(a), that the box is optional and unticked and how it is withdrawn; section 6 names the partners as receiving nothing and section 7 the retention; no approved text changes, and `/admin/tasks` carries the open row `promoNotice` until a notice naming the field takes effect (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §562).
50. No Romanian legal template, title or body, says «trail» (a whole word, any case), «cursă montană» or «cursă trail»: the mountain race is «eveniment montan» and the ground «teren accidentat», and the race's and the group run's mountain templates ask for «încălțăminte adecvată terenului, cu aderență corespunzătoare (de preferat încălțăminte pentru teren accidentat)»; English keeps "trail" for the ground. The texts in force change only when the club approves a new version (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §564).
51. Given an approved version that a signature, an event or a registration relies on, when it is deleted from `/admin/legal`, then its row, its number and its text in every language stay: every acceptance still points at it, and the registration's page, the desk, the signed PDF and a group-run signature (`findSignedGroupRunDeclaration`) still read that text by id (`tests/integration/legal/delete-when-signed.test.ts`) (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §567).
52. Every declaration template (the race's mountain and road/park texts and the two group-run texts, Romanian and English) names serious injury and death among the inherent risks («Aceste riscuri pot duce la accidentare, la vătămare gravă sau chiar la deces.» / "These risks can lead to injury, to serious injury or even to death."). It says the participant takes part «pe propriul risc» / "at my own risk" and accepts the risks «inclusiv riscul de vătămare gravă sau de deces» / "including the risk of serious injury or death" (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §568).
53. Every declaration template, after the art. 1355(4) sentence, waives «în limitele permise de lege» / "to the extent the law allows" the claims for harm arising solely from the inherent risks, without the organiser's fault, naming serious injury and death in the waiver sentence itself («…ce decurg exclusiv din riscurile inerente, fără vina organizatorului, inclusiv vătămare gravă sau deces.» / "…arising solely from the inherent risks, without the organiser's fault, serious injury or death included."), against the organiser, its organising team, its volunteers and its partners. It binds the heirs within the same limits («Renunțarea îi obligă și pe moștenitorii mei…» / "This waiver also binds my heirs…"), and says it does not cover harm caused through their fault and does not remove the organiser's safety duties. No sentence that names death says the organiser does not answer for it, and the race texts keep the sentence that nothing limits the organiser's liability for intent, gross fault or bodily harm (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §568).
54. The race declarations' minors paragraph says the parent or legal guardian who signs beside a 14–17-year-old accepts for them the risks, death included, and the waiver, «în limitele permise de lege» / "to the extent the law allows"; the group-run texts, adults only, have no such sentence (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §568).
55. Given the platform's privacy-notice template, when it is read in either language, then section 5 no longer says the partners never receive the address and a paragraph «Lista pentru parteneri» / “The list for partners” names `{{promotionalMaterialsShared}}` (filled with the first name, the last name and the email address), the partners as a category, never sold, never a minor's data, one consent for the club's and the partners' messages, a withdrawal that stops every later sharing with the partners told to stop, the basis, the recipients on request with the club keeping whom each list went to and which registrations it held, and the complaint; section 6 names it as the one exception; section 7 carries its retention; the four placeholders and the twelve sections are kept; `{{promotionalMaterials}}` is unchanged (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §570).
56. Given the privacy notice in force, when it does not name `{{promotionalMaterialsShared}}` in every language, then «Descarcă lista pentru sponsori» is a disabled button with «Nota de confidențialitate în vigoare nu spune că lista poate fi dată partenerilor.», the route refuses the file (409) and writes no audit row, no caption at the offers box speaks of partners, and `/admin/tasks` shows the open row `sponsorNotice` with the steps; and when it does, the list holds only a yes whose recorded notice version and whose notice in force at `promo_consent_at` are both approved versions naming the field — a yes given under a notice that said the partners receive nothing is never listed — and the offers box on the form, on the declaration page and on the manage page says the club may give the partners the first name, the last name and the address (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §570).
57. Amends criterion 15. The share pictures carry the club's name as the lockup's artwork, drawn from `LOGO` in `@/theme/brand`, not as a typed heading read from `CLUB_NAME`, and a card with the logo off names the club only through the host (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §609).
58. Given the platform's terms template, then it states that the club may refuse or cancel a registration only on the objective grounds it lists, that the person is told by email with the ground and the place released by the ordinary rules, and that no registration is refused on a ground the law forbids, in both languages; the texts in force are unchanged until the club approves a new version (2026-10-01, `DECISIONS.md` §618).
59. Given the platform's terms template, then §3's last paragraph spells the five refusal grounds as words, byte for byte as §618 wrote them, never a merge field; terms carry the club's right to refuse while one paragraph spells those grounds word for word, and only the terms in force in every language count — never a draft (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §636).
60. Given the terms in force carry the club's right to refuse in every language, then the registration form's express box also names «refuzarea sau anularea unei înscrieri de către club» / “the club refusing or cancelling a registration”, and «Cum funcționează înscrierea» ends with «Clubul poate refuza o înscriere», naming all five of the terms' grounds; otherwise the box reads §421's words exactly and the fold is unchanged, and, while terms are in force, `/admin/tasks` keeps the row `refusalTerms` open (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §636).
61. The privacy notice's template names `{{eventInvitations}}` once in each language, in the paragraph that describes the invitations by email, and `/admin/tasks` shows the row `invitationsNotice` open, never blocking, until a notice naming it is in force in every language (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §647).

**Verification:** integration `legal/versions.test.ts`; unit `legal/inline.test.ts`; e2e `legal-pages.spec.ts`

#### BR-REQ-053-02 — The club writes its own legal text

- **Source:** BR-BUS-053
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §12.5, §11.1, §10.2
- **Priority:** MUST
- **Release:** M1
- **Status:** built, and recorded in `DECISIONS.md` §46 and §53. It narrows BR-REQ-053-01
  criterion 4 rather than reversing it: what may never be edited is a version somebody has
  accepted, and requiring a developer and a migration to *create* one was an accident of that
  rule rather than a consequence of it. §53 adds deletion for a version that was never approved,
  and records why un-approving an approved one is not offered.

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given an Administrator, when they write a new version of a legal document, then it is created as the next version number for that key, unapproved, with its content hash computed from the text saved.
2. Given a draft that is unapproved and unreferenced, when it is rewritten, then the text and the content hash are replaced together, and neither can describe the other's contents.
3. Given a version that is approved, when an edit is attempted, then it is refused with a conflict and the words are unchanged.
4. Given a version that a participant has accepted or an event points at, when an edit is attempted, then it is refused with a conflict.
5. Given a draft, when it is approved, then it becomes public from the moment of approval, records who approved it, and can no longer be edited.
6. Given an approved version, when approval is attempted again, then it is refused.
7. Given a document written in only one language, or with an empty body in either, when it is saved, then it is refused — a public page cannot fall back to the other language (BR-REQ-040-02).
8. Given any role below the one that administers staff, when any of this is attempted, then it is refused.
9. Given a version that was never approved and that nothing references, when it is deleted, then the version and its text in every language are removed together, and the version number becomes available again.
10. Given a Superadministrator on `/admin/legal` while any of the three documents has no approved version, when the club's four facts are set (`CLUB_LEGAL_NAME`, `CLUB_REGISTRATION_NUMBER`, `CLUB_REGISTERED_ADDRESS`, `EMAIL_REPLY_TO`), then one press creates and approves version 1 of each missing document from the platform's text with the facts written in, in the presser's name and effective at that moment; a document already in force is left alone; a fact still unknown refuses the whole act naming the placeholder, and the page names the missing variable instead of the button; below the Superadministrator it is refused (`DECISIONS.md` §132).
11. Given a version that is approved, when deletion is attempted, then it is refused with a conflict whether or not anything references it — the record of what the club published outlives whether anybody acted on it.
12. Given a version that an acceptance names, an event points at, or a registration recorded the number of as the privacy notice it acknowledged, when deletion is attempted, then it is refused with a conflict.
13. Given the backoffice list of versions, when it renders, then each row states how many acceptances, events and registrations depend on that version, and a version that cannot be deleted states which of those reasons applies rather than omitting the control.
14. Given a version deleted between the moment approval was checked and the moment it was written, when approval completes, then it is refused rather than reporting success.
15. Given an approved version, when withdrawing *the approval itself* is attempted — moving which version is current — then there is no such operation: `DECISIONS.md` §53 records that a declaration is bound to the participant at submission rather than at render, so changing which version is current would let somebody sign text they never read.
16. Given an approved version that no acceptance, event or registration depends on and that is not the version currently in force for its key, when an Administrator withdraws it, then the row, its number and its text in every language stay exactly as they were; it is no longer resolved as the version in force, no longer offered to the event editor, and no longer counted as “this key already has approved text”; and an audit row is written first, in the same transaction, naming the key, the version, its effective date, who approved it and the content hash of each language — never the text (`DECISIONS.md` §181).
17. Given the version currently in force for its key, when withdrawal is attempted, then it is refused whatever the counts say: zero acceptances is what a quiet week looks like, and taking the notice down would refuse every registration in the same instant (BR-REQ-053-01).
18. Given a version that is already withdrawn, or a draft, or a version something depends on, when withdrawal is attempted, then it is refused and no audit row is written; a withdrawn version still cannot be deleted, and the next version number still counts it.
19. Given the backoffice list of versions, when it renders, then withdrawn versions are folded behind a count rather than removed — a version that vanished from the screen that administers it would read as deleted, which is the impression withdrawal exists to avoid.
20. Given an approved terms version that is not the version in force and that no registration was submitted under, and no declaration was signed under, while it was the text in force, when a Superadministrator deletes it with the typed confirmation and a reason, then it is deleted, its number is retired and the audit row keeps its hash. This holds whether it was withdrawn first or not, and whether it was superseded before it ever took effect.
21. Given an approved terms version during whose time in force any registration was submitted (counting a registration whose first and latest submissions straddle that time) or had its declaration signed, of any kind and any source, when deletion is attempted, then it is refused with a conflict naming the count and the window, nothing is written, and withdrawal is still open to it.
22. Given the backoffice list of versions, when it renders, then each row offers deletion exactly when the service would delete it, because both ask the same function. A terms version shows how many registrations agreed while it was in force instead of "not used yet". One the service would refuse shows the reason, with the count and the dates, instead of the link.
23. Given the legal document editor, when its toolbar renders, then it is a toolbar named after its box. The heading and paragraph buttons read "H2" and "Text", and the link, the picture, undo and redo are filled Material glyphs of one size and colour. Each button is 44 pixels square and named by its `aria-label` and a tooltip (§361). Verification: unit `content/editor-toolbar-icons.test.ts`; e2e `legal-versions.spec.ts`.
24. Given the platform's privacy-notice template, when it is read, then its section 5 names the organizers' announcements about the event registered for (a change of place or time, a cancellation, or a message they write) under art. 6(1)(b), not marketing. The notice in effect on a deployment changes only when the club approves a new version in `/admin/legal` (2026-09-24, `DECISIONS.md` §364).
25. Given the legal document editor, when the page is first painted and before the editor has mounted, then the writing area already holds the stored text, drawn with the editor's own box and rules. It is hidden from assistive technology and holds nothing to focus, press or follow. When the editor mounts, the writing area keeps its height and nothing under it moves (2026-09-24, `DECISIONS.md` §369). Verification: unit `legal-documents/legal-body-reserved-height.test.ts`; e2e `legal-versions.spec.ts`.
26. Given the declaration's token legend, when it renders, then every token's example is made up and names neither the club, its wordmark nor its town. Each example is given in both languages where they differ, the reader's first. The dates are written by the helper a signature uses, and the declarant reads as a minor's signature produces it (2026-09-24, `DECISIONS.md` §369). Verification: unit `legal-documents/no-hardcoded-values.test.ts`.
27. There are five legal document kinds: the privacy notice, the terms, and three declarations named "Declarație pe propria răspundere (concurs)" / "(alergare de grup, asfalt)" / "(alergare de grup, trail)" and their English twins. Each has a platform template in both languages whose only blanks are merge fields and the four club facts as <PLACEHOLDER>s. The sample seed wraps every kind in the not-approved banner everywhere but production, and production is refused (tests/unit/legal-documents/group-run-declaration.test.ts, tests/integration/legal/versions.test.ts).
28. A signature on a group-run declaration counts as reliance on its version, like an acceptance: the version cannot be edited or deleted under it (tests/integration/group-run-declarations/sign.test.ts).
29. An Administrator writes, approves, withdraws and deletes a legal document version, and uses the one-press approval of the platform's texts. An Organizer or Redactor reads.
30. Given an Administrator on `/admin/legal` while any text of the catalogue has no approved version and the club's four facts are set, when they press «Aprobă textele platformei», then version 1 of each missing text is created from the platform's template with the facts written in and approved in the presser's name. The press covers exactly the catalogue's keys: the privacy notice, the terms, both race declarations and the two group-run declarations. A text already in force is left alone. The confirm dialog names each text. The intro and the button count the same list in plain sentences, never an ICU plural and never a fixed number (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §515). Verification: integration `legal/platform-approve.test.ts`; unit `legal-documents/race-declaration-keys.test.ts`; e2e `legal-versions.spec.ts`.
31. Given the platform's templates, when the race declarations are read, then both are built from one shared body with a risk section per course, titled «Declarație pe propria răspundere — cursă trail» / «— cursă pe asfalt / în parc» and in English «Self-declaration — trail race» / «Self-declaration — road / park race». Both open by declaring the participant at least {{minimumAge}}, and no text carries an under-14 flow (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §515). Verification: unit `legal-documents/declaration-risks.test.ts`, `legal-documents/merge-fields.test.ts`.
32. «Regenerează din șabloane» makes one draft per legal text whose platform template, with the club's facts, says something no approved version still offered says, never over a waiting draft, in one transaction, and puts nothing in force (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §532).
33. «Aprobă ciornele» approves, in one transaction, exactly the drafts its dialog named, each by the one-version approval; a draft that is superseded, behind an approved version or still carries a club-fact placeholder is held with its reason, and a named draft that is no longer ready — checked before and again inside the transaction — refuses the whole press with nothing approved (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §532).
34. The ticked versions are deleted in one press, all or none: drafts as one draft's delete, approved versions with one reason, `DELETE <n>` and an audit row each, the version that stops the press named (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §532).
35. The platform's group-run self-declaration templates (asphalt and trail, Romanian and English) are valid for the whole series and signed once. They apply from the date of signing and stay valid until withdrawn or replaced by a new version, and never say «fără termen de încetare» / "with no end date". The health statement is the runner's own assessment («din câte cunosc» / "to the best of my knowledge"), followed at once by a bullet saying the organiser does not and cannot assess participants medically. No text asks about a diagnosis, a treatment or a medical history. They say once, in the opening, that the run is a group run and not a (mountain) guiding service with individual supervision. The signature names «momentul semnării» / "the moment of signing" and never a time stamp. Retention is stated by purpose, never as three years from the signing, in the templates and in the privacy notice's sections 3 and 7 (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §534).
36. Given `/admin/legal`, when it renders, then it is one card per legal text in the catalogue's order. The card's header says «În vigoare: versiunea N din <data>», with the whole date in words, or «Nicio versiune în vigoare», and «O ciornă așteaptă aprobarea: versiunea N» while a draft numbered above every approved version still offered waits. The card offers «Regenerează din șablon» for that text alone, behind a confirmation that names the version it makes, or says why there is nothing to make. Its versions fold under it and open only for a waiting draft, for nothing in force, or for a filter that keeps rows in them. The chip row filters by state (Toate, În vigoare, Ciorne, Înlocuite, Retrase) and by text through the address, without JavaScript. «Toate» never lists a withdrawn version, and `?withdrawn=1` still means «Retrase». A regenerate from a card, from the list's tools or from «Versiune nouă» makes drafts only, writes one `legal_document.regenerated` audit row per draft, and lands on the list filtered to «Ciorne» (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §539).
37. Given «Versiune nouă», when nothing has been chosen yet, then the six templates are grouped in three labelled rows, each button with its text's state line under it. A «Șablon nou» chip shows when the text in force does not have the template's words, the club's facts written in, and «Regenerează toate (N)» sits beside them (2026-09-27, `DECISIONS.md` §539).
38. Given `/admin/legal` with texts whose template moved, when «Toate textele deodată» renders, then its button reads «Regenerează din șabloane (N din 6)» / "Regenerate from the templates (N of 6)", and one sentence says that only the texts marked «Șablon nou» are regenerated (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §555).
39. The race declarations' templates are titled «Declarație pe propria răspundere — eveniment montan» / «— eveniment pe asfalt / în parc» and in English «Self-declaration — mountain event» / «Self-declaration — road / park event»; the group run's mountain one «Declarație pe propria răspundere (alergare de grup, teren accidentat)» and in English «Self-declaration (group run, trail)». `/admin/legal` names each declaration by its template's title, and the Romanian catalogue's only «Trail» is the surface's own pill (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §564).
40. No tracked file carries the value of CLUB_LEGAL_NAME, CLUB_REGISTRATION_NUMBER or CLUB_REGISTERED_ADDRESS as the machine's environment or .env.local holds it, nor the CIF with its own label («CIF», «CUI») dropped, as the site shows it. `yarn secrets:check` refuses such a commit, naming the variable and never the value, and tests/unit/legal-documents/club-facts-no-literal.test.ts fails on it. The identity's sources and catalogue words carry no telephone-shaped number (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §565).
41. Given an approved version that is not in force and that something relies on, when an Administrator presses «Șterge», then its screen asks in two steps — the consequences with the real counts and a reason of 3 to 200 characters, then the version's number typed by hand — and the server checks both: a missing or longer reason is refused naming «Motivul», a wrong number naming «Numărul versiunii», and nothing changes (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §567).
42. Given such a version deleted, then it is withdrawn and marked deleted with who, when and why; it is never listed among the versions, never offered to an event, never resolved as the text in force — not on a later effective date either — and never counted as «În vigoare» or «Înlocuită»; no verb reaches it again; and one audit row `legal_document_version.deleted` carries the key, the number, the reason and the counts, never the text (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §567).
43. Given a text with deleted versions, when `/admin/legal` renders, then a closed fold «Versiuni șterse» under its versions, its count on the summary line, shows each one read-only — the number, who, when, the reason, the counts and a link to read its text — and restores nothing (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §567).
44. Given a version nothing relies on, «Șterge» stays the real delete with the typed phrase and a reason; given the version in force, it is refused, signed or not; and a deleted version's number is never reused — the next draft continues the sequence (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §567).
45. Given the counts on a version's row, in a refusal or on the delete screen, then each noun takes its form from its count without ICU plurals: «1 semnătură», «2 semnături», «0 semnături», «20 de semnături», «1 eveniment», «1 înscriere», and "1 signature" in English (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §567).
46. The death-and-waiver wording changes only the platform's templates: no migration and no data change, the declarations in effect keep their text and hash, and `/admin/legal` offers the four declarations as «Șablon nou» until the club starts a new version from the template and approves it (2026-09-29, `DECISIONS.md` §568).
47. Given a legal text whose platform template, with the club's facts, is newer than the version in force — `/admin/legal`'s «Șablon nou», the overview's `templateNewer` — when the maintenance job runs and its check is due (the first time, an hour after the last check, or at once when the release's templates differ from those the last check read), then one `LEGAL_TEMPLATES_CHANGED` email is queued to every Administrator and Superadministrator with an address, in the language of their staff row, naming the newer texts, asking a lawyer to read the declarations only when a declaration is among them and the terms only when they are (the changed texts otherwise), and linking `/admin/legal/new` on `APP_BASE_URL`; an Organizer, a Tehnic, a Redactor, a member or a participant gets none; the same change, or part of it, is never announced twice, while a later change of the templates is; a change approved before the check is never announced; and nothing is drafted or approved by it (2026-10-02, `DECISIONS.md` §639).
48. Given a `LEGAL_TEMPLATES_CHANGED` email, when it is rendered, then its one button names the screen it opens and what is done there — «Versiune nouă: regenerează textele» / “New version: regenerate the texts”, the screen's name read from the catalogues — never promises an approval, and leads to `/admin/legal/new` in the recipient's language (2026-10-03, `DECISIONS.md` §654).

**Verification:** integration `legal/editor.test.ts`, `legal/deletion.test.ts`, `legal/withdrawal.test.ts`

#### BR-REQ-053-03 — A legal document version downloads as a PDF

- **Source:** BR-BUS-053
- **Implements:** AGENTS.md §12.5, §9.2
- **Priority:** SHOULD
- **Release:** M1
- **Status:** built (`DECISIONS.md` §63). A rendering of a stored version — never a source of
  text — for the owner to read on paper, send to the club's adviser, or file.

**Acceptance criteria**

1. Given an Administrator on a version's page, when it renders, then it offers one download per language the version has, as a plain link to `GET /api/admin/legal/<id>/pdf?locale=<locale>` that works with JavaScript off.
